深度学习 · 网络结构 · 从一次乘加到多模态Deep learning · Network architecture · From one multiply–add to multimodal models

拆开神经网络Inside a Neural Network

在厨房里，你对一台机器人说："把那杯水递给我。"它听见了，找到台面上那杯水，伸手抓起来，递到你面前，等你的手接住才松开，前后十秒钟。这十秒里，它身上跑着好几个神经网络：一个一直在听有没有人叫它，一个把你的话写成文字，一个读懂这句话、想好步骤，一个看着三台相机、算出手臂接下来怎么动，一个每秒 50 次把目标变成 21 个关节的指令，最后还有一个要判断：你的手接住了没有，该不该松开。它们的名字各不相同，GRU、Whisper、Transformer、ViT、MLP……拆开来看，做的却是同一件事：把一串数乘上一串权重，加起来，弯一下，一层接一层。这一篇从一次乘加讲起：一个神经元怎么算，一层怎么算，叠起来是什么样子；然后一种一种看：图像为什么用卷积，声音和句子为什么要记忆或注意力，图像和声音怎样变成词元，怎样输出一整张图，点云怎么处理，几种输入怎样拼进同一个网络。每一种都先用手算得出来的小数字走一遍，再换成真实的大小算一遍：要多少个参数，占多少字节，一个输入要做多少次运算。In the kitchen, you say to a robot: "Pass me that glass of water." It hears you, finds the glass on the worktop, reaches out and picks it up, holds it out to you and lets go only once your hand has taken it: ten seconds in all. During those ten seconds several neural networks are running on it: one is always listening for someone calling it; one writes what you said as text; one understands the sentence and works out the steps; one watches three cameras and works out how the arms should move next; one turns the target into commands for the 21 joints, 50 times a second; and a last one has to judge whether your hand has taken the glass and whether to let go. Their names all differ (GRU, Whisper, Transformer, ViT, MLP…), but take them apart and they all do the same thing: multiply a list of numbers by a list of weights, add them up, bend the result, one layer after another. This page starts from a single multiply–add: how one neuron computes, how one layer computes, and what stacking layers looks like. Then it takes the kinds one at a time: why images use convolution, why sound and sentences need memory or attention, how images and sound become tokens, how a network can output a whole image, how point clouds are handled, and how several kinds of input are put into one network. Each one is first worked through with small numbers you can compute by hand, then again at real size: how many parameters it needs, how many bytes they take, and how many operations one input costs.

读法先把公式念成一句话Read it asFirst say the formula as a sentence
为什么为什么偏偏是这个样子Why this wayWhy it has to look like this
符号每个字母、记号是什么意思SymbolsWhat every letter and sign means
算例代入这台机器人的数验证一遍Worked examplePlug in this robot’s numbers and check
要点最容易出错、最关键的地方Key pointThe part that matters most and is easiest to get wrong
速查忘了某个记号，查下面的速查表LookupForgot a symbol? Check the table below

单位约定：一个参数是网络里一个可以学的数；一个数占几个字节（B）看精度，FP32 是 4 字节，BF16 是 2，FP8 是 1，FP4 是半个；一次乘法加一次加法叫一次乘加，算 2 次运算（FLOP）。k、M、G、T 依次是千、百万、十亿、万亿；本篇的 GB 都是 109 字节。这些单位在开场后面的"先把单位说清楚"一节里一个一个拆开讲。Units: a parameter is one number in the network that can be learned; how many bytes (B) a number takes depends on the precision: 4 bytes in FP32, 2 in BF16, 1 in FP8 and half a byte in FP4; one multiplication plus one addition is called one multiply–add, and it counts as 2 operations (FLOP). k, M, G and T stand in turn for thousand, million, billion and trillion; on this page a GB is always 109 bytes. Each of these units is taken apart, one at a time, in the units section that follows the opening.

记号速查表：本篇用到的字母、说法和缩写Quick reference: the letters, terms and abbreviations used here

一个神经元和一层One neuron and one layer

x, xi
输入：一串数（向量），xi 是其中第 i 个（第 ① 步）Input: a list of numbers (a vector); xi is the i-th of them (step ①)
w, b
权重和偏置：一个神经元里可以学的数（第 ① 步）Weights and bias: the numbers in a neuron that can be learned (step ①)
z, σ, y
加权和；"弯一下"的函数（激活函数）；输出（第 ① 步）Weighted sum; the "bend" function (activation function); output (step ①)
W, h
一层的权重排成的表（矩阵），一行是一个神经元；中间层算出来的一串数（第 ② 步）A layer’s weights laid out as a table (a matrix), one row per neuron; the list of numbers computed by a layer in the middle (step ②)
nin, nout, B
一层的输入个数和输出个数；一次喂进去几个输入（批的大小；这个 B 是个数，不是字节）（第 ② 步）Number of inputs and number of outputs of a layer; how many inputs are fed in at once (the batch size; this B is a count, not bytes) (step ②)

尺寸和成本Size and cost

P, q, M
参数个数；每个数占几个字节；占的存储 M = P × q（单位一节）Number of parameters; bytes per number; storage taken, M = P × q (units section)
F
算一个输入要做的运算次数，单位 FLOP（一次乘加算 2 次）（单位一节）Number of operations needed for one input, in FLOP (a multiply–add counts as 2) (units section)
L, l
层数，也是块数（第 ④、⑧ 步）；第几层（第 ③ 步）Number of layers, which is also the number of blocks (steps ④ and ⑧); which layer it is (step ③)
NMAC, f, R
乘加次数；每秒算几次；每秒的运算（单位一节）Number of multiply–adds; calls per second; operations per second (units section)
BW, t
内存带宽；把全部权重读一遍要的时间（单位一节；第 ⑥ 步的 t 是第几个时刻）Memory bandwidth; the time to read all the weights once (units section; in step ⑥, t is the index of the time step)
f(x), fattn, fe
一段网络，当成一个函数（第 ⑤、⑧、⑫、⑭ 步）；单独一个 f 是每秒算几次A piece of a network, treated as a function (steps ⑤, ⑧, ⑫ and ⑭); f on its own is calls per second
N(x)
归一化（第 ⑧ 步）；单独一个 N 是点数Normalisation (step ⑧); N on its own is the number of points
pi
第 i 个选项的概率（第 ③ 步）；第 i 个点（第 ⑫ 步）；单独一个 p 是补零的圈数Probability of the i-th option (step ③); the i-th point (step ⑫); p on its own is the number of rings of zero padding

卷积和图像Convolution and images

k, s, p
卷积核的边长；步长（每次滑几格）；四周补零的圈数（第 ④ 步）Side length of the kernel; stride (how many cells it slides each time); how many rings of zeros are padded around the edges (step ④)
n, n′
正方形图像或特征图一条边上有几个像素；卷积之后的边长（第 ④ 步）Number of pixels along one side of a square image or feature map; the side length after the convolution (step ④)
Cin, Cout
输入和输出的通道数：每个位置上有几个数（第 ④ 步）Number of input and output channels: how many numbers there are at each position (step ④)

序列和注意力Sequences and attention

T, t
序列里有几个词元或几个时刻；第几个时刻（第 ⑥ 步）Number of tokens or time steps in a sequence; which time step it is (step ⑥)
nh
循环网络隐藏状态里有几个数（第 ⑥ 步）How many numbers the hidden state of a recurrent network holds (step ⑥)
Q, K, V, A
查询、键、值；注意力权重表（第 ⑦ 步）Queries, keys, values; the table of attention weights (step ⑦)
d, dff
每个词元用几个数表示（模型的宽度）；MLP 中间那层的宽度（第 ⑦、⑧ 步）How many numbers represent each token (the model’s width); the width of the MLP’s middle layer (steps ⑦ and ⑧)
nvocab
词表里有几个词元（第 ⑧ 步）Number of tokens in the vocabulary (step ⑧)

点云、多模态和训练Point clouds, multimodal models and training

N
点云里有几个点（第 ⑫ 步）Number of points in a point cloud (step ⑫)
E, kE
混合专家里一共几个专家；每个词元选几个（第 ⑭ 步）Total number of experts in a mixture of experts; how many each token picks (step ⑭)
D
训练用了多少个词元（第 ⑮ 步）Number of tokens used in training (step ⑮)

单位Units

比特、字节（B）Bit, byte (B)
一个 0 或 1；8 个比特是 1 个字节A single 0 or 1; 8 bits make 1 byte
kB, MB, GB, TB
103、106、109、1012 字节；KiB、MiB、GiB 按 1024 进位（单位一节）103, 106, 109, 1012 bytes; KiB, MiB, GiB count in 1024s (units section)
FLOP
一次浮点运算：一次加法、减法或乘法；一次乘加算 2 次One floating-point operation: one addition, subtraction or multiplication; a multiply–add counts as 2
MFLOP, GFLOP, TFLOP
106、109、1012 次运算（工作量）106, 109, 1012 operations (amount of work)
FLOP/s, TFLOPS
每秒多少次运算（速度）；TOPS 是每秒万亿次运算，多用来标整数（INT8）运算，也有厂商拿它标 FP4Operations per second (speed); TOPS is trillions of operations per second, mostly used to rate integer (INT8) arithmetic, though some vendors also use it for FP4
乘加（MAC）Multiply–add (MAC)
乘一下再加到累计的和上；很多论文写的"FLOPs"其实数的是它Multiply, then add to the running sum; the "FLOPs" in many papers actually count these
Hz, GB/s
每秒几次；内存每秒送出多少 GB（带宽）Times per second; how many GB the memory delivers per second (bandwidth)
"8B"
模型名字里的 B 是十亿（billion）个参数，不是字节In a model’s name, B means billion (parameters), not bytes

缩写Abbreviations

MLP
多层感知机：全连接层一层层叠起来（第 ③ 步）Multilayer perceptron: dense (fully connected) layers stacked one on top of another (step ③)
CNN
卷积神经网络（第 ④、⑤ 步）Convolutional neural network (steps ④ and ⑤)
RNN, GRU, LSTM
循环网络，和它两种带门的版本（第 ⑥ 步）Recurrent network, and its two gated versions (step ⑥)
ViT
把图像切成小块、当成词元的 Transformer（第 ⑨ 步）A Transformer that cuts an image into small patches and treats them as tokens (step ⑨)
VLA
视觉–语言–动作模型：看图、读话、出动作（第 ⑬ 步）Vision–language–action model: looks at images, reads words, outputs actions (step ⑬)
MoE
混合专家：每个词元只用一部分参数（第 ⑭ 步）Mixture of experts: each token uses only part of the parameters (step ⑭)
FP32, BF16, FP8, FP4
存一个数用 32、16、8、4 个比特A number stored in 32, 16, 8 or 4 bits

第 0 步 · 厨房里的十秒Step 0 · Ten seconds in the kitchen

一台机器人身上，跑着好几个网络Several networks, running on one robot

先把这台机器人说清楚。它有一个带两个轮子的底盘，一个能升降的身子，两条 7 个关节的手臂，每条末端一个夹爪，头能左右上下转：2 + 1 + 14 + 2 + 2，一共 21 个关节。它的眼睛是四台相机：头上一台彩色相机，每秒拍 30 张 1280 × 720 的照片；两个手腕上各一台小一点的彩色相机（640 × 480）；还有一台深度相机，每个像素给出那一点沿相机光轴的深度。耳朵是四个麦克风，每秒各采 16000 个数。两个手腕上各有一个力传感器，测 3 个方向的力和 3 个方向的力矩。它的"脑子"是身上一块和 NVIDIA Jetson Thor 同一类的计算模块，有 128 GB 内存。First, a clear picture of the robot. It has a base with two wheels, a body that can move up and down, two arms with 7 joints each and a gripper at the end of each, and a head that turns from side to side and up and down: 2 + 1 + 14 + 2 + 2, 21 joints in all. Its eyes are four cameras: a colour camera on the head that takes 30 pictures a second at 1280 × 720; a smaller colour camera on each wrist (640 × 480); and a depth camera, which gives for each pixel the depth of that point along the camera’s optical axis. Its ears are four microphones, each taking 16000 numbers a second. Each wrist has a force sensor that measures force along 3 axes and torque about 3 axes. Its "brain" is an on-board computing module of the same class as the NVIDIA Jetson Thor, with 128 GB of memory.

这十秒可以切成四段：① 听（0 到 2 秒）：你说话，它把声音变成文字；② 想（2 到 3 秒）：它读懂这句话，想好要先找杯子、再抓、再递；③ 看和伸手（3 到 8 秒）：它看着相机，算出手臂一路怎么动，抓起杯子；④ 递和松手（8 到 10 秒）：它把杯子递到你面前，你的手一拉，它松开。每一段都有神经网络在算，下面这张表就是这一篇要拆开的全部网络：The ten seconds split into four phases. ① Hear (0 to 2 s): you speak, and it turns the sound into text. ② Think (2 to 3 s): it understands the sentence and decides to find the glass first, then grasp it, then hand it over. ③ Look and reach (3 to 8 s): it watches through its cameras, works out how the arm should move the whole way, and picks up the glass. ④ Hand over and let go (8 to 10 s): it holds the glass out to you; your hand pulls, and it lets go. In every phase there are neural networks computing, and the list below is every network this page takes apart:

  • 唤醒词：一个很小的循环网络（GRU），一直听着麦克风，听到有人叫它才叫醒后面的网络。约 79 kB，听 1 秒的声音算约 380 万次运算（第 ⑥ 步）。Wake word: a tiny recurrent network (GRU) that listens to the microphones all the time and wakes the networks after it only when it hears someone calling the robot. About 79 kB; listening to 1 s of sound takes about 3.8 million operations (step ⑥).
  • 听写：Whisper，把 2 秒的话写成"把那杯水递给我"。2.4 亿个参数，它总是按 30 秒一段来听，光把一段声音编码就要约 3400 亿次运算（第 ⑩ 步）。Transcription: Whisper writes out the command from the 2 s of speech. It has 240 million parameters and always listens in 30-second windows; just encoding one window takes about 340 billion operations (step ⑩).
  • 规划：一个 80 亿参数的大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B），读懂这句话，写出步骤。每写一个词元约 150 亿次运算（第 ⑦、⑧ 步）。Planning: a large language model with 8 billion parameters (Llama 3.1 8B) understands the sentence and writes out the steps. Each token it writes takes about 15 billion operations (steps ⑦ and ⑧).
  • 看：把一张相机照片缩成 224 × 224，切成 256 块，变成 256 个词元（SigLIP，约 4 亿个参数，一张图约 2200 亿次运算）（第 ⑨ 步）。Seeing: a camera picture is shrunk to 224 × 224, cut into 256 patches and turned into 256 tokens (SigLIP, about 400 million parameters, about 220 billion operations per image) (step ⑨).
  • 动作模型：一个像 π0 那样的视觉–语言–动作模型，33 亿个参数：三张图、一句话和 21 个关节的角度一起进去，出来的是手臂接下来 50 步怎么动。每秒算 10 次，一次约 4.3 万亿次运算（第 ⑬ 步）。Action model: a vision–language–action model like π0, with 3.3 billion parameters: three images, a sentence and the angles of the 21 joints go in together, and out comes how the arms should move over the next 50 steps. It runs 10 times a second, at about 4.3 trillion operations each time (step ⑬).
  • 另一条路：不用一个大模型包办，也可以几个小网络各管一段：ResNet-18 认出画面里有杯子（一张图约 36 亿次运算，第 ⑤ 步），U-Net 把杯子的像素抠出来（第 ⑪ 步），PointNet 从深度相机的点里找抓的位置（第 ⑫ 步）。Another route: instead of one big model doing everything, several small networks can each handle one part: ResNet-18 recognises that there is a glass in the picture (about 3.6 billion operations per image, step ⑤), U-Net cuts out the glass’s pixels (step ⑪), and PointNet finds where to grasp from the depth camera’s points (step ⑫).
  • 手的小策略：一个四层的全连接网络（MLP），每秒 50 次，把关节的状态和目标变成 21 个关节的指令。约 21 万个参数，一次约 42 万次运算（第 ③ 步）。The hand’s small policy: a four-layer fully connected network (MLP) that runs 50 times a second, turning the state of the joints and the target into commands for the 21 joints. About 210,000 parameters and about 420,000 operations per call (step ③).
  • 松手：最后那一下"该不该松开"，我们用一个神经元来算，小到可以手算（第 ① 步）。Letting go: for the very last decision, "should it let go?", we use a single neuron, small enough to compute by hand (step ①).

同是神经网络，最小的那个只有 4 个参数、算一次 6 次运算；最大的有 80 亿个参数，动作模型算一次要 4.3 万亿次运算：参数差了 9 个数量级，运算差了将近 12 个数量级。可它们都由同样的零件拼成。这一篇要回答六个问题：They are all neural networks, but the smallest has only 4 parameters and takes 6 operations per call, while the largest has 8 billion parameters, and one call of the action model takes 4.3 trillion operations: 9 orders of magnitude apart in parameters and nearly 12 in operations. Yet they are all built from the same parts. This page answers six questions:

  1. 一个输入，怎样在权重里一步步算成输出？How does one input get worked, step by step, through the weights into an output?
  2. 一个网络里面到底长什么样？What does a network actually look like inside?
  3. 一个网络要占多少存储？How much storage does a network take?
  4. 多大的输入，要做多少次运算？How many operations does an input of a given size need?
  5. 什么任务、什么模态（声音、图像、文字、点云、关节状态），该用什么网络？Which network suits which task and which modality (sound, images, text, point clouds, joint states)?
  6. 几种模态、几种操作，怎样拼进一个网络？How are several modalities and several operations put together in one network?
  • 最上面是厨房里的十秒：▶ 播放，或者拖 时刻 t；四个按钮 ① 听、② 想、③ 看和伸手、④ 递和松手 直接跳到那一段。At the top are the ten seconds in the kitchen: press ▶ Play, or drag Time t; the four buttons ① Hear, ② Think, ③ Look and reach and ④ Hand over and let go jump straight to that phase.
  • 中间每一行是一个网络，色块是它在算的时候，旁边数着到现在算了几次。点一行（或者用 上一步、下一步 一个一个换），下面的流程图画出数据怎么流过这个网络：从输入到输出每一段是什么形状、有几个数，一个小包沿着走，计数器数着运算。In the middle, each row is one network: the coloured blocks show when it is computing, and the number beside them counts how many times it has run so far. Tap a row (or go through them one by one with Back and Next step) and the flow diagram below draws how the data flows through that network: the shape of each stage from input to output and how many numbers it holds, with a small packet travelling along and a counter counting the operations.
  • 最下面的卡片写出它吃进什么、吐出什么，有多少参数、用 BF16 占多少字节，算一次要多少运算，每秒算几次、每秒多少运算，在第几步细讲。The card at the bottom gives what it takes in and what it puts out, how many parameters it has and how many bytes they take in BF16, how many operations one call needs, how many calls it makes per second and how many operations per second that comes to, and which step covers it in detail.

先玩：哪个网络一直在算，哪个只算一次？动作模型算一次的运算，是松手那个神经元的多少倍？手的小策略每秒算 50 次，一秒加起来的运算，比动作模型算一次多还是少？Try first: Which network is computing all the time, and which runs only once? How many times more operations does one call of the action model take than the let-go neuron? The hand’s small policy runs 50 times a second: added up over one second, is that more or fewer operations than one call of the action model?

这一步的要点：机器人身上的"智能"是好几个大小悬殊的网络，各管一段：有的一直开着，有的每秒几十次，有的只在需要时算一次。它们的大小（参数和字节）和成本（每次多少运算）都能算出来，下面就从最小的那个开始拆。The point of this step: the robot’s "intelligence" is several networks of vastly different sizes, each handling one part: some are always on, some run dozens of times a second, and some run only once, when needed. Their size (parameters and bytes) and their cost (operations per call) can all be worked out, and below we start taking them apart, beginning with the smallest.

第 0 步 · 单位Step 0 · Units

先把单位说清楚：参数、字节、FLOPGetting the units straight: parameters, bytes, FLOP

下面每拆一个网络，都要算它的三个数：有多少个参数，占多少字节，一个输入要做多少次运算。这三个数各有各的单位，先一个一个说清楚。Each time we take a network apart below, we work out three numbers for it: how many parameters it has, how many bytes it takes up and how many operations one input needs. Each of the three has its own unit, so first let’s go through them one by one.

参数：网络里一个可以学的数Parameters: a number the network can learn

一个参数就是网络里一个可以学的数：一个权重、一个偏置，或者归一化里的一个缩放倍数、一个平移（第 ⑤、⑧ 步）。第 ① 步那个神经元有 3 个权重、1 个偏置，就是 4 个参数。参数按"个"数，数大了用千、百万、十亿。Llama 3.1 8B 名字里的"8B"，是 80 亿个参数（B 是英文的 billion，十亿），不是 8 个字节。A parameter is one number in the network that can be learned: a weight, a bias, or a scale factor or a shift inside a normalisation (steps ⑤ and ⑧). The neuron in step ① has 3 weights and 1 bias, so 4 parameters. Parameters are counted one by one, and big counts are given in thousands, millions and billions. The "8B" in the name Llama 3.1 8B means 8 billion parameters (B stands for billion), not 8 bytes.

字节：一个数存起来占多大地方Bytes: how much room a stored number takes

计算机里最小的单位是比特（bit），一个 0 或一个 1；8 个比特是 1 个字节（byte，写作 B）。权重是带小数的数，计算机用浮点数来存，道理和科学计数法一样：−10 写成 −1.25 × 23，只要存三样东西：正还是负（1 个比特），2 的几次方（叫指数），1.25 的小数部分（叫尾数）。指数的比特越多，能存的数越大、越小；尾数的比特越多，存得越准。常用的几种格式，名字里的数字就是一共几个比特：The smallest unit in a computer is the bit, a single 0 or 1; 8 bits make 1 byte (written B). Weights are numbers with fractional parts, and a computer stores them as floating-point numbers, on the same principle as scientific notation: −10 is written as −1.25 × 23, so only three things need storing: positive or negative (1 bit), which power of 2 (called the exponent), and the fractional part of 1.25 (called the mantissa). The more bits the exponent has, the larger (and the smaller) the numbers that can be stored; the more bits the mantissa has, the more precisely they are stored. In the common formats, the number in the name is the total number of bits:

格式Format比特：符号 + 指数 + 尾数Bits: sign + exponent + mantissa字节Bytes最大能存Largest valueσ(2) = 0.880797… 存进去变成σ(2) = 0.880797… is stored as
FP321 + 8 + 23 = 324约 3.4 × 1038about 3.4 × 10380.88079709
BF161 + 8 + 7 = 162约 3.4 × 1038about 3.4 × 10380.87890625
FP161 + 5 + 10 = 16265,5040.88085938
FP8（E4M3）FP8 (E4M3)1 + 4 + 3 = 814480.875
FP4（E2M1）FP4 (E2M1)1 + 2 + 1 = 40.561

σ(2) ≈ 0.8808 是第 ① 步那个神经元的输出。比特越少，存进去的数越粗：BF16 只剩两三位有效数字；FP8 只能存成 0.875；FP4 一共只有 15 个不同的值（0、±0.5、±1、±1.5、±2、±3、±4、±6），0.8808 只能存成 1，一个 −10 的权重甚至存不下，只能存成 −6。所以真用 FP8、FP4 的时候，会给一组数配一个共同的缩放倍数（FP8 常常整个张量共用一个，FP4 是每 16 或 32 个数一个），先把它们缩到格式能表示的范围里（第 13 篇第 ④、⑭ 步）。这些倍数也要存：每 16 个数多存一个 8 比特的倍数，FP4 实际每个数约 4.5 比特；本篇为了好算，FP4 按 0.5 字节算。BF16 和 FP32 的指数一样多，能存的范围一样大，只是没那么准，所以训练常用它；推理越来越多用 FP8、FP4：比特减半，要存、要搬的字节也差不多减半。还有一种存整数的格式 INT8，8 个比特存 −128 到 127。σ(2) ≈ 0.8808 is the output of the neuron in step ①. The fewer the bits, the coarser the stored number: BF16 keeps only two or three significant digits; FP8 can only store it as 0.875; FP4 has just 15 different values in all (0, ±0.5, ±1, ±1.5, ±2, ±3, ±4, ±6), so 0.8808 can only be stored as 1, and a weight of −10 does not even fit and can only be stored as −6. So when FP8 and FP4 are used for real, each group of numbers is given a shared scale factor (in FP8 often one for a whole tensor, in FP4 one for every 16 or 32 numbers), which first scales them into the range the format can represent (page 13, steps ④ and ⑭). These factors have to be stored too: one extra 8-bit factor for every 16 numbers makes FP4 really about 4.5 bits per number; to keep the arithmetic simple, this page counts FP4 as 0.5 bytes. BF16 has as many exponent bits as FP32, so it covers the same range, only less precisely, which is why training often uses it. Inference uses FP8 and FP4 more and more: halve the bits and the bytes to store and to move are roughly halved too. There is also a format for integers, INT8: 8 bits store −128 to 127.

运算：FLOP 是什么Operations: what is a FLOP?

M = P × qF = 2 NMACR = F × ft = M / BW

存储等于参数个数乘每个数的字节数。一个输入的运算次数，等于乘加次数的 2 倍。每秒的运算次数，等于一个输入的运算次数乘每秒算几次。把全部权重从内存里读一遍要的时间，等于存储除以内存每秒能送出的字节数。Storage equals the number of parameters times the bytes per number. The operations for one input are 2 times the number of multiply–adds. The operations per second are the operations for one input times the calls per second. The time to read all the weights from memory once is the storage divided by the bytes the memory can deliver per second.

FLOP 是 floating-point operation 的缩写，一次浮点运算：两个浮点数做一次加法、减法或乘法。神经网络里几乎全是"乘一下，再加到累计的和上"，这一对叫一次乘加（multiply–add，也写作 MAC），算 2 次运算。要分清两种写法：FLOP 或 FLOPs（小写 s，复数）说的是"做了多少次"，是工作量；FLOP/s 或 FLOPS（大写 S）说的是"每秒做多少次"，是速度。还有两个常见的坑。第一，很多论文和工具写的"FLOPs"数的其实是乘加：ResNet-18 的"1.8 GFLOPs"是 18 亿次乘加，按本篇的数法是 36 亿次运算（第 ⑤ 步）。第二，芯片标的峰值速度常常是"稀疏"的：假设权重里每 4 个就有 2 个是 0、可以跳过，标出来的数是实际稠密速度的 2 倍。整数运算不叫 FLOP，叫 OP，速度写成 TOPS（每秒万亿次）。softmax 和 S 形函数里的 e 的次方、除法次数很少，一般不算进去。FLOP stands for floating-point operation: one addition, subtraction or multiplication of two floating-point numbers. Almost everything in a neural network is "multiply, then add to the running sum"; that pair is one multiply–add (also written MAC) and counts as 2 operations. Keep two spellings apart: FLOP or FLOPs (lower-case s, plural) means "how many were done", an amount of work; FLOP/s or FLOPS (capital S) means "how many per second", a speed. There are two more common traps. First, the "FLOPs" in many papers and tools actually count multiply–adds: ResNet-18’s "1.8 GFLOPs" is 1.8 billion multiply–adds, which is 3.6 billion operations as this page counts them (step ⑤). Second, the peak speed quoted for a chip is often a "sparse" figure: it assumes that 2 in every 4 weights are 0 and can be skipped, so the quoted number is 2 times the real dense speed. Integer operations are not called FLOP but OP, and their speed is written TOPS (trillions of operations per second). The powers of e and the divisions in softmax and the sigmoid are few, so they are usually not counted.

P
参数个数（个）number of parameters (a count)
q
每个数几个字节：FP32 是 4，BF16 是 2，FP8 是 1，FP4 是 0.5bytes per number: 4 for FP32, 2 for BF16, 1 for FP8, 0.5 for FP4
M
存储，单位字节（B）storage, in bytes (B)
NMAC
算一个输入要做几次乘加multiply–adds needed for one input
F
算一个输入要做几次运算，单位 FLOPoperations needed for one input, in FLOP
f
每秒算几次，单位赫兹（Hz）：1 Hz 就是每秒 1 次calls per second, in hertz (Hz): 1 Hz is 1 call per second
R
每秒的运算次数，单位 FLOP/soperations per second, in FLOP/s
BW
内存带宽：内存每秒能送出多少字节，单位 GB/smemory bandwidth: how many bytes the memory can deliver per second, in GB/s
t
把全部权重读一遍要多久（秒）how long it takes to read all the weights once (in seconds)

第 ① 步的神经元：P = 4，用 FP32 存，q = 4，M = 16 B。z = b + w1x1 + w2x2 + w3x3，每一项"乘一下再加上去"是一次乘加，NMAC = 3，F = 6 FLOP；每秒算 50 次，f = 50 Hz，R = 300 FLOP/s。换成 Llama 3.1 8B：P ≈ 8.03 × 109，用 BF16 存，M ≈ 1.61 × 1010 B = 16.1 GB；写一个词元 F ≈ 1.5 × 1010 FLOP = 15 GFLOP；机器人的模块每秒能从内存读 273 GB，读一遍权重 t ≈ 16.1 / 273 ≈ 0.059 秒，也就是 59 毫秒。The neuron from step ①: P = 4; stored in FP32, q = 4 and M = 16 B. In z = b + w1x1 + w2x2 + w3x3, each term, "multiply, then add on", is one multiply–add, so NMAC = 3 and F = 6 FLOP; at 50 calls a second, f = 50 Hz and R = 300 FLOP/s. Now Llama 3.1 8B: P ≈ 8.03 × 109; stored in BF16, M ≈ 1.61 × 1010 B = 16.1 GB; writing one token takes F ≈ 1.5 × 1010 FLOP = 15 GFLOP; the robot’s module can read 273 GB a second from memory, so reading the weights once takes t ≈ 16.1 / 273 ≈ 0.059 seconds, that is, 59 milliseconds.

"一个输入"一定要说清楚是什么：一组关节状态、一张图、一个词元、一段 30 秒的声音、一团 1024 个点。同一个网络，按一个词元算和按一整句话算，能差几十倍。本篇每个数后面都会写明是"每什么"。Always be clear about what "one input" is: one set of joint states, one image, one token, a 30-second stretch of sound, a cloud of 1024 points. For the same network, counting per token and counting per whole sentence can give numbers dozens of times apart. Every number on this page says what it is "per".

数量的前缀Prefixes for big numbers

数一大就用前缀。英文的前缀每次乘 1000，中文的"万""亿"每次乘一万：Once numbers get big, we use prefixes. The prefixes step up by 1000 each time, and so do the English words thousand, million, billion, trillion and quadrillion:

前缀Prefix是多少Value换成中文In words本篇的例子Example from this page
k（千）k (kilo)103千thousand唤醒词网络约 79 kBThe wake-word network: about 79 kB
M（百万）M (mega)106百万million手的小策略算一次 0.42 MFLOP = 42 万次运算One call of the hand’s small policy: 0.42 MFLOP = 420,000 operations
G（十亿）G (giga)109十亿billionResNet-18 看一张图 3.63 GFLOP = 36.3 亿次运算ResNet-18 looking at one image: 3.63 GFLOP = 3.63 billion operations
T（万亿）T (tera)1012万亿trillionπ0 类动作模型算一次 4.3 TFLOP = 4.3 万亿次运算One call of a π0-style action model: 4.3 TFLOP = 4.3 trillion operations
P（千万亿）P (peta)1015千万亿quadrillion机器人模块标的 FP4 稀疏峰值约 2 PFLOP/sThe robot module’s quoted sparse FP4 peak: about 2 PFLOP/s

中文的万是 104，亿是 108，所以 1 G 是 10 亿，1 T 是 1 万亿。存储也用这套前缀：本篇的 1 kB 是 1000 字节，1 MB 是 106 字节，1 GB 是 109 字节。电脑的系统有时按 1024 进位，写成 KiB、MiB、GiB：1 GiB = 230 ≈ 1.074 × 109 字节，所以 16.1 GB 在那里显示成 15.0 GiB，不是哪里丢了数据。A million is 106 and a billion is 109, so 1 G is 1 billion and 1 T is 1 trillion. Storage uses the same prefixes: on this page 1 kB is 1000 bytes, 1 MB is 106 bytes and 1 GB is 109 bytes. Computer operating systems sometimes count in 1024s and write KiB, MiB, GiB: 1 GiB = 230 ≈ 1.074 × 109 bytes, so 16.1 GB shows up there as 15.0 GiB; no data has gone missing.

注意：公式里的 P 和 M 是参数个数和存储；跟在数后面、写在单位前面的 M、P（比如 0.42 MFLOP、2 PFLOP/s）是前缀，是两回事。B 也一样：写在单位里是字节（16 GB），写在模型名字里是十亿（8B），第 ② 步的 B 是一批有几个输入。Careful: in the formulas, P and M are the number of parameters and the storage; an M or P that follows a number and comes right before a unit (as in 0.42 MFLOP or 2 PFLOP/s) is a prefix, which is a different thing. The same goes for B: in a unit it means bytes (16 GB), in a model name it means billion (8B), and the B in step ② is the number of inputs in one batch.

  • 一个数怎么存：选一个数（预设有神经元的输出 0.8808、一个权重 −10、拉力 6 N、很小的 0.001、很大的 300），或者拖滑块。每种格式画出它的比特：红色是符号，橙色是指数，蓝紫色是尾数；旁边是真正存进去的值和相对误差。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：分四步看一种格式怎么存（符号、指数、尾数、读回来），细看 换成别的格式，点一行也行。How a number is stored: pick a number (the presets are the neuron’s output 0.8808, a weight of −10, a pull force of 6 N, a tiny 0.001 and a big 300), or drag the slider. Each format draws its bits: red is the sign, orange the exponent, blue-violet the mantissa; beside them are the value actually stored and the relative error. ▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: four steps show how one format stores it (sign, exponent, mantissa, reading it back); Explain switches to another format, and so does tapping a row.
  • 参数 × 精度 = 字节：选一个网络和一种精度，看它占多少字节，按 1000 进和按 1024 进各是多少，和机器人的 128 GB 比一比。Parameters × precision = bytes: pick a network and a precision to see how many bytes it takes, counted in thousands and in 1024s, and how that compares with the robot’s 128 GB.
  • 乘加 → 运算 → 每秒：同样选一个网络，看"一个输入"是什么、要几次乘加、几次运算（写成前缀，也写成万、亿）；拖 每秒几次 f，看每秒的运算，和读一遍权重要多久。Multiply–adds → FLOP → per second: again pick a network to see what "one input" is, and how many multiply–adds and operations it takes (written with a prefix and also in words); drag Calls per second f to see the operations per second and how long one read of all the weights takes.

先玩：把 −10 存进 FP4，变成了多少？0.8808 存进 BF16，误差是百分之几？选 Llama 3.1 8B：从 FP32 换到 FP4，字节少了几倍？它写一个词元的运算，写成中文是多少亿次？Try first: store −10 in FP4: what does it become? Store 0.8808 in BF16: what is the error, as a percentage? Pick Llama 3.1 8B: going from FP32 to FP4, by what factor do the bytes shrink? And how many billion operations does it take to write one token?

这一步的要点：参数是可以学的数，按个数；字节 = 参数 × 每个数的字节数（FP32 4，BF16 2，FP8 1，FP4 0.5），比特越少越省也越粗；FLOP 是一次浮点加法或乘法，一次乘加算 2 次；FLOP 是工作量，FLOP/s 是速度；k、M、G、T、P 每级乘 1000，1 G = 10 亿，1 T = 1 万亿。The point of this step: parameters are the numbers that can be learned, and they are simply counted; bytes = parameters × bytes per number (FP32 4, BF16 2, FP8 1, FP4 0.5), and fewer bits save space but are coarser; a FLOP is one floating-point addition or multiplication, and one multiply–add counts as 2; FLOP is an amount of work, FLOP/s a speed; k, M, G, T, P each step up by 1000, 1 G = 1 billion, 1 T = 1 trillion.

第 ① 步 · 一个神经元Step ① · One neuron

乘、加，再弯一下Multiply, add, then bend

从最后那一下说起。杯子已经递到你面前，夹爪还夹着它。你的手握上去，往回一拉。机器人要决定：现在松开吗？松早了，杯子掉在地上；松晚了，你拉不动，水洒出来。它手里有三条线索：Let’s start with the very last moment. The glass is already in front of you, still held in the gripper. Your hand takes hold of it and pulls back. The robot has to decide: let go now? Too early, and the glass falls to the floor; too late, and you can’t pull it away and the water spills. It has three clues to go on:

  • x1：手腕上的力传感器测到的往外拉的力，单位牛顿（N）。现在是 6 N。x1: the outward pull measured by the force sensor at the wrist, in newtons (N). Right now it is 6 N.
  • x2：相机看到"有一只手握住了杯子"的把握，0 到 1 之间。现在是 1，很确定。x2: how sure the camera is that "a hand is holding the glass", between 0 and 1. Right now it is 1: very sure.
  • x3：手臂自己还在动的速度，单位米每秒。现在是 0，已经停下了。x3: how fast the arm itself is still moving, in metres per second. Right now it is 0: it has stopped.

一个神经元处理这三条线索的办法很朴素：给每条线索一个权重，表示它有多重要、往哪个方向推；把"线索 × 权重"加起来，再加一个偏置，得到一个分数；最后把分数"弯一下"，变成一个 0 到 1 之间的数，就是"该松手"的把握。比如权重取 w1 = 0.5（每牛顿拉力加 0.5 分），w2 = 3（看到有手握着，加 3 分），w3 = −10（手臂每秒还在动 1 米，减 10 分：自己还在动的时候不该松），偏置 b = −4（不管怎样先扣 4 分，相当于一道门槛）。A neuron handles these three clues in a very plain way. It gives each clue a weight that says how much it matters and which way it pushes; it adds up "clue × weight", then adds a bias, to get a score; finally it "bends" the score into a number between 0 and 1, which is how sure it is that it "should let go". For example, take the weights w1 = 0.5 (0.5 points for each newton of pull), w2 = 3 (3 points for seeing a hand holding it), w3 = −10 (10 points off for each metre per second the arm is still moving: it shouldn’t let go while it is still moving itself) and the bias b = −4 (4 points off whatever happens, which acts as a threshold).

z = w1x1 + w2x2 + w3x3 + by = σ(z)σ(z) = 1 / (1 + e−z)

分数 z 等于每条线索乘上它的权重，全部加起来，再加上偏置。输出 y 是把 z 弯一下的结果：z 越大，y 越接近 1；z 越小，y 越接近 0；z 等于 0 时，y 正好是一半。The score z is each clue times its weight, all added up, plus the bias. The output y is z after the bend: the bigger z, the closer y gets to 1; the smaller z, the closer y gets to 0; when z is 0, y is exactly one half.

为什么是"乘了再加"？这是把几条重要性不同的线索合成一个判断最简单的办法：每条线索各自贡献一份，贡献的大小和方向由权重决定，正的往"松"推，负的往"别松"推。偏置是门槛：只有加起来超过 4 分，z 才是正的。为什么还要弯一下？只是加权和的话，z 可以是任意大小，拉力从 6 N 涨到 600 N，z 就从 2 涨到 299，"该松手"涨了快 150 倍，这说不通；弯一下以后，输出被压在 0 到 1 之间，过了门槛很快就接近"确定"，没过门槛就接近"不"，像一个有点软的开关。这个形状像拉长的 S，所以叫 S 形函数（sigmoid）。网络中间更常用的是更简单的 ReLU：负数变成 0，正数原样保留，写成 ReLU(z) = max(0, z)，算起来只要比一下大小。还有 GELU、ELU、SiLU 这些圆滑一点的版本（Whisper 用 GELU，Radford 等 2022；训练四足机器人的 legged_gym 默认用 ELU，legged_gym；第 ⑧ 步的 Llama 用 SiLU）。不管哪一种，它们都叫激活函数，这一篇叫它"弯一下"。Why "multiply, then add"? It is the simplest way to combine several clues of different importance into one judgement: each clue contributes its own share, and the weight sets the size and direction of that share, a positive weight pushing towards "let go" and a negative one towards "hold on". The bias is a threshold: z is positive only when the clues add up to more than 4 points. Why the bend as well? With the weighted sum alone, z can be any size: if the pull rises from 6 N to 600 N, z goes from 2 to 299, and "should let go" grows nearly 150 times, which makes no sense. After the bend, the output is squeezed between 0 and 1: past the threshold it quickly gets close to "certain", short of it close to "no", like a slightly soft switch. The shape looks like a stretched-out S, so it is called the sigmoid, or S-shaped, function. In the middle of a network the simpler ReLU is more common: negative numbers become 0 and positive numbers stay as they are, written ReLU(z) = max(0, z); computing it takes just one comparison. There are also smoother versions such as GELU, ELU and SiLU (Whisper uses GELU, Radford et al. 2022; legged_gym, used to train four-legged robots, uses ELU by default, legged_gym; the Llama in step ⑧ uses SiLU). Whichever one it is, they are all called activation functions; this page calls it "the bend".

x1, x2, x3
三条线索：拉力（N），看到有手握着的把握（0 到 1），手臂速度（m/s）the three clues: pull force (N), how sure it is that a hand is holding the glass (0 to 1), arm speed (m/s)
w1, w2, w3
权重：每条线索的分量。它们的单位正好抵掉线索的单位，比如 w1 是"每牛顿几分"weights: how much each clue counts. Their units exactly cancel the clues’ units; w1, for example, is in "points per newton"
b
偏置：不看任何线索时的起始分数，也就是门槛的负数bias: the starting score before any clue is taken into account, which is minus the threshold
z
加权和，也叫分数，可以是任意实数the weighted sum, also called the score; it can be any real number
σ, y
"弯一下"的函数（这里是 S 形函数）；神经元的输出the function that does "the bend" (here the sigmoid); the neuron’s output
e
自然常数，约 2.718；e−z 在 z 很大时接近 0，在 z 很小时很大Euler’s number, about 2.718; e−z is close to 0 when z is large and very large when z is very negative

先算三个乘积：6 × 0.5 = 3，1 × 3 = 3，0 × (−10) = 0。加起来是 6，再加偏置：6 + (−4) = 2。弯一下：e−2 ≈ 0.135，y = 1 / 1.135 ≈ 0.88。有八成八的把握该松手，松开。换一个时刻：杯子在半路晃了一下，拉力 2 N，相机没看到手（x2 = 0），手臂停着：z = 2 × 0.5 + 0 + 0 − 4 = −3，y = 1 / (1 + e3) ≈ 0.05，不松。再换一个：你的手握住了、拉了 6 N，可手臂还在以 0.5 m/s 移动：z = 3 + 3 − 5 − 4 = −3，也是 0.05，不松。First the three products: 6 × 0.5 = 3, 1 × 3 = 3, 0 × (−10) = 0. They add up to 6; add the bias: 6 + (−4) = 2. Bend: e−2 ≈ 0.135, y = 1 / 1.135 ≈ 0.88. It is 88% sure it should let go, so it lets go. Another moment: the glass wobbles on the way, the pull is 2 N, the camera sees no hand (x2 = 0) and the arm is not moving: z = 2 × 0.5 + 0 + 0 − 4 = −3, y = 1 / (1 + e3) ≈ 0.05, so it holds on. One more: your hand has hold of the glass and pulls with 6 N, but the arm is still moving at 0.5 m/s: z = 3 + 3 − 5 − 4 = −3, again 0.05, so it holds on.

这个神经元一共只有 4 个可以学的数：3 个权重和 1 个偏置，这就是它的全部参数。用 FP32 存，每个 4 字节，一共 16 字节；用 BF16 存，8 字节。算一次要 3 次乘法和 3 次加法（两次把乘积加起来，一次加偏置），一共 6 次运算，再加上弯一下的那一点点。记住这个比例：每个权重带来 1 次乘法和 1 次加法，也就是 2 次运算。后面不管网络多大，"参数有多少"和"算一次要多少运算"都从这里数起。This neuron has only 4 numbers that can be learned, 3 weights and 1 bias, and those are all its parameters. Stored in FP32 at 4 bytes each, they take 16 bytes in all; in BF16, 8 bytes. Computing it once takes 3 multiplications and 3 additions (two to add up the products, one to add the bias), 6 operations in all, plus a tiny bit more for the bend. Remember this ratio: each weight brings 1 multiplication and 1 addition, that is, 2 operations. However big the networks get later on, "how many parameters" and "how many operations per call" are counted from here.

换个角度看这个神经元。把 x3 固定成 0，只看拉力和"看到有手"：z = 0 的地方是一条直线 0.5 x1 + 3 x2 − 4 = 0，线的一边该松，另一边不该松。一个神经元能做的判断，就是在线索构成的空间里画一条直的分界线（三条线索时是一个平面），再把离线远近变成把握的大小。权重决定线的方向，偏置决定线的位置。Look at this neuron another way. Fix x3 at 0 and look only at the pull and "sees a hand": the points where z = 0 form a straight line, 0.5 x1 + 3 x2 − 4 = 0; on one side of the line it should let go, on the other it shouldn’t. The judgement one neuron can make is to draw one straight dividing line through the space of the clues (with three clues, a plane) and then turn the distance from the line into how sure it is. The weights set the line’s direction, and the bias sets its position.

这些权重是谁定的？刚才是我们手写的，真的网络里没人手写：它们是从大量例子里学出来的，先随便取一些数，看输出和正确答案差多少，再把每个权重往"让差距变小"的方向拧一点，重复几百万次（第 ③ 步末尾和第 9 篇）。所以一个训练好的网络，说到底就是一串数：几个，或者几十亿个。Who decides these weights? Just now we wrote them by hand; in a real network nobody does. They are learned from lots of examples: start with some arbitrary numbers, see how far the output is from the right answer, nudge each weight a little in the direction that "makes the gap smaller", and repeat millions of times (the end of step ③, and page 9). So a trained network is, in the end, just a list of numbers: a few of them, or several billion.

  • 左边三个格子是三条线索，线上写着权重，中间的 Σ 格子把乘积和偏置加起来，右边是弯一下以后的输出。The three boxes on the left are the three clues, with the weights written on the lines; the Σ box in the middle adds up the products and the bias; on the right is the output after the bend.
  • ▶ 播放 或者 下一步、上一步、重来，一次算一个乘积，Σ 格子里显示加到现在的和，下面一栏一行一行记下算式。▶ Play, or Next step, Back and Restart: one product at a time, with the sum so far shown in the Σ box and the working written out line by line in the panel below.
  • 拖三个滑块改线索：拉力 F、人手握住了吗、手臂速度 v；改权重 打开 w1、w2、w3 和 b 的滑块，还原权重 放回原来的数。三个按钮换"弯一下"的方式：不弯、ReLU、S 形。Drag the three sliders to change the clues: Pull force F, Hand holds it, Arm speed v. Edit weights opens sliders for w1, w2, w3 and b, and Reset weights puts the original numbers back. Three buttons change the bend: None, ReLU, Sigmoid.
  • 三个场景：你已经握住杯子了、杯子晃了一下、手臂还在动。下面的小图是输出随拉力变化的曲线，点是现在的位置，虚线是"过了一半"的拉力门槛 F*。Three scenes: Your hand holds the glass, The glass wobbled, The arm is still moving. The small chart below shows the output against the pull force; the dot is where you are now, and the dashed line is the pull threshold F* where the output "passes one half".

先玩：选"你已经握住杯子了"，把拉力从 0 拖到 10 N：拉力过了多少牛，输出才超过一半？把"弯一下"换成 ReLU 和"不弯"，曲线变成什么形状？哪一种最像一个开关？Try first: choose "Your hand holds the glass" and drag the pull from 0 to 10 N: how many newtons of pull does it take before the output goes above one half? Switch the bend to ReLU and then to "None": what shape does the curve take? Which one is most like a switch?

    这一步的要点：一个神经元 = 每个输入乘一个权重，加起来，加一个偏置，再弯一下。它有"输入个数 + 1"个参数，每个权重算一次带来 2 次运算。弯一下把分数变成像开关一样的判断；没有它，网络只会"乘上固定的权重再相加"，叠多少层都只能画直的分界线（第 ③ 步）。The point of this step: a neuron = multiply each input by a weight, add them up, add a bias, then bend. It has "number of inputs + 1" parameters, and each weight brings 2 operations per call. The bend turns the score into a switch-like decision; without it, a network can only "multiply by fixed weights and add", and however many layers it stacks, it can only draw straight dividing lines (step ③).

    第 ② 步 · 一层Step ② · One layer

    很多个神经元并排，就是一次矩阵乘向量Many neurons side by side: one matrix times a vector

    一个神经元只回答一个问题。夹爪那一刻要回答的其实不止一个：该松开吗？该握紧吗（被拉了，却没看到人的手，可能是撞到了什么）？该停下吗？该往后退一点吗？给每个问题一个神经元，四个神经元看的是同样三条线索，只是各有各的权重和偏置。四个并排，就是一层：One neuron answers only one question. At that moment the gripper actually has more than one to answer: should it let go? Should it squeeze (it is being pulled but sees no human hand, so it may have hit something)? Should it stop? Should it back off a little? Give each question its own neuron: the four neurons look at the same three clues, each with its own weights and bias. Four side by side make one layer:

    • 松手：权重 (0.5, 3, −10)，偏置 −4（第 ① 步那个）Let go: weights (0.5, 3, −10), bias −4 (the one from step ①)
    • 握紧：权重 (0.5, −3, 0)，偏置 −1：被拉得越用力越该握紧，可看到有人握着就不该Squeeze: weights (0.5, −3, 0), bias −1: the harder it is pulled, the more it should squeeze, but not if it sees someone holding on
    • 停下：权重 (0, 2, 5)，偏置 −1：看到有人的手，或者自己还在动，都该停Stop: weights (0, 2, 5), bias −1: it should stop if it sees a human hand, or if it is still moving itself
    • 后退：权重 (−0.5, 0, 0)，偏置 1：没什么拉力时，往后让一让Back off: weights (−0.5, 0, 0), bias 1: when there is hardly any pull, move back a little to make room

    把这四行权重上下摞起来，就是一张 4 行 3 列的表，数学上叫矩阵，记作 W；三条线索排成一列，叫向量 x。"每个神经元算一遍加权和"，正好就是"矩阵乘向量"：W 的每一行和 x 对应相乘、加起来，得到一个数。四行得到四个数，再各加各的偏置，各弯一下。Stack these four rows of weights one above the other and you get a table of 4 rows and 3 columns, which mathematics calls a matrix, written W; the three clues written in a column are called a vector x. "Each neuron works out its weighted sum" is exactly "a matrix times a vector": multiply each row of W by x entry by entry and add up, and you get one number. Four rows give four numbers; then each adds its own bias and gets its own bend.

    z = W x + bh = σ(z)P = nin nout + noutF ≈ 2 nin nout

    一层的输出，等于权重矩阵乘上输入向量，加上偏置向量，再一个一个弯一下。这一层的参数个数是"输入个数乘输出个数"个权重，加上"输出个数"个偏置；算一个输入要做的运算，大约是权重个数的 2 倍。A layer’s output is the weight matrix times the input vector, plus the bias vector, with each number then bent on its own. The layer’s parameters are "number of inputs times number of outputs" weights plus "number of outputs" biases; the operations needed for one input are about 2 times the number of weights.

    每个输出是一个神经元，每个神经元要把所有输入看一遍，所以权重排成一张"输出个数 × 输入个数"的表（nout 行、nin 列），一个格子对应一条连线：从第 j 个输入到第 i 个输出的那条线，权重就是 W 第 i 行第 j 列的那个数。每个格子算一次是 1 次乘法加 1 次加法，所以运算是权重个数的 2 倍（偏置那几次加法很少，忽略）。每个输入都连到这一层的每个神经元，所以这种层叫全连接层。写成矩阵还有一个实际的好处：GPU 和专用芯片里，专门有一大片电路是为"矩阵乘"做的（第 13 篇），网络里绝大部分运算都能变成它。Each output is one neuron, and each neuron looks at all the inputs, so the weights form an "outputs × inputs" table (nout rows, nin columns), and each cell is one connection: the weight on the line from input j to output i is the number in row i, column j of W. Each cell costs 1 multiplication and 1 addition, so the operations are 2 times the number of weights (the few additions for the biases are negligible, so we ignore them). Every input is connected to every neuron in the layer, so this kind of layer is called a dense (fully connected) layer. Writing it as a matrix has a practical benefit too: GPUs and dedicated chips have a large area of circuitry built just for "matrix multiply" (page 13), and almost all the operations in a network can be turned into it.

    W
    权重矩阵，nout 行、nin 列；第 i 行是第 i 个神经元的权重weight matrix, nout rows and nin columns; row i holds the weights of neuron i
    x, b
    输入向量（nin 个数）；偏置向量（nout 个数）input vector (nin numbers); bias vector (nout numbers)
    z, h
    这一层的加权和（nout 个数）；弯一下以后的输出，送给下一层this layer’s weighted sums (nout numbers); the output after the bend, passed on to the next layer
    nin, nout
    输入个数，输出个数（神经元个数）number of inputs, number of outputs (number of neurons)
    P, F
    参数个数；算一个输入要做的运算次数（FLOP）number of parameters; number of operations needed for one input (FLOP)

    逐行算：松手 0.5 × 6 + 3 × 1 + (−10) × 0 − 4 = 2；握紧 0.5 × 6 + (−3) × 1 + 0 − 1 = −1；停下 0 + 2 × 1 + 5 × 0 − 1 = 1；后退 −0.5 × 6 + 0 + 0 + 1 = −2。z = (2, −1, 1, −2)。用 ReLU 弯一下，负数变 0：h = (2, 0, 1, 0)，"松手"2 分，"停下"1 分，另外两个没有。参数：4 × 3 = 12 个权重，加 4 个偏置，一共 16 个，FP32 是 64 字节。运算：12 次乘加，24 次运算。Row by row: let go 0.5 × 6 + 3 × 1 + (−10) × 0 − 4 = 2; squeeze 0.5 × 6 + (−3) × 1 + 0 − 1 = −1; stop 0 + 2 × 1 + 5 × 0 − 1 = 1; back off −0.5 × 6 + 0 + 0 + 1 = −2. z = (2, −1, 1, −2). Bend with ReLU, so negative numbers become 0: h = (2, 0, 1, 0), "let go" scores 2, "stop" scores 1, the other two nothing. Parameters: 4 × 3 = 12 weights plus 4 biases, 16 in all, 64 bytes in FP32. Operations: 12 multiply–adds, 24 operations.

    这一层把 3 个数变成了 4 个数，可以把它看成"换一种说法描述同一个时刻"：原来说的是"拉力 6 N、看到有手、手臂停着"，现在说的是"松手 2 分、握紧 0、停下 1 分、后退 0"。网络一层层地换说法，每一层都比上一层更接近要做的决定。This layer turned 3 numbers into 4. You can think of it as "describing the same moment in other words": before, it said "pull 6 N, a hand in sight, arm stopped"; now it says "let go 2, squeeze 0, stop 1, back off 0". A network rephrases layer by layer, and each layer is closer than the one before to the decision it has to make.

    一次喂几个：从矩阵乘向量到矩阵乘矩阵Feeding several at once: from matrix times vector to matrix times matrix

    如果同时有三个时刻要判断（你握住杯子的那一刻、杯子晃了一下的那一刻、手臂还在动的那一刻），可以把三个输入向量并排，拼成一个 3 × 3 的表 X（一列是一个时刻），一次算完：W X 是一张 4 × 3 的表，每一列是一个时刻的四个分数。这叫一批（batch），一批有 B 个输入（这里的 B 是个数，不是字节）。运算变成 B 倍，可权重还是那 16 个，读一次就用了 B 次。第 13 篇会讲到，读权重往往比做乘法更慢，所以一次多喂几个输入，芯片的效率会高很多。If there are three moments to judge at the same time (the moment your hand takes the glass, the moment the glass wobbles, the moment the arm is still moving), you can put the three input vectors side by side to make a 3 × 3 table X (one column per moment) and compute them all in one go: W X is a 4 × 3 table, and each column holds one moment’s four scores. This is called a batch, and a batch holds B inputs (here B is a count, not bytes). The operations grow B times, but the weights are still the same 16: read once, used B times. As page 13 will explain, reading the weights is often slower than doing the multiplications, so feeding in several inputs at once makes the chip much more efficient.

    真实大小At real size

    玩具层只有 16 个参数。真实网络里的一层大得多：大语言模型 Llama 3.1 8B 里，把每个词元的 4096 个数变成"查询"的那一层，就是一个 4096 进、4096 出的全连接层：4096 × 4096 = 16,777,216 个参数（约 1678 万个；Llama 的这些层不带偏置，所以不加 4096），用 BF16 存，每个 2 字节，一共 33,554,432 字节，约 33.6 MB；每处理一个词元，每个权重乘加一次，约 1678 万次乘加，也就是约 3355 万次运算，写作 33.6 MFLOP。这个模型里像这样的矩阵有两百多个（第 ⑧ 步）。可算法和上面的玩具一模一样：一行一行地乘、加。The toy layer has only 16 parameters. A layer in a real network is far bigger. In the large language model Llama 3.1 8B, the layer that turns each token’s 4096 numbers into a "query" is a dense layer with 4096 in and 4096 out: 4096 × 4096 = 16,777,216 parameters (about 16.8 million; these layers in Llama have no bias, so no 4096 is added). Stored in BF16 at 2 bytes each, that is 33,554,432 bytes, about 33.6 MB. For each token processed, every weight does one multiply–add: about 16.8 million multiply–adds, or about 33.6 million operations, written 33.6 MFLOP. The model has more than two hundred matrices like this (step ⑧). Yet the method is exactly the same as for the toy above: multiply and add, row by row.

    • 画法：画成网络（3 个输入点连到 4 个神经元，线的粗细是权重的大小，虚线是负的）和 写成表格（4 × 3 的矩阵 W、输入 x、偏置 b、结果）。两种画法是同一件事，切换时进度不变。View: As a network (3 input dots connected to 4 neurons; the thickness of a line shows the size of its weight, and dashed lines are negative) and As a table (the 4 × 3 matrix W, input x, bias b, result). The two views show the same thing, and switching keeps your progress.
    • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：一行一行地算，每算一个乘积，网络里对应的线和表格里对应的格子同时亮起来；下面一栏一行一行记下算式。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: compute row by row; with each product, the matching line in the network and the matching cell in the table light up together, and the panel below writes down the working line by line.
    • 一次喂三个：三个时刻一起算，输入变成 3 × 3 的表，输出变成 4 × 3 的表（一列一个时刻）；看计数器里运算变成多少，每个权重用了几次。Three inputs at once: compute three moments together; the input becomes a 3 × 3 table and the output a 4 × 3 table (one column per moment). Watch the counters: how many operations it takes now, and how many times each weight is used.
    • 换成真实大小：四个预设，玩具 3 → 4、手的策略 82 → 512、Llama 投影 4096 → 4096、Llama MLP 4096 → 14336；也可以拖 nin、nout，选精度，开关 带偏置（Llama 的这些层不带偏置），看参数、字节和每个输入的运算。Real sizes: four presets, Toy 3 → 4, Hand policy 82 → 512, Llama projection 4096 → 4096 and Llama MLP 4096 → 14336; you can also drag nin and nout, choose the precision and switch With bias on or off (these layers in Llama have no bias), and see the parameters, bytes and operations per input.

    先玩：在表格里一行一行地看：第二行"握紧"为什么最后是 0？点"一次喂三个"：运算变成了几倍，要读的权重变成了几倍？在真实大小里选 Llama 投影 4096 → 4096，再换成 Llama MLP 4096 → 14336：这一层占多少字节，是前一层的几倍？Try first: Go through the table row by row: why does the second row, "squeeze", end up at 0? Press "Three inputs at once": how many times more operations are there, and how many times more weights have to be read? Under Real sizes, choose Llama projection 4096 → 4096, then switch to Llama MLP 4096 → 14336: how many bytes does this layer take, and how many times as many as the one before?

      这一步的要点：一层 = 一排神经元 = 一次矩阵乘向量。W 的每一行是一个神经元，每个格子是一条连线。参数 = nin × nout + nout，算一个输入约 2 × nin × nout 次运算；一次喂 B 个输入，运算变成 B 倍，权重却只读一次。The point of this step: One layer = neurons side by side = one matrix times a vector. Each row of W is one neuron, and each cell is one connection. Parameters = nin × nout + nout, and one input takes about 2 × nin × nout operations; feed in B inputs at once and the operations grow B times, yet the weights are read only once.

      第 ③ 步 · 叠起来Step ③ · Stacking up

      一层接一层：多层感知机Layer after layer: the multilayer perceptron

      第 ② 步那一层给出了四个分数：松手 2、握紧 0、停下 1、后退 0。这还不是决定。把这四个数当成新的输入，再接一层，这一层只有两个神经元，最后的两个选项：松手，还是握着。The layer in step ② gave four scores: let go 2, squeeze 0, stop 1, back off 0. That is not yet a decision. Take these four numbers as new inputs and add another layer. This one has only two neurons, for the two final options: let go or keep holding.

      • 松手：权重 (1, 0, 0.5, 0)，偏置 0：第一层的"松手"分数，加上一半"停下"分数Let go: weights (1, 0, 0.5, 0), bias 0: the first layer’s "let go" score plus half its "stop" score
      • 握着：权重 (−1, 1, 0, 1)，偏置 1：先给 1 分，第一层越想松手越减分，想握紧、想后退（没什么拉力）都加分Keep holding: weights (−1, 1, 0, 1), bias 1: start from 1 point; the more the first layer wants to let go, the more is taken off, while wanting to squeeze or to back off (little pull) both add points

      算一下：松手 1 × 2 + 0 × 0 + 0.5 × 1 + 0 × 0 + 0 = 2.5；握着 −1 × 2 + 1 × 0 + 0 × 1 + 1 × 0 + 1 = −1。两个分数还要变成两个加起来等于 1 的"把握"，用的是 softmax：每个分数先取 e 的这么多次方（都变成正数，大的变得更大），再除以它们的和。e2.5 ≈ 12.18，e−1 ≈ 0.37，松手的把握是 12.18 / 12.55 ≈ 0.971，握着是 0.029。这样两层叠起来的网络（3 个输入，中间 4 个数，2 个输出），就是一个最小的多层感知机（MLP）。中间那 4 个数既不是输入也不是输出，叫隐藏层。Work it out: let go 1 × 2 + 0 × 0 + 0.5 × 1 + 0 × 0 + 0 = 2.5; keep holding −1 × 2 + 1 × 0 + 0 × 1 + 1 × 0 + 1 = −1. The two scores still have to become two "confidences" that add up to 1, and this is done by the softmax: raise e to the power of each score (everything becomes positive, and the big ones get bigger), then divide each by their sum. e2.5 ≈ 12.18 and e−1 ≈ 0.37, so the confidence for let go is 12.18 / 12.55 ≈ 0.971, and for keep holding 0.029. A network of two stacked layers like this (3 inputs, 4 numbers in the middle, 2 outputs) is the smallest multilayer perceptron (MLP). The 4 numbers in the middle are neither inputs nor outputs; they are called the hidden layer.

      h = σ(W1 x + b1)z = W2 h + b2pi = ezi / Σj ezj

      第一层把输入乘上第一张权重表、加偏置、弯一下，得到隐藏层 h；第二层把 h 乘上第二张表、加偏置，得到分数 z；最后把每个分数取指数，除以全部指数的和，得到每个选项的概率。The first layer multiplies the input by the first weight table, adds the biases and bends, giving the hidden layer h; the second layer multiplies h by the second table and adds the biases, giving the scores z; finally, take the exponential of each score and divide by the sum of all the exponentials to get each option’s probability.

      每一层都是第 ② 步那样的"矩阵乘向量"，前一层的输出就是后一层的输入，数据只往一个方向流，所以也叫前馈网络。最后一步为什么用 e 的次方？因为分数可以是负数，概率不行；取指数以后全是正数，而且分数差 1，概率差 e ≈ 2.7 倍，差得越多，大的那个越接近 1。分数一样时，两个各是一半。Each layer is a "matrix times a vector" like the one in step ②, and the output of one layer is the input of the next. Data flows in one direction only, which is why this is also called a feedforward network. Why use powers of e in the last step? Because scores can be negative and probabilities cannot. After the exponential everything is positive, and a score that is 1 higher gives a probability e ≈ 2.7 times as large; the bigger the difference, the closer the larger one gets to 1. When the scores are equal, each gets one half.

      W1, b1
      第一层的权重（4 × 3）和偏置（4 个），就是第 ② 步那一层weights (4 × 3) and biases (4) of the first layer, which is the layer from step ②
      W2, b2
      第二层的权重（2 × 4）和偏置（2 个）weights (2 × 4) and biases (2) of the second layer
      h
      隐藏层：第一层弯一下以后的 4 个数hidden layer: the 4 numbers from the first layer after the bend
      z, zi
      输出层的分数；第 i 个选项的分数scores of the output layer; score of option i
      pi
      第 i 个选项的概率，全部加起来等于 1probability of option i; they all add up to 1
      Σj
      把 j 取遍所有选项，加起来sum with j running over all options

      h = (2, 0, 1, 0)，z = (2.5, −1)，p = (0.971, 0.029)：松开。参数：第一层 16 个，第二层 4 × 2 + 2 = 10 个，一共 26 个；乘加：12 + 8 = 20 次，40 次运算。h = (2, 0, 1, 0), z = (2.5, −1), p = (0.971, 0.029): let go. Parameters: 16 in the first layer, 4 × 2 + 2 = 10 in the second, 26 in all; multiply–adds: 12 + 8 = 20, which is 40 operations.

      "握紧"那个神经元在第一层被弯成了 0，它在第二层的权重再大也不起作用。弯一下不只是把数压平：它决定了这一次哪些神经元说话、哪些闭嘴。换一个输入，说话的神经元就换一批。The "squeeze" neuron was bent to 0 in the first layer, so however large its weight in the second layer, it has no effect. The bend does more than flatten numbers: it decides which neurons speak this time and which stay quiet. Change the input and a different set of neurons speaks.

      去掉弯一下会怎样What happens when you remove the bend

      如果第一层不弯，h 就直接等于第一层的加权和 W1 x + b1 = (2, −1, 1, −2)，第二层得到 (2.5, −4)。奇怪的是，这两层可以合成一层：If the first layer does not bend, h is simply the first layer’s weighted sum W1 x + b1 = (2, −1, 1, −2), and the second layer gives (2.5, −4). Oddly, the two layers can then be merged into one:

      W2(W1 x + b1) + b2 = (W2W1) x + (W2b1 + b2)

      先乘第一张表再乘第二张表，等于先把两张表乘成一张，再乘一次。Multiplying by the first table and then by the second is the same as first multiplying the two tables into one and then multiplying once.

      乘法可以先合并：每一层都只是"乘一个数再加起来"，乘两次还是"乘一个数再加起来"，只是那个数换了。所以不弯的话，叠 100 层也只是一层，画出来的还是一条直线。The multiplications can be combined first: each layer only does "multiply by a number, then add up", and doing that twice is still "multiply by a number, then add up", only with a different number. So without the bend, 100 stacked layers are still just one layer, and what they draw is still a straight line.

      W2W1
      两张权重表相乘得到的一张 2 × 3 的表the single 2 × 3 table you get by multiplying the two weight tables

      W2W1 的第一行 = 1 × (0.5, 3, −10) + 0.5 × (0, 2, 5) = (0.5, 4, −7.5)，第二行是 (−0.5, −6, 10)；合并后的偏置是 (−4.5, 5)。用这一层直接算：0.5 × 6 + 4 × 1 − 4.5 = 2.5，−0.5 × 6 − 6 × 1 + 5 = −4，和两层不弯的结果一模一样。The first row of W2W1 = 1 × (0.5, 3, −10) + 0.5 × (0, 2, 5) = (0.5, 4, −7.5), and the second row is (−0.5, −6, 10); the merged bias is (−4.5, 5). Computing directly with this one layer: 0.5 × 6 + 4 × 1 − 4.5 = 2.5, −0.5 × 6 − 6 × 1 + 5 = −4, exactly the same as the two layers without the bend.

      弯一下才让叠层有意义。比如"拉力在 3 N 到 8 N 之间才松"（太小是晃了一下，太大可能是撞到了东西）：一个神经元只能画一条线，"大于 3"或"小于 8"，没法同时说两句；第一层两个神经元各画一条线、各自弯一下，第二层把它们组合起来，就围出了中间那一段。层数和每层的神经元越多，能围出的形状越复杂。It is the bend that makes stacking layers worthwhile. Take "let go only when the pull is between 3 N and 8 N" (too little is just a wobble, too much may mean it has hit something): one neuron can only draw one line, "more than 3" or "less than 8", and cannot say both at once. Two neurons in the first layer each draw one line and each bend; the second layer combines them and fences off the stretch in between. The more layers, and the more neurons in each, the more complicated the shapes they can fence off.

      机器人的手：真实大小The robot’s hand at real size

      第 0 步里那个每秒算 50 次的手的小策略，就是一个多层感知机，只是大一点。它吃进 82 个数：21 个关节的角度、21 个关节的速度、两个手腕力传感器的 12 个数、手要到的目标位置和朝向 7 个数（位置 3 个，朝向用 4 个数表示），还有上一次发出的 21 个指令。吐出 21 个数，每个关节一个目标。中间三层隐藏层，宽 512、256、128，照训练四足机器人常用的 legged_gym 的默认值（legged_gym），每秒 50 次也和它一样（Rudin 等 2022）。The hand’s small policy from step 0, which runs 50 times a second, is a multilayer perceptron, just a bigger one. It takes in 82 numbers: the angles of the 21 joints, the velocities of the 21 joints, 12 numbers from the two wrist force sensors, 7 numbers for the target position and orientation the hand should reach (3 for the position; the orientation is written with 4 numbers), and the 21 commands it sent last time. It puts out 21 numbers, one target per joint. In between are three hidden layers, 512, 256 and 128 wide, following the defaults of legged_gym, which is widely used to train four-legged robots (legged_gym), and 50 times a second is the rate used there too (Rudin et al. 2022).

      P = Σl (nl−1 nl + nl)F ≈ 2 Σl nl−1 nl ≈ 2P

      多层感知机的参数，是每一层"上一层宽度乘这一层宽度"个权重加上"这一层宽度"个偏置，全部层加起来；算一次的运算，差不多是参数个数的 2 倍。A multilayer perceptron’s parameters are, for each layer, "previous layer’s width times this layer’s width" weights plus "this layer’s width" biases, added up over all layers; the operations for one call are roughly 2 times the number of parameters.

      每个权重对一个输入只用一次：乘一下、加一下，然后这一次就再也用不到它了。所以 MLP 的运算和参数几乎一样多（2 倍）。记住这一点，后面的卷积和 Transformer 会不一样：卷积的一个权重在图上每个位置各用一次，Transformer 的一个权重对每个词元各用一次，一个输入就要用几百、几千次（大语言模型一个一个写词元时，每个权重还是只用一次）。Each weight is used only once per input: one multiply, one add, and then it is not needed again for that input. So an MLP’s operations match its parameters almost one for one (a factor of 2). Remember this, because convolutions and Transformers later on are different: a convolution uses each weight once at every position in the image, and a Transformer uses each weight once for every token, so a single input uses it hundreds or thousands of times (when a large language model writes tokens one at a time, each weight is again used only once).

      nl
      第 l 层有几个神经元（宽度）；n0 是输入的个数number of neurons in layer l (its width); n0 is the number of inputs
      Σl
      把所有层加起来sum over all layers

      82 → 512：82 × 512 + 512 = 42,496；512 → 256：131,328；256 → 128：32,896；128 → 21：2,709。加起来 P = 209,429 个参数，FP32 是 837,716 字节，约 0.84 MB。乘加 41,984 + 131,072 + 32,768 + 2,688 = 208,512 次，运算是它的 2 倍，417,024 次，约 42 万次，写作 F ≈ 0.42 MFLOP；每秒 50 次，每秒约 2085 万次运算，写作约 21 MFLOP/s。82 → 512: 82 × 512 + 512 = 42,496; 512 → 256: 131,328; 256 → 128: 32,896; 128 → 21: 2,709. In total P = 209,429 parameters, 837,716 bytes in FP32, about 0.84 MB. Multiply–adds: 41,984 + 131,072 + 32,768 + 2,688 = 208,512; the operations are 2 times that, 417,024, about 420,000, written F ≈ 0.42 MFLOP. At 50 times a second, that is about 20.9 million operations a second, written as about 21 MFLOP/s.

      参数最多的是中间那层 512 → 256，它一层占了六成多。把三层隐藏层都加宽一倍（1024、512、256），参数变成 746,517 个，是原来的 3.6 倍，不是 2 倍：一层的权重是"上一层宽度乘这一层宽度"，两边都变宽，就接近 4 倍。The layer with the most parameters is the middle one, 512 → 256: that one layer holds more than 60 per cent of them. Double the width of all three hidden layers (1024, 512, 256) and the parameters become 746,517, 3.6 times as many, not 2 times: a layer’s weights are "previous layer’s width times this layer’s width", and when both sides get wider, that comes close to 4 times.

      每秒 2100 万次运算，对这台机器人的计算模块来说几乎不算什么（第 13 篇：它的峰值要按每秒万亿次来数）。这个网络的难处不在算得多，在算得准时：每 20 毫秒必须给出一次答案，晚了关节就收不到新的指令（第 5 篇讲这些一圈套一圈的循环）。For this robot’s compute module, 21 million operations a second is next to nothing (page 13: its peak is counted in trillions of operations a second). The hard part of this network is not computing a lot but computing on time: it must give an answer every 20 milliseconds, or the joints get no new command (page 5 covers these loops within loops).

      权重从哪来Where the weights come from

      上面的权重是我们手写的。真的网络里，209,429 个参数没人能手写，它们是训练出来的：先随便取一些小数；拿一个例子（比如人示范时的关节状态和正确的指令，第 3 篇），正着算一遍，看输出和正确答案差多少，这个差距叫损失；再倒着算一遍，得到每个权重拧一点、损失会跟着变多少，叫梯度（它指向损失变大最快的方向）；每个权重往梯度的反方向拧一小步。换一个例子，重复几百万次。倒着算的办法叫反向传播，第 9 篇专门讲。We wrote the weights above by hand. In a real network nobody can write 209,429 parameters by hand; they come from training. Start with some small random numbers. Take an example (say, the joint states and the correct commands from a human demonstration, page 3), compute forwards once and see how far the output is from the correct answer; this gap is called the loss. Then compute backwards once to find how much the loss changes when each weight is nudged a little; this is called the gradient (it points in the direction in which the loss grows fastest). Nudge each weight a small step against the gradient. Take another example and repeat, millions of times. The way of computing backwards is called backpropagation; page 9 covers this.

      训练比用（推理）贵得多。正着算每个参数约 2 次运算；倒着算要算两样东西，传回上一层的误差和每个权重自己的梯度，各约 2 次，所以训练一个例子约 6P 次运算，是推理的 3 倍（Kaplan 等 2020）。存储也多：除了参数本身（BF16，2 字节），还要存每个参数的梯度（2 字节）、一份 FP32 的参数副本和优化器（常用的叫 Adam）为每个参数记的两个数（共 12 字节）；用混合精度训练时，每个参数约 16 字节（Rajbhandari 等 2020，第 13 篇第 ⑨ 步）。对这只手来说，209,429 × 16 字节 ≈ 3.4 MB，不算什么；对 80 亿参数的大语言模型，是约 128 GB，还没算中间结果。Training costs much more than using the network (inference). Going forwards takes about 2 operations per parameter; going backwards has to compute two things, the error passed back to the previous layer and each weight’s own gradient, about 2 each, so training on one example takes about 6P operations, 3 times as much as inference (Kaplan et al. 2020). It needs more memory too: besides the parameters themselves (BF16, 2 bytes), it has to store each parameter’s gradient (2 bytes), plus an FP32 copy of the parameters and the two numbers the optimiser (usually one called Adam) keeps for each parameter (12 bytes together); in mixed-precision training that comes to about 16 bytes per parameter (Rajbhandari et al. 2020, page 13, step ⑨). For this hand, 209,429 × 16 bytes ≈ 3.4 MB, which is nothing; for a large language model with 8 billion parameters, it is about 128 GB, before counting the intermediate results.

      • 上面是 3 → 4 → 2 的小网络，每个圆圈里写着它现在的值。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：一步算一个神经元，数一层一层地往右流，算完的圆圈填上颜色，下面一栏记下算式，最后一步是 softmax。At the top is the small 3 → 4 → 2 network; each circle shows its current value. ▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: one neuron per step; the numbers flow to the right layer by layer, finished circles fill with colour, the panel below writes down the working, and the last step is the softmax.
      • 去掉弯一下：第一层不再把负数变成 0；下面出现合并以后的那一张 2 × 3 的表，和它算出来的 (2.5, −4)。Remove the bend: the first layer no longer turns negative numbers into 0; below it appear the single merged 2 × 3 table and what it computes, (2.5, −4).
      • 下面 机器人的手：真实大小：每一层画成一张按比例的权重表（高是输出个数，宽是输入个数），旁边一张表写出每层的参数和乘加。拖 宽度（隐藏层宽度乘 0.25 到 4）和 层数（2 到 6 个隐藏层，多出来的层每层比上一层窄一半），看总的参数、字节、每次的运算和每秒的运算。指着或点一下 82 个输入那一条，看它们各是什么。Below, The robot’s hand at real size: each layer is drawn as a weight table to scale (height = number of outputs, width = number of inputs), with a table beside it listing each layer’s parameters and multiply–adds. Drag Width (hidden-layer widths times 0.25 to 4) and Depth (2 to 6 hidden layers; each extra layer is half as wide as the one before it) and watch the total parameters, bytes, operations per call and operations per second. Point at or tap the bar of 82 inputs to see what each of them is.

      先玩：先 ▶ 播放一遍，看"握紧"那个圆圈为什么是 0、它连出去的线有没有起作用。打开"去掉弯一下"：合并成一层以后，"握着"的分数变成多少？在下面把宽度拖到 ×2：参数变成几倍，运算变成几倍？再把层数加到 6：参数涨得多，还是宽度加倍涨得多？Try first: First press ▶ Play and watch it through once: why is the "squeeze" circle 0, and do the lines leaving it have any effect? Turn on "Remove the bend": once the two layers merge into one, what does the "keep holding" score become? Below, drag Width to ×2: how many times more parameters, and how many times more operations? Then raise Depth to 6: do the parameters grow more that way, or by doubling the width?

        这一步的要点：多层感知机 = 全连接层一层层叠起来，层与层之间弯一下；没有弯，叠多少层都等于一层。它的参数是每层 nin × nout + nout 加起来；每个权重对一个输入只用一次，所以算一次约是参数的 2 倍。隐藏层加宽一倍，参数和运算接近 4 倍。权重是训练出来的：训练一个例子约 6P 次运算，每个参数约 16 字节。The point of this step: A multilayer perceptron = dense layers stacked one on another, with a bend between layers; without the bend, any number of layers equals one. Its parameters are nin × nout + nout per layer, added up; each weight is used only once per input, so one call takes about 2 operations per parameter. Doubling the width of the hidden layers makes the parameters and operations nearly 4 times as many. The weights come from training: training on one example takes about 6P operations and about 16 bytes per parameter.

        第 ④ 步 · 卷积Step ④ · Convolution

        一小块权重，滑遍整张图A small grid of weights, slid across the whole picture

        现在换成眼睛。头上那台相机一张照片是 1280 × 720 个像素，每个像素 3 个数（红、绿、蓝），一共 2,764,800 个数。能不能直接接一个全连接层？先看一个小得多的情况：把照片缩到 224 × 224（150,528 个数），让第一层输出 112 × 112 个位置、每个位置 64 个数（802,816 个数）。全连接的话，每个输出连到每个输入，要 150,528 × 802,816 ≈ 1.2 × 1011 个权重，FP32 是约 483 GB，是这台机器人全部内存（128 GB）的将近 4 倍。而且这样学出来的东西也不对劲：左上角的"杯子边"和右下角的"杯子边"要各学一遍。Now switch to the eyes. A picture from the camera on the robot’s head is 1280 × 720 pixels, with 3 numbers per pixel (red, green, blue): 2,764,800 numbers in all. Could we just feed them straight into a dense layer? Look first at a much smaller case: shrink the picture to 224 × 224 (150,528 numbers) and have the first layer output 112 × 112 positions with 64 numbers at each (802,816 numbers). Fully connected, with every output wired to every input, that takes 150,528 × 802,816 ≈ 1.2 × 1011 weights: about 483 GB in FP32, nearly 4 times the robot’s entire memory (128 GB). And what such a layer learns is not right either: a "glass edge" in the top-left corner and a "glass edge" in the bottom-right corner would each have to be learned separately.

        图像有两个特点可以利用。第一，局部：判断一个地方是不是边，只要看它周围几个像素，不用看整张图。第二，到处一样：边出现在左上角还是右下角，长得都一样，可以用同一组权重去找。把这两点用上，就是卷积：拿一小块权重（比如 3 × 3 = 9 个），叫卷积核，在图上一格一格地滑；每到一个位置，把它盖住的 9 个像素和 9 个权重对应相乘、加起来，得到一个数，写在输出的对应位置。Pictures have two properties we can make use of. First, they are local: to tell whether a spot is an edge, you only need the few pixels around it, not the whole picture. Second, they are the same everywhere: an edge looks the same whether it is in the top-left corner or the bottom-right, so one set of weights can look for it everywhere. Put these two to use and you have convolution: take a small grid of weights (say 3 × 3 = 9 of them), called a kernel, and slide it across the picture one pixel at a time; at each position, multiply the 9 pixels it covers by the 9 weights, pair by pair, add up the products to get one number, and write it at the matching position in the output.

        看一个手算的例子：杯子左边缘的一小块，6 × 6 个像素，只看亮度（这里用 0 到 9，真实照片是 0 到 255）。左边是浅色的墙，亮度 8；右下是杯身，暗一些，亮度 2；最上面两行在杯口以上，全是墙。卷积核是"左边一列减右边一列"：Here is an example to work by hand: a small patch at the left edge of the glass, 6 × 6 pixels, brightness only (0 to 9 here; a real photo uses 0 to 255). On the left is the pale wall, brightness 8; at the bottom right is the body of the glass, darker, brightness 2; the top two rows are above the rim, so they are all wall. The kernel is "left column minus right column":

        yi,j = Σu,v wu,v xsi+u, sj+v + bn′ = ⌊(n + 2p − k) / s⌋ + 1

        输出第 i 行第 j 列的数，等于卷积核放在对应位置时，核里每个权重乘上它盖住的像素，全部加起来，再加偏置。输出的边长，等于输入边长加上两边补的零、减去核的边长，除以步长取整，再加 1。The number in row i, column j of the output: put the kernel at the matching position, multiply each weight in the kernel by the pixel it covers, add them all up, then add the bias. The side length of the output: take the input’s side length, add the zeros padded on both sides, subtract the kernel’s side length, divide by the stride and round down, then add 1.

        这还是第 ① 步那个神经元：乘、加、加偏置。区别只有两点：它只看一小块（9 个输入，不是全部），而且同一个神经元在每个位置都用一遍，权重不变。边长公式数的是"核能放下几次"：核的左边从第 0 格开始，每次挪 s 格，右边不能超出输入（两边各补 p 圈零以后的）边界。This is still the neuron from step ①: multiply, add, add the bias. There are only two differences: it looks at just a small patch (9 inputs, not all of them), and the same neuron is used again at every position, with the same weights. The side-length formula counts "how many times the kernel fits": the kernel’s left edge starts at cell 0 and moves s cells each time, and its right edge must not go past the edge of the input (after p rings of zeros have been padded on each side).

        xr,c
        输入（四周补过 p 圈零以后）第 r 行第 c 列的像素（从 0 数起）the pixel in row r, column c of the input (after p rings of zeros have been padded all round), counting from 0
        wu,v
        卷积核第 u 行第 v 列的权重，u 和 v 都从 0 数到 k − 1the kernel’s weight in row u, column v; u and v both count from 0 to k − 1
        yi,j, b
        输出第 i 行第 j 列的数；偏置the number in row i, column j of the output; the bias
        k, s, p
        卷积核的边长；步长：每次挪几格；补零：输入四周各补几圈 0the kernel’s side length; stride: how many cells it moves each time; padding: how many rings of 0s are added all round the input
        n, n′
        输入的边长；输出的边长the input’s side length; the output’s side length
        ⌊ ⌋
        向下取整round down

        核盖住左上角（第 0 到 2 行、第 0 到 2 列）：左列 8 + 8 + 8 = 24，右列也是 24，24 − 24 = 0：没有边。往右挪一格，盖住第 1 到 3 列：左列 24，右列 8 + 8 + 2 = 18，得 6。往下再挪两格，盖住第 2 到 4 行：左列 24，右列 2 + 2 + 2 = 6，得 18。整张输出是 4 × 4（n′ = (6 − 3) / 1 + 1 = 4）：中间两列从上到下是 6、12、18、18，两边全是 0。边在哪里，哪里就亮。乘加一共 16 个位置 × 9 = 144 次，权重只有 9 个。With the kernel over the top-left corner (rows 0 to 2, columns 0 to 2): left column 8 + 8 + 8 = 24, right column also 24, 24 − 24 = 0: no edge. Move one cell to the right, covering columns 1 to 3: left column 24, right column 8 + 8 + 2 = 18, giving 6. Move two cells further down, covering rows 2 to 4: left column 24, right column 2 + 2 + 2 = 6, giving 18. The whole output is 4 × 4 (n′ = (6 − 3) / 1 + 1 = 4): the middle two columns read 6, 12, 18, 18 from top to bottom, and the two outer columns are all 0. Wherever there is an edge, the output lights up. In all, 16 positions × 9 = 144 multiply–adds, and only 9 weights.

        一个卷积核找一种图案。这个核找"左亮右暗"的竖边；把它转 90 度，找横边；9 个权重都是 1/9，就是把周围平均一下（模糊）。训练出来的网络里，第一层的核大多就是这样的边、颜色块；越往后的层，组合出来的图案越复杂。Each kernel looks for one pattern. This one looks for vertical edges that are "bright on the left, dark on the right"; turn it 90 degrees and it finds horizontal edges; make all 9 weights 1/9 and it averages the neighbourhood (a blur). In a trained network, most first-layer kernels are just such edges and patches of colour; the later the layer, the more complex the patterns it puts together.

        通道：每个位置上不止一个数Channels: more than one number at each position

        彩色照片每个像素有红、绿、蓝 3 个数，叫 3 个通道。卷积核因此也是三层的：3 × 3 × 3 = 27 个权重，同时盖住三个通道，27 个乘积加在一起。一个核得到一张输出图，叫特征图：哪里有这个核要找的图案，哪里的数就大。一层通常有几十到几百个核，每个找一种图案，输出就有几十到几百个通道。下一层的核再跨过这些通道，把"竖边""横边""红色块"组合成"杯口的弧线"。In a colour photo each pixel has 3 numbers, red, green and blue, called 3 channels. So the kernel is three deep as well: 3 × 3 × 3 = 27 weights covering all three channels at once, with the 27 products added together. One kernel produces one output picture, called a feature map: wherever the pattern the kernel looks for appears, the numbers are large. A layer usually has tens to hundreds of kernels, each looking for one pattern, so its output has tens to hundreds of channels. The next layer’s kernels then reach across all these channels and combine "vertical edge", "horizontal edge" and "red patch" into "curve of the rim".

        P = k2 Cin Cout + CoutF = 2 k2 Cin Cout n′2

        一层卷积的参数，是"核的面积 × 输入通道数"个权重一个核，一共"输出通道数"个核，再加每个核一个偏置。运算是每个核在每个输出位置做一遍：核的大小 × 核的个数 × 输出位置数，乘加再乘 2。The parameters of a convolution layer: "kernel area × input channels" weights per kernel, "output channels" kernels in all, plus one bias per kernel. The operations: each kernel is run once at each output position, so kernel size × number of kernels × number of output positions multiply–adds, then times 2.

        参数里没有图的大小：同一组核在 224 × 224 和 1280 × 720 的图上滑，权重一个不多。运算里有：图越大，核要放的位置越多。这正是卷积和全连接的分别：全连接的权重随输入变大而变多，卷积的权重不变，变多的是"同一个权重被用了几次"。The size of the picture does not appear in the parameters: the same kernels slide over a 224 × 224 picture and a 1280 × 720 one without a single extra weight. It does appear in the operations: the bigger the picture, the more positions the kernels have to visit. This is exactly the difference between convolution and a dense layer: a dense layer’s weights grow as the input grows; a convolution’s weights stay the same, and what grows is "how many times the same weight is used".

        Cin, Cout
        输入的通道数；输出的通道数（核的个数）number of input channels; number of output channels (the number of kernels)
        k2
        核的面积：k × k 个位置the kernel’s area: k × k positions
        n′2
        输出的位置数：n′ × n′ 个the number of output positions: n′ × n′

        ResNet-18 的第一层：k = 7，Cin = 3，Cout = 64，输入 224 × 224，步长 2，四周补 3 圈零：n′ = ⌊(224 + 6 − 7) / 2⌋ + 1 = 112。权重 7 × 7 × 3 × 64 = 9,408 个（ResNet 的卷积不带偏置，偏置的事交给后面的归一化），乘加 9,408 × 112 × 112 = 118,013,952 次，约 0.24 GFLOP。同样的输入输出用全连接层要 1.2 × 1011 个权重，是它的约 1280 万倍。ResNet-18’s first layer: k = 7, Cin = 3, Cout = 64, input 224 × 224, stride 2, 3 rings of zeros padded all round: n′ = ⌊(224 + 6 − 7) / 2⌋ + 1 = 112. Weights: 7 × 7 × 3 × 64 = 9,408 (ResNet’s convolutions have no bias; that job is left to the normalisation that follows). Multiply–adds: 9,408 × 112 × 112 = 118,013,952, about 0.24 GFLOP. A dense layer with the same input and output would need 1.2 × 1011 weights, about 12.8 million times as many.

        这一层每个权重被用了 112 × 112 = 12,544 次。参数少、运算多，是卷积网络的样子：几十 MB 的权重（第 ⑤ 步的 ResNet-18 用 FP32 约 47 MB），一张图却要算几十亿次。In this layer each weight is used 112 × 112 = 12,544 times. Few parameters, many operations: that is the shape of a convolutional network. Tens of MB of weights (ResNet-18 in step ⑤ is about 47 MB in FP32), yet a single picture takes several billion operations.

        越叠看得越远，池化让图变小Stack more and you see further; pooling shrinks the picture

        一个 3 × 3 的核只看 3 × 3 个像素。可第二层的一个数看的是第一层的 3 × 3 个数，每个又看 3 × 3 个像素，合起来是 5 × 5；叠 L 层 3 × 3 的卷积（步长 1），一个输出能看到 (1 + 2L) × (1 + 2L) 个像素，这个范围叫感受野。光靠叠，感受野长得太慢，所以网络会隔几层把图缩小一半：要么用步长 2 的卷积，要么用池化：把每 2 × 2（或 3 × 3）个数里最大的留下（取最大），或者取平均。池化没有参数，图变小以后，同样 3 × 3 的核就能看到原图上大一倍的范围，运算也少了四分之三。A 3 × 3 kernel sees only 3 × 3 pixels. But a number in the second layer looks at 3 × 3 numbers in the first layer, each of which looks at 3 × 3 pixels, so together it sees 5 × 5; stack L layers of 3 × 3 convolution (stride 1) and one output can see (1 + 2L) × (1 + 2L) pixels. This area is called the receptive field. Stacking alone makes the receptive field grow too slowly, so every few layers the network halves the picture: either with a stride-2 convolution, or with pooling: from each 2 × 2 (or 3 × 3) group of numbers, keep the largest (max pooling) or take the average. Pooling has no parameters. Once the picture is smaller, the same 3 × 3 kernel covers an area twice as wide on the original picture, and the operations fall by three quarters.

        • 上面是那块 6 × 6 的像素（青框是 3 × 3 的窗口）和 4 × 4 的输出；旁边三个小格子是"窗口里的数 × 卷积核 = 乘积"，再加起来得到 Σ。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步：窗口一格一格地滑，9 个乘积一个个出来，Σ 落进输出的格子；算完 一下子填满，点任何一个输出格子直接跳过去。At the top are the 6 × 6 patch of pixels (the teal box is the 3 × 3 window) and the 4 × 4 output; next to them, three small grids show "window × kernel = products", which then add up to Σ. ▶ Play, Next step, Back: the window slides one cell at a time, the 9 products appear one by one, and Σ drops into its output cell; Finish fills everything in at once, and tapping any output cell jumps straight to it.
        • 换卷积核：竖直的边、水平的边、模糊。两个开关：补一圈零（输出变成 6 × 6）、步长 2；公式 n′ 跟着显示现在的数。Change the kernel: Vertical edge, Horizontal edge, Blur. Two switches: Pad with zeros (the output becomes 6 × 6) and Stride 2; the formula for n′ follows along, showing the current numbers.
        • 真实的一层：两个预设，ResNet-18 的第一层（默认）和 ResNet-18 layer3 里的一层；也可以拖 k、Cin、Cout、输入边长 n 和步长 s（补零自动取 (k − 1) / 2），看输出的形状、参数和乘加。下面一行是"同样的输入输出用全连接层"要多少权重。A real layer: two presets, ResNet-18’s first layer (the default) and A layer inside ResNet-18’s layer3; you can also drag k, Cin, Cout, the input side n and the stride s (padding is set to (k − 1) / 2 automatically) and see the output’s shape, the parameters and the multiply–adds. The line below shows how many weights "a dense layer with the same input and output" would need.
        • 最下面是一张画出来的厨房小图，每个像素是 0 到 9 的一个数（青框里就是上面那 6 × 6 块），和用现在这个卷积核算出来的特征图。At the bottom is a small drawn picture of the kitchen, each pixel a number from 0 to 9 (the teal box holds the 6 × 6 patch from above), together with the feature map computed with the current kernel.

        先玩：一步一步看窗口滑到杯子边上，9 个乘积为什么加起来是 18。换成"水平的边"：输出哪里不是 0，为什么？先用公式算一下，再打开"补一圈零"和"步长 2"看对不对。在真实的一层里把 Cout 从 64 拖到 128，参数和运算各变成几倍；把输入边长从 224 拖到 448 呢？换成 layer3 里的那一层：参数是第一层的几倍，乘加呢？Try first: step through as the window slides onto the edge of the glass: why do the 9 products add up to 18? Switch to "Horizontal edge": where is the output not 0, and why? Work it out with the formula first, then turn on "Pad with zeros" and "Stride 2" to see if you were right. In the real layer, drag Cout from 64 to 128: by what factor do the parameters and the operations each grow? And if you drag the input side from 224 to 448? Switch to the layer inside layer3: its parameters are how many times the first layer’s? And its multiply–adds?

        这一步的要点：卷积 = 一个只看一小块的神经元，在图上每个位置用同一组权重算一遍。参数 k² × Cin × Cout 和图的大小无关；运算要再乘上输出的位置数 n′²，所以图越大越贵，每个权重被用了成千上万次。叠层和池化让一个数看到的范围越来越大。The point of this step: convolution = a neuron that looks at only a small patch, computed at every position in the picture with the same set of weights. The parameters, k² × Cin × Cout, do not depend on the size of the picture; the operations are further multiplied by the number of output positions, n′², so the bigger the picture, the higher the cost, and each weight is used thousands of times. Stacking layers and pooling let each number see a larger and larger area.

        第 ⑤ 步 · 一个真正的卷积网络Step ⑤ · A real convolutional network

        ResNet-18：一张照片怎样变成"这是杯子"ResNet-18: how a photo becomes "this is a glass"

        把第 ④ 步的零件串起来，就是一个卷积神经网络（CNN）。拿 ResNet-18 当例子（He 等 2016）：它在 ImageNet 上训练，吃进一张 224 × 224 的彩色照片，吐出 1000 个分数，每个对应一类东西，里面有好几种杯子。机器人的模块化那条路，就可以用它先认出"画面中间是一个杯子"。它一路上是这样变的：String the parts from step ④ together and you have a convolutional neural network (CNN). Take ResNet-18 as the example (He et al. 2016): trained on ImageNet, it takes in a 224 × 224 colour photo and gives out 1000 scores, one for each class of object, among them several kinds of glass. The robot’s modular route can use it to recognise first that "there is a glass in the middle of the picture". Along the way, the data changes like this:

        • 输入：224 × 224 × 3，150,528 个数。Input: 224 × 224 × 3, 150,528 numbers.
        • 第一层：7 × 7 的卷积，64 个核，步长 2（第 ④ 步算过）→ 112 × 112 × 64；再一个 3 × 3、步长 2 的取最大池化 → 56 × 56 × 64。First layer: a 7 × 7 convolution, 64 kernels, stride 2 (worked out in step ④) → 112 × 112 × 64; then a 3 × 3 max pooling with stride 2 → 56 × 56 × 64.
        • 四个阶段：每个阶段两个"残差块"，每块两层 3 × 3 卷积。第一阶段保持 56 × 56 × 64；后面三个阶段一开始都用步长 2 把边长减半、通道加倍：28 × 28 × 128 → 14 × 14 × 256 → 7 × 7 × 512。图越来越小，每个位置上的数越来越多。Four stages: each stage has two "residual blocks", each block two 3 × 3 convolution layers. Stage 1 stays at 56 × 56 × 64; the next three stages each begin with stride 2, halving the side length and doubling the channels: 28 × 28 × 128 → 14 × 14 × 256 → 7 × 7 × 512. The picture gets smaller and smaller, and each position holds more and more numbers.
        • 收尾：把 7 × 7 个位置上的数取平均，变成 512 个数（全局平均池化）；一个 512 → 1000 的全连接层，给出 1000 个分数；softmax 变成概率。Wrapping up: average the numbers over the 7 × 7 positions to get 512 numbers (global average pooling); a 512 → 1000 dense layer gives the 1000 scores; softmax turns them into probabilities.

        "18"数的是带权重的层：第一层 1 个，四个阶段 16 个，最后的全连接层 1 个。可以把它想成一条流水线：前面的层找边和颜色，中间的层把它们拼成弧线、角、纹理，后面的层拼成"杯口""把手""玻璃的反光"，最后一层根据这些判断是什么东西。The "18" counts the layers with weights: 1 for the first layer, 16 in the four stages and 1 for the final dense layer. You can think of it as an assembly line: the early layers find edges and colours, the middle layers put these together into curves, corners and textures, the later layers put those together into "rim", "handle" and "reflections on the glass", and the last layer decides from all this what the object is.

        残差连接和归一化：让深网络训练得动Residual connections and normalisation: making deep networks trainable

        y = σ(x + f(x))ẑ = γ (z − μ) / s + β

        一个残差块的输出，等于它的输入加上两层卷积算出来的"改动"，再弯一下。归一化：每个通道先减去平均值、除以标准差，再乘一个可以学的 γ、加一个可以学的 β。A residual block’s output: take its input, add the "change" computed by the two convolution layers, then bend it. Normalisation: for each channel, subtract the mean and divide by the standard deviation, then multiply by a learnable γ and add a learnable β.

        He 等人在 ResNet 的论文里试过：普通的卷积网络从 18 层加到 34 层，连训练时的误差都变大了。这不是过拟合，也不是信号或梯度在半路消失了（网络里有归一化，他们查过，两者的大小都正常），而是难训练：多叠的那几层，连"原样传过去"都很难学会。残差连接给了一条直路：输入原样跳过这两层，和它们的输出加在一起，这两层只需要学"在原来的基础上改一点"。一层什么都不学（f = 0）时，整块就是原样传过去，至少不会更差。He 等人靠它把网络做到了 152 层。归一化管的是另一件事：每层输出的数可能越变越大或越变越小，把每个通道拉回平均 0、标准差 1 左右，下一层就总在一个好学的范围里工作；γ 和 β 让网络自己决定要不要再放大、挪动。He et al. tested this in the ResNet paper: when a plain convolutional network went from 18 to 34 layers, even its training error went up. This was not overfitting, nor signals or gradients fading away along the way (the network had normalisation, and they checked that both were of normal size); the problem was training: the extra layers found it hard to learn even "pass it through unchanged". A residual connection provides a shortcut: the input skips past the two layers unchanged and is added to their output, so the two layers only need to learn "a small change on top of what is already there". If those layers learn nothing (f = 0), the whole block just passes its input through unchanged, so at least it is no worse. With this, He et al. took the network to 152 layers. Normalisation deals with something else: the numbers each layer puts out can drift larger and larger or smaller and smaller; pulling each channel back to a mean of about 0 and a standard deviation of about 1 keeps the next layer always working in a range where learning is easy; γ and β let the network decide for itself whether to scale and shift them again.

        x, y
        残差块的输入和输出，形状一样（比如 56 × 56 × 64）the residual block’s input and output, which have the same shape (for example 56 × 56 × 64)
        f(x)
        块里的两层：卷积 → 归一化 → 弯一下 → 卷积 → 归一化the two layers inside the block: convolution → normalisation → bend → convolution → normalisation
        z, ẑ
        卷积算出来、还没弯一下的一个数（第 ① 步的加权和）；归一化以后的值a number computed by the convolution, before the bend (the weighted sum from step ①); its value after normalisation
        μ, s
