        这个通道的数在这一批图片、所有位置上的平均值和标准差（这里的 s 不是步长）the mean and standard deviation of this channel’s numbers across this batch of pictures and all positions (here s is not the stride)
        γ, β
        每个通道两个可以学的数：放大多少、挪多少two learnable numbers per channel: how much to scale and how much to shift

        第一阶段的一个块：两层卷积各 3 × 3 × 64 × 64 = 36,864 个权重，加上归一化 64 × 2 = 128 个，各 36,992 个参数；每层在 56 × 56 个位置上做 36,864 次乘加，约 1.16 亿次。加法那条直路没有参数，只多了 56 × 56 × 64 次加法。阶段之间边长减半、通道加倍时，输入和输出形状对不上，直路上要加一个 1 × 1、步长 2 的卷积来对齐（第二阶段的这个卷积有 8,448 个参数）。One block in stage 1: its two convolution layers have 3 × 3 × 64 × 64 = 36,864 weights each, plus 64 × 2 = 128 for normalisation, so 36,992 parameters each; each layer does 36,864 multiply–adds at each of the 56 × 56 positions, about 116 million in all. The shortcut, which only adds, has no parameters and costs just 56 × 56 × 64 extra additions. Between stages, where the side length halves and the channels double, the input and output shapes no longer match, so a 1 × 1, stride-2 convolution goes on the shortcut to line them up (in stage 2 this convolution has 8,448 parameters).

        训练时 μ 和 s 按这一批来算，同时记下它们的滑动平均；推理时只用记下的这两个固定的数，所以归一化只是"乘一个数、加一个数"，可以直接并进前面那层卷积的权重里，不花额外的时间。后面的 Transformer 也用归一化和残差（第 ⑧ 步），只是归一化的方式不同：按每个词元自己的数来算，不按一批来算。During training, μ and s are computed from the current batch, while a running average of each is kept; at inference only these two stored, fixed numbers are used, so normalisation is just "multiply by a number, add a number", and it can be folded straight into the weights of the convolution layer before it, costing no extra time. The Transformer later on also uses normalisation and residuals (step ⑧), but normalises differently: from each token’s own numbers, not across a batch.

        多大、多少运算How big, how many operations

        按第 ④ 步的公式一层一层加起来（数据来自我们自己的计算，和 torchvision 公布的一致，torchvision）：Adding it up layer by layer with the formulas from step ④ (the figures are our own calculation and agree with those torchvision publishes, torchvision):

        阶段Stage输出形状Output shape参数Parameters乘加Multiply–adds一个数看到的范围What one number sees
        第一层 + 池化First layer + pooling56 × 56 × 649,536118.0 M11 像素11 pixels
        第一阶段Stage 156 × 56 × 64147,968462.4 M43 像素43 pixels
        第二阶段Stage 228 × 28 × 128525,568411.0 M99 像素99 pixels
        第三阶段Stage 314 × 14 × 2562,099,712411.0 M211 像素211 pixels
        第四阶段Stage 47 × 7 × 5128,393,728411.0 M435 像素435 pixels
        平均池化 + 全连接Average pooling + dense layer1000513,0000.5 M整张图Whole picture
        一共Total11,689,5121.81 G

        一共 11,689,512 个参数，FP32 约 46.8 MB，BF16 约 23.4 MB；一张 224 × 224 的图要 1.81 G 次（18.1 亿次）乘加，也就是约 3.63 GFLOP（36.3 亿次运算）。注意论文和 torchvision 写的"1.8 GFLOPs"数的是乘加，按本篇的算法要乘 2。In all: 11,689,512 parameters, about 46.8 MB in FP32 and about 23.4 MB in BF16; one 224 × 224 picture takes 1.81 G (1.81 billion) multiply–adds, which is about 3.63 GFLOP (3.63 billion operations). Note that the "1.8 GFLOPs" given in the paper and by torchvision counts multiply–adds; by this page’s way of counting, it has to be multiplied by 2.

        这张表里有两件事值得多看一眼。第一，参数堆在后面，运算摊得很平。第四阶段占了 72% 的参数，运算却只占 23%：它的核大（3 × 3 × 512），可图只剩 7 × 7，每个核只用 49 次；第一阶段反过来，核小，图大，每个核用 3136 次。每次边长减半（位置变成 1/4）、通道加倍（每个位置的乘加变成 4 倍），两头正好抵消，所以后面三个阶段的乘加都是 4.11 亿次。第二，参数和图的大小无关，运算和图的面积成正比。把头部相机的原图 1280 × 720 直接送进去（最后的平均池化不管图多大，都变成 512 个数），参数一个不多，乘加变成 33.5 G 次，是 224 × 224 时的 18.5 倍，正好差不多是面积之比。Two things in this table are worth a second look. First, the parameters pile up at the end, while the operations are spread evenly. Stage 4 holds 72% of the parameters but only 23% of the operations: its kernels are big (3 × 3 × 512), but the picture is down to 7 × 7, so each kernel is used only 49 times; stage 1 is the other way round: small kernels, a big picture, and each kernel used 3136 times. Each time the side length halves (the positions drop to 1/4) and the channels double (4 times the multiply–adds per position), the two cancel exactly, so the last three stages each come to 411 million multiply–adds. Second, the parameters do not depend on the size of the picture; the operations are proportional to its area. Feed the head camera’s full 1280 × 720 picture straight in (the final average pooling turns a picture of any size into 512 numbers), and there is not a single extra parameter, but the multiply–adds rise to 33.5 G, 18.5 times as many as at 224 × 224: almost exactly the ratio of the areas.

        最右一列是第 ④ 步说的感受野：第四阶段的一个数，理论上能看到 435 × 435 个像素的范围，比整张 224 × 224 的图还大，所以最后那几层看的是"整体是什么"，不再是"这一小块是什么"。ResNet-50 换了一种更深、更省的块（1 × 1、3 × 3、1 × 1 三层卷积），2560 万个参数，4.09 G 次乘加（torchvision）。The rightmost column is the receptive field from step ④: a number in stage 4 can, in theory, see an area of 435 × 435 pixels, bigger than the whole 224 × 224 picture, so the last few layers look at "what is the whole thing", no longer "what is this small patch". ResNet-50 switches to a deeper, more economical block (three convolution layers: 1 × 1, 3 × 3, 1 × 1), with 25.6 million parameters and 4.09 G multiply–adds (torchvision).

        • 上面一排方块是数据在网络里的样子：宽窄表示边长，厚薄表示通道数，方块上方的弧线是残差连接。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步：一张图一段一段地往右走，边长变小，通道变多，每一段写出形状。The row of boxes at the top is the data as it passes through the network: the width shows the side length, the thickness the number of channels, and the arcs above the boxes are residual connections. ▶ Play, Next step, Back: a picture moves right stage by stage, its side length shrinking and its channels growing, with each stage’s shape written out.
        • 下面两排条：每一层卷积（和最后的全连接层）一根，上排是参数，下排是运算（乘加）。The two rows of bars below: one bar for each convolution layer (and the final dense layer); the top row shows parameters, the bottom row operations (multiply–adds).
        • 输入大小：224 × 224、448 × 448、1280 × 720。点一个阶段：输入图上画出它的一个数能看到的范围，旁边写出它的形状、参数和运算。Input size: 224 × 224, 448 × 448, 1280 × 720. Tap a stage: the area that one of its numbers can see is drawn on the input picture, and its shape, parameters and operations are written beside it.

        先玩：先 ▶ 播放一遍，数一数图变小了几次。对比上下两排条：参数最多的是哪几层，运算最多的又是哪几层？把输入换成 1280 × 720：哪排条变了，哪排没变？点第四阶段：它的一个数能看到多大的范围？Try first: press ▶ Play once and count how many times the picture shrinks. Compare the two rows of bars: which layers have the most parameters, and which have the most operations? Switch the input to 1280 × 720: which row of bars changes, and which does not? Tap stage 4: how large an area can one of its numbers see?

        这一步的要点：CNN 一路把图变小、把通道变多，最后平均成一串数，用全连接层给出分数。残差连接（y = x + f(x)）和归一化让几十上百层的网络训练得动。ResNet-18 有 1169 万个参数（FP32 约 47 MB），一张 224 × 224 的图 3.63 GFLOP；参数堆在后面，运算摊得很平；换成大图，参数不变，运算和面积成正比。The point of this step: a CNN keeps shrinking the picture and adding channels, then averages everything into a list of numbers and uses a dense layer to give the scores. Residual connections (y = x + f(x)) and normalisation make networks of tens or even hundreds of layers trainable. ResNet-18 has 11.69 million parameters (about 47 MB in FP32) and takes 3.63 GFLOP per 224 × 224 picture; the parameters pile up at the end, while the operations are spread evenly; with a bigger picture, the parameters stay the same and the operations grow in proportion to the area.

        第 ⑥ 步 · 循环网络Step ⑥ · Recurrent networks

        带着记忆，一步一步读Reading one step at a time, with a memory

        到现在为止，网络看的都是"一个时刻"：一组线索，或者一张照片。可很多东西是一串：麦克风每秒 16000 个数，一句话一个词接一个词，力传感器每毫秒一个读数。机器人的唤醒词网络要从一串声音里听出"有人在叫我"，光看其中 10 毫秒什么也听不出来，而且这一串有多长，事先不知道。So far, every network has looked at "one moment": a set of clues, or a single photo. But many things come as a sequence: a microphone gives 16000 numbers a second, a sentence arrives one word after another, a force sensor gives a reading every millisecond. The robot’s wake-word network has to pick out "someone is calling me" from a stream of sound. From just 10 milliseconds of it you can’t make anything out, and how long the stream will be is not known in advance.

        办法是让网络带一个小本子：读一个新的输入，就把本子上的内容和新输入合起来，改写本子，再读下一个。这个本子叫隐藏状态，记作 h。每一步用的是同一套权重，所以不管这一串多长，网络都一样大。这就是循环网络（RNN）。先看一个最简单的：把手腕上的拉力做一个"滑动平均"，每 10 毫秒取一次读数，五次读数是 0、2、6、6、2 N。The trick is to give the network a little notebook: each time it reads a new input, it combines what is in the notebook with the new input, rewrites the notebook, and then reads the next one. The notebook is called the hidden state, written h. Every step uses the same set of weights, so the network is the same size however long the sequence is. This is a recurrent network (RNN). Start with the simplest one: a "running average" of the pull force at the wrist, with a reading every 10 milliseconds; the five readings are 0, 2, 6, 6, 2 N.

        ht = a ht−1 + (1 − a) xt

        这一刻的记忆，等于上一刻的记忆留下 a 那么多，再加上这一刻的新输入的 1 − a 那么多。This moment’s memory keeps a fraction a of the memory from the moment before and adds a fraction 1 − a of this moment’s new input.

        a 是"记住多少"：a 接近 1，记忆变化很慢，一下的抖动影响不大；a 接近 0，几乎只看眼前。这一步又是"乘、加"：两个输入（旧记忆和新读数）、两个权重（a 和 1 − a），只是其中一个输入是自己上一步的输出，这就是"循环"二字的意思。Think of a as "how much to keep": with a close to 1, the memory changes slowly and a single jolt has little effect; with a close to 0, it looks at almost nothing but the present. This step is once again "multiply, add": two inputs (the old memory and the new reading) and two weights (a and 1 − a), except that one of the inputs is the network’s own output from the step before. That is what the word "recurrent" means.

        xt
        第 t 步的输入（这里是第 t 次的拉力读数，N）Input at step t (here the t-th pull reading, in N)
        ht, ht−1
        第 t 步和上一步的隐藏状态；开始之前 h 是 0Hidden state at step t and at the step before; before the start, h is 0
        a
        记住多少，0 到 1 之间How much to keep, between 0 and 1

        a = 0.5：h1 = 0.5 × 0 + 0.5 × 0 = 0；h2 = 0 + 0.5 × 2 = 1；h3 = 0.5 × 1 + 0.5 × 6 = 3.5；h4 = 1.75 + 3 = 4.75；h5 = 2.375 + 1 = 3.375。最后那次读数掉回了 2 N，记忆还停在 3.4 左右：它记得刚才拉得很用力。a = 0.5: h1 = 0.5 × 0 + 0.5 × 0 = 0; h2 = 0 + 0.5 × 2 = 1; h3 = 0.5 × 1 + 0.5 × 6 = 3.5; h4 = 1.75 + 3 = 4.75; h5 = 2.375 + 1 = 3.375. The last reading has dropped back to 2 N, but the memory is still at about 3.4: it remembers the hard pull just before.

        一个输入在记忆里每过一步就乘一次 a，过 t 步还剩 at。a = 0.5 时，过 1 步剩一半，2 步剩 1/4，5 步剩 3%，10 步剩千分之一，20 步只剩百万分之一。a = 0.9 时，10 步后还剩 35%，可 50 步后也只剩 0.5%。普通的循环网络就是这样健忘：很难记住几十步以前的事，训练时梯度往回传也一样一步步衰减。With every step that passes, an input held in the memory is multiplied by a once more, so after t steps at of it is left. With a = 0.5, half is left after 1 step, 1/4 after 2, 3% after 5, a thousandth after 10, and only a millionth after 20. With a = 0.9, 35% is still left after 10 steps, but after 50 steps only 0.5% remains. That is how forgetful a plain recurrent network is: it can hardly remember anything from a few dozen steps back, and during training the gradient flowing backwards fades step by step in just the same way.

        真实的循环层，和门A real recurrent layer, and gates

        真实的循环层里，输入和隐藏状态都是一串数，a 和 1 − a 换成了两张权重表，再加偏置、弯一下：In a real recurrent layer, the input and the hidden state are both lists of numbers, a and 1 − a turn into two tables of weights, and then we add a bias and bend it:

        ht = σ(Wx xt + Wh ht−1 + b)P = nh (nin + nh + 1)

        新的隐藏状态，是"新输入乘一张表"加上"旧状态乘另一张表"，加偏置，再弯一下。参数是两张表加偏置。For the new hidden state, take "the new input times one table", add "the old state times another table" and a bias, then bend it. The parameters are the two tables plus the bias.

        这就是一个全连接层，只是它的输入是"新输入和旧状态拼在一起"，nin + nh 个数，输出 nh 个数，每一步用一次。参数公式就是第 ② 步的 nin × nout + nout。This is just a dense layer, except that its input is "the new input and the old state joined together", nin + nh numbers, and its output is nh numbers; it runs once per step. The parameter formula is the one from step ②, nin × nout + nout.

        Wx, Wh
        输入到状态的权重（nh × nin）；状态到状态的权重（nh × nh）Input-to-state weights (nh × nin); state-to-state weights (nh × nh)
        nin, nh
        每一步输入几个数；隐藏状态有几个数How many numbers go in at each step; how many numbers the hidden state holds

        听声音的网络常常每 10 毫秒吃进 80 个数（声音在 80 个频带上的强弱，第 ⑩ 步细讲），nin = 80；隐藏状态取 nh = 512。参数 512 × (80 + 512 + 1) = 303,616 个，每一步 303,104 次乘加，一秒 100 步，约 6060 万次运算，写作约 61 MFLOP/s。Networks that listen to sound often take in 80 numbers every 10 milliseconds (the strength of the sound in each of 80 frequency bands; step ⑩ covers this in detail), so nin = 80; take a hidden state of nh = 512. That gives 512 × (80 + 512 + 1) = 303,616 parameters and 303,104 multiply–adds per step; at 100 steps a second, that is about 60.6 million operations, written as about 61 MFLOP/s.

        为了不那么健忘，人们给它加了门：几层 S 形神经元（每层 nh 个，每个隐藏数配一个），每一步看着新输入和旧状态，现算出"这一步记住多少、忘掉多少、写进多少"，相当于把上面那个固定的 a 换成每一步现算的、每个数各自的 a。GRU（Cho 等 2014）有两个门，加上算新内容的那一组，一共三组和上面一样大的权重；LSTM（Hochreiter 和 Schmidhuber 1997；今天用的是 Gers 等 2000 年加上"忘记门"的版本）有三个门，一共四组。所以同样 80 → 512，GRU 有 910,848 个参数，LSTM 有 1,214,464 个，正好是 3 倍和 4 倍（这是每组一个偏置的算法；PyTorch 每组存两份偏置，数出来是 912,384 和 1,216,512，倍数不变）。有了门，一个重要的输入可以被"锁"在记忆里几百步。To make it less forgetful, people added gates: a few layers of sigmoid neurons (nh in each layer, one for each hidden number) that look at the new input and the old state at every step and work out on the spot "how much to keep, how much to forget and how much to write in at this step". In effect, the fixed a above becomes an a worked out afresh at every step, one for each number. The GRU (Cho et al. 2014) has two gates; together with the group that computes the new content, that makes three groups of weights, each as large as the one above. The LSTM (Hochreiter and Schmidhuber 1997; the version used today is the one from Gers et al. 2000, which added a "forget gate") has three gates, so four groups in all. So for the same 80 → 512, a GRU has 910,848 parameters and an LSTM 1,214,464: exactly 3 and 4 times as many (this counts one bias per group; PyTorch stores two biases per group, which gives 912,384 and 1,216,512, with the same ratios). With gates, an important input can be "locked" into the memory for hundreds of steps.

        唤醒词：一个只有 79 kB 的网络Wake word: a network of only 79 kB

        机器人一直开着的那个唤醒词网络，要小到可以在一块单片机上跑，耗电极少。Hello Edge 这篇论文（Zhang 等 2017）比较过在单片机上听关键词的网络：每段 1 秒的声音，每隔 20 或 40 毫秒算一组声音特征（隔多久也是搜出来的）；限定内存约 80 kB、每次判断约 600 万次运算以内时，最好的 GRU（隐藏状态 154 个数，每 40 毫秒一步，一共 25 步）用 78.8 kB（权重和中间结果都按 8 比特算）、每次判断 380 万次运算（论文假设每秒判断 10 次），能认对 93.5%；同样大小的 LSTM 是 92.9%。公平地说，同一张表里还有一个卷积网络（DS-CNN），38.6 kB、540 万次运算，认对 94.4%：声音也可以当成一张"频率 × 时间"的图，用卷积来看。The wake-word network that the robot keeps running all the time has to be small enough to run on a microcontroller and use very little power. The Hello Edge paper (Zhang et al. 2017) compared networks that listen for keywords on microcontrollers. Each clip is 1 second of sound, with a set of sound features computed every 20 or 40 milliseconds (the interval, too, was found by search). With memory capped at about 80 kB and at most about 6 million operations per decision, the best GRU (a hidden state of 154 numbers, one step every 40 milliseconds, 25 steps in all) uses 78.8 kB (weights and intermediate results all counted at 8 bits) and 3.8 million operations per decision (the paper assumes 10 decisions a second), and gets 93.5% right; an LSTM of the same size gets 92.9%. To be fair, the same table also has a convolutional network (DS-CNN): 38.6 kB, 5.4 million operations, 94.4% right: sound can also be treated as a "frequency × time" picture and looked at with convolutions.

        循环网络的好处是：不管听多久，它要记的只有那 nh 个数，每一步的运算也一样多。坏处是：第 t 步必须等第 t − 1 步算完，一串很长的输入只能一步一步地算，训练时没法把一整段同时铺开交给 GPU 的成千上万个运算单元。下一步的注意力正好反过来。近几年的 Mamba 这类"状态空间模型"（Gu 和 Dao 2023）把两边的好处拼在一起：像循环网络一样只带一个固定大小的状态、运算随长度线性增长，门由输入现算，又能在训练时并行。The upside of a recurrent network: however long it listens, all it has to remember is those nh numbers, and every step takes the same number of operations. The downside: step t has to wait until step t − 1 is done, so a long string of inputs can only be computed one step at a time, and in training you cannot lay a whole stretch out at once and hand it to the thousands of compute units in a GPU. Attention, in the next step, is exactly the other way round. In recent years, "state-space models" such as Mamba (Gu and Dao 2023) have put the strengths of both together: like a recurrent network, they carry only a fixed-size state and their operations grow linearly with length, with gates worked out from the input, and yet they can be trained in parallel.

        • 上面是五个时刻摊开来画：下面一排是拉力读数 x，上面一排是记忆 h，每一格写着 × a 和 × (1 − a)：每一步用的是同一份权重。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：一个时刻一个时刻地往右算，下面写出这一步的算式；点一列直接跳过去。一排小条显示每个读数在现在的记忆里还剩多少。The picture at the top shows five moments unrolled side by side: the bottom row is the pull readings x, the top row is the memory h, and every cell is marked × a and × (1 − a): the same weights at every step. ▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: compute one moment at a time, moving right, with the arithmetic for that step written out below; tap a column to jump straight to it. A row of small bars shows how much of each reading is still left in the memory now.
        • 拖 记住多少 a（0 到 0.99），下面一行是第一个输入过 t 步还剩多少。加一个门：a 不再固定，每一步由一个小神经元现算（我们取 a = σ(−2x + 8)）：输入小时几乎全记住，输入大时换成新的。Drag How much to keep a (0 to 0.99); the row below shows how much of the first input is left after t steps. Add a gate: a is no longer fixed but is worked out at every step by a small neuron (we take a = σ(−2x + 8)): when the input is small, almost everything is kept; when it is large, the new reading takes over.
        • 真实大小：普通循环、GRU、LSTM 三个按钮，拖 nin 和 nh，选精度，看参数、字节、每一步的乘加和每秒 100 步的运算；下面一行是 Hello Edge 的唤醒词网络。Real size: three buttons, Plain RNN, GRU and LSTM; drag nin and nh, choose a precision, and see the parameters, the bytes, the multiply–adds per step and the operations at 100 steps a second; the line below is Hello Edge’s wake-word network.
        • 和注意力比：拖序列长度 T，看循环网络要一步接一步算几步、要记几个数，注意力的打分表有多大。Versus attention: drag the sequence length T to see how many steps the recurrent network has to compute one after another and how many numbers it has to remember, and how big attention’s score table is.

        先玩：a = 0.5 时，第一个输入过 10 步还剩多少？拖到 0.9 再看。打开"加一个门"：那次 6 N 的大拉力，能不能熬过后面安静的那一步？状态栏还算了：再安静 20 步，它还剩多少？在真实大小里，GRU 的参数为什么正好是普通循环的 3 倍？把 T 拖到 10 万：循环网络要串着算多少步，注意力的打分表有多少个数？Try first: With a = 0.5, how much of the first input is left after 10 steps? Drag it to 0.9 and look again. Turn on "Add a gate": does that big 6 N pull survive the quiet step that follows? The status line also works out how much of it is left after 20 more quiet steps. In Real size, why does the GRU have exactly 3 times as many parameters as the Plain RNN? Drag T to 100,000: how many steps does the recurrent network have to compute in a row, and how many numbers are in attention’s score table?

        这一步的要点：循环网络用一个固定大小的隐藏状态，带着记忆一步一步读一串输入，每一步用同一套权重：参数 nh(nin + nh + 1)，GRU 是它的 3 倍，LSTM 是 4 倍。它省内存，但健忘（门能缓解），而且只能一步一步地算。The point of this step: A recurrent network reads a sequence of inputs one step at a time, carrying its memory in a fixed-size hidden state and using the same weights at every step: nh(nin + nh + 1) parameters, 3 times that for a GRU and 4 times for an LSTM. It needs little memory, but it is forgetful (gates help), and it can only compute one step at a time.

        第 ⑦ 步 · 注意力Step ⑦ · Attention

        按内容现算：这个词该看谁Worked out on the spot from the content: who this word should look at

        "把那杯水递给我"。读到"递"的时候，要弄明白递什么、递给谁：答案在"那杯水"和"给我"，一个在它前面，一个在它后面。循环网络要靠那个小本子把"那杯水"一路带过来，中间隔得越远越容易忘。注意力换了个办法：每个词直接去看句子里所有的词，按内容现算"该看谁、看多少"，再把看到的东西按这个比例混合起来（Vaswani 等 2017）。"Pass me that glass of water." On reaching "pass", the network has to work out what to pass and to whom: the answers are in "that glass of water" and "to me", which in the Chinese sentence come one before it and one after it. A recurrent network has to carry "that glass of water" all the way along in its little notebook, and the further apart the two are, the more easily it forgets. Attention does it differently: each word looks directly at every word in the sentence, works out on the spot, from their content, "who to look at, and how much", and then mixes what it sees in those proportions (Vaswani et al. 2017).

        手算一个小例子。句子切成 4 个词元：把、那杯水、递、给我。每个词元先查表变成 4 个数（第 ⑧ 步讲这张表），这里故意取得很简单，4 个数分别表示"是东西""是人""是动作""是虚词"，每个词元只有一个是 1：那杯水 = (1, 0, 0, 0)，给我 = (0, 1, 0, 0)，递 = (0, 0, 1, 0)，把 = (0, 0, 0, 1)。然后每个词元算出三样东西：Let’s work a small example by hand. The sentence is cut into 4 tokens: "ba", "that glass of water", "pass", "to me" ("ba" is a grammar word in the Chinese sentence that marks the thing being handed over). An embedding lookup first turns each token into 4 numbers (step ⑧ covers this table). Here they are made deliberately simple: the 4 numbers stand for "is a thing", "is a person", "is an action" and "is a grammar word", and each token has a 1 in only one of them: "that glass of water" = (1, 0, 0, 0), "to me" = (0, 1, 0, 0), "pass" = (0, 0, 1, 0), "ba" = (0, 0, 0, 1). Then each token computes three things:

        • 查询 Q：我在找什么。"递"的查询是 (2, 2, 0, 0)：找东西，也找人。Query Q: what I am looking for. The query of "pass" is (2, 2, 0, 0): it looks for a thing, and also for a person.
        • 键 K：我是什么，给别人的查询来比对。这里键就是词元本身的 4 个数。Key K: what I am, for other tokens’ queries to compare against. Here the key is simply the token’s own 4 numbers.
        • 值 V：被看中以后，我交出去的内容。这里也是词元本身。Value V: the content I hand over once I have been picked. Here it is also the token itself.
        Q = X WQ K = X WK V = X WVS = Q KT / √dAij = eSij / Σm eSimY = A V

        每个词元的查询、键、值，都是它的 d 个数乘上一张权重表。第 i 个词元给第 j 个词元打的分，是 i 的查询和 j 的键对应相乘再相加，除以 √d。每一行的分数用 softmax 变成加起来等于 1 的权重。第 i 个词元的输出，是所有词元的值按这一行的权重加起来。Each token’s query, key and value are its d numbers multiplied by a weight table. The score that token i gives token j: multiply i’s query and j’s key number by number, add up, and divide by √d. Softmax turns each row of scores into weights that add up to 1. The output of token i is the values of all the tokens, added up with the weights in its row.

        "对应相乘再相加"（点积）量的是两串数有多像：查询和键在同一些位置上都大，分数就高。softmax 和第 ③ 步一样，把分数变成比例。除以 √d，是因为 d 个乘积加在一起，d 越大，点积的起伏越大：如果每个数都是平均 0、方差 1 的随机数，点积的标准差正好是 √d。除以 √d 把起伏拉回 1 左右；不除的话 softmax 会几乎只挑一个、别的全是 0，梯度几乎为零，训练不动。三张权重表 WQ、WK、WV 是要学的；"谁看谁"不是学出来的固定连线，而是每来一句话，按内容现算出来的。"Multiply number by number and add up" (the dot product) measures how alike two lists of numbers are: when the query and the key are both large in the same places, the score is high. Softmax, as in step ③, turns the scores into proportions. We divide by √d because the dot product adds up d products, so the bigger d is, the more the dot product swings: if every number is random with mean 0 and variance 1, the standard deviation of the dot product is exactly √d. Dividing by √d brings the swings back to about 1; without it, softmax would pick almost only one and give all the rest 0, the gradients would be almost zero, and training would stall. The three weight tables WQ, WK, WV are learned; "who looks at whom" is not fixed wiring learned in training, but is worked out on the spot from the content of each new sentence.

        X
        T 个词元的数，排成 T 行、d 列的表the numbers of the T tokens, laid out as a table of T rows and d columns
        WQ, WK, WV
        三张 d × d 的权重表three d × d weight tables
        Q, K, V
        查询、键、值，各是 T 行 d 列；KT 是 K 转过来（行变列）queries, keys and values, each T rows by d columns; KT is K transposed (rows become columns)
        S, A
        打分表和注意力权重表，都是 T × T：第 i 行是第 i 个词元看各个词元的分数和比例the score table and the table of attention weights, both T × T: row i holds the scores and the proportions with which token i looks at each token
        Y
        输出，T 行 d 列：每个词元混合了它看到的内容the output, T rows by d columns: each token has mixed in what it looked at
        T, d
        词元个数；每个词元用几个数表示number of tokens; how many numbers represent each token

        "递"的查询 (2, 2, 0, 0) 和四个键的点积：把 0，那杯水 2，递 0，给我 2；除以 √4 = 2，得 (0, 1, 0, 1)。e0 = 1，e1 ≈ 2.72，加起来 7.44，权重是 (0.134, 0.366, 0.134, 0.366)："递"把三分之一多的注意力给了"那杯水"，三分之一多给了"给我"。输出 = 0.134 × 把的值 + 0.366 × 那杯水的值 + 0.134 × 递的值 + 0.366 × 给我的值 = (0.366, 0.366, 0.134, 0.134)：原本只有"动作"的"递"，现在主要装着"东西"和"人"：递的是什么、递给谁；它自己的"动作"只剩 0.134，第 ⑧ 步会把原来的数加回去。四个词元一共 320 次乘加。The dot products of the query of "pass", (2, 2, 0, 0), with the four keys: "ba" 0, "that glass of water" 2, "pass" 0, "to me" 2; dividing by √4 = 2 gives (0, 1, 0, 1). e0 = 1 and e1 ≈ 2.72; all four add up to 7.44, so the weights are (0.134, 0.366, 0.134, 0.366): "pass" gives just over a third of its attention to "that glass of water" and just over a third to "to me". Output = 0.134 × the value of "ba" + 0.366 × the value of "that glass of water" + 0.134 × the value of "pass" + 0.366 × the value of "to me" = (0.366, 0.366, 0.134, 0.134): "pass", which held only "action", now mostly holds "thing" and "person", that is, what is being passed and to whom; its own "action" is down to 0.134, and step ⑧ adds the original numbers back. The four tokens take 320 multiply–adds in all.

        注意力里只有那三张表是参数，T × T 的打分表不是参数，是每句话现算的中间结果。所以注意力的参数和句子多长无关，运算和内存却随长度变：句子长到 2 倍，打分表变成 4 倍。In attention only those three tables are parameters. The T × T score table is not a parameter; it is an intermediate result, worked out afresh for every sentence. So attention’s parameters do not depend on how long the sentence is, but its operations and memory do: make the sentence 2 times as long and the score table becomes 4 times as big.

        只看前面、几个头、词序Only looking back, several heads, word order

        只看前面。一个一个往外写字的模型（第 ⑧ 步的大语言模型），写第 t 个词元时后面的还不存在。可训练时整句话是一起算的，后面的都在；为了和写字时一样，算注意力时把"看后面"的分数设成负无穷，e 的负无穷次方是 0。这叫掩码。"递"看不到"给我"了，剩下三个分数 (0, 1, 0)，权重变成 (0.212, 0.576, 0.212, 0)。Only looking back. A model that writes out its text one token at a time (the large language model of step ⑧) writes token t when the tokens after it do not exist yet. In training, though, the whole sentence is computed at once and the later tokens are all there; to match what happens when writing, the scores for "looking ahead" are set to minus infinity when attention is computed, and e to the power of minus infinity is 0. This is called a mask. Now "pass" can no longer see "to me": the three remaining scores are (0, 1, 0), and the weights become (0.212, 0.576, 0.212, 0).

        几个头。一句话里同时有好几种关系：动作找对象，代词找它指的东西，形容词找名词。只用一组 Q、K、V，一行只能有一种比例。多头注意力让几个头并排算：每个头都读进全部 d 个数，各有自己的 WQ、WK、WV，但只算出 d / 头数 个数的查询、键、值，各算各的打分表（除以的是每头宽度的平方根）；最后把各头的输出拼回 d 个数，再乘一张 d × d 的输出表 WO。分成几个头不增加运算，只是把一张大表拆成几张小表。Llama 3.1 8B 有 32 个查询头，每头 128 个数（它的键和值是 4 个头共用一组，第 ⑧ 步讲）。Several heads. A sentence holds several kinds of relation at once: an action looks for its object, a pronoun looks for the thing it refers to, an adjective looks for its noun. With only one set of Q, K, V, each row can hold only one set of proportions. Multi-head attention runs several heads side by side: each head reads in all d numbers and has its own WQ, WK, WV, but computes queries, keys and values of only d / (number of heads) numbers, and each head works out its own score table (dividing by the square root of the per-head width). At the end the heads’ outputs are joined back into d numbers and multiplied by a d × d output table WO. Splitting into heads adds no operations; it just breaks one big table into several small ones. Llama 3.1 8B has 32 query heads of 128 numbers each (its keys and values are shared, one set per 4 heads; step ⑧ covers this).

        词序。上面的算法（不带掩码时）里没有"第几个"：把句子顺序打乱，每个词元得到的输出一模一样，只是排列跟着变。可"我递给你"和"你递给我"意思相反。所以要把位置也放进去：最早的 Transformer 给每个位置加一串由正弦和余弦算出来的数；Llama 用的是 RoPE（Su 等 2021，Llama 3 沿用）：把查询和键的 d 个数两两配成一对，每一对按"位置 × 这一对的转速"转一个角度，各对的转速从快到很慢；这样两个词元的分数里，位置的影响就只取决于它们隔多远。Word order. The calculation above (without the mask) has no notion of "which position": shuffle the sentence and every token gets exactly the same output, only in the shuffled order. Yet "I pass it to you" and "you pass it to me" mean opposite things. So position has to go in as well: the original Transformer adds to each position a list of numbers computed from sines and cosines; Llama uses RoPE (Su et al. 2021, still used in Llama 3): the d numbers of the query and of the key are paired up two by two, and each pair is rotated by an angle of "position × this pair’s rotation speed", with the speeds of the pairs ranging from fast to very slow. That way, in the score between two tokens, the effect of position depends only on how far apart they are.

        注意力有多贵How expensive attention is

        F ≈ 8 T d2 + 4 T2 d

        一层注意力的运算，一部分和词元个数成正比、和宽度的平方成正比（四张权重表），一部分和词元个数的平方成正比（打分和混合）。The operations in one attention layer come in two parts: one is proportional to the number of tokens and to the square of the width (the four weight tables), the other to the square of the number of tokens (scoring and mixing).

        Q、K、V 和输出 WO 四次乘表，每次 T 个词元、每个 d × d 次乘加：4 × 2 × T × d2。打分表 T × T 个格子，每格一次 d 个数的点积：2 × T2 × d；按权重混合值也是这么多。T 小于 2d 时，大头在乘表；T 大于 2d 时，大头在打分。Q, K, V and the output WO are four multiplications by a table, each for T tokens at d × d multiply–adds per token: 4 × 2 × T × d2. The score table has T × T cells, each one a dot product of d numbers: 2 × T2 × d; mixing the values by the weights takes as much again. When T is less than 2d, the table multiplications are the bigger part; when T is greater than 2d, the scoring is.

        F
        一层注意力算 T 个词元的运算（标准多头注意力）operations for one attention layer on T tokens (standard multi-head attention)

        d = 4096 时，2d = 8192。一段 2000 个词元的对话，乘表约 2.7 × 1011 次，打分和混合约 6.6 × 1010 次；到 13 万个词元（Llama 3.1 支持的最长上下文），打分和混合约 2.8 × 1014 次，是乘表的 16 倍。With d = 4096, 2d = 8192. For a conversation of 2000 tokens, the table multiplications take about 2.7 × 1011 operations and scoring and mixing about 6.6 × 1010; at 130,000 tokens (the longest context Llama 3.1 supports), scoring and mixing take about 2.8 × 1014, 16 times as much as the table multiplications.

        "T 的平方"是注意力最出名的代价，也是长上下文贵的原因之一；另一个原因是每个词元的键和值要存起来给后面的词元看，这就是第 13 篇讲的键值缓存。和循环网络比：循环网络串着算 T 步、只记 nh 个数；注意力一次就能把 T 个词元全部并行算完，代价是 T2。"T squared" is attention’s best-known cost, and one of the reasons long contexts are expensive; the other is that every token’s keys and values have to be stored for later tokens to look at, which is the KV cache covered on page 13. Compared with a recurrent network: a recurrent network works through T steps one after another and keeps only nh numbers; attention can compute all T tokens in parallel in one go, at a cost of T2.

        • 上面是四个词元；下面的表里，每个词元占一行（X、Q、K、V、打分表 S、权重表 A、输出 Y 都是这样），每个数都写出来。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来，或者用 阶段 那排按钮直接跳：① 查表、② 算 Q、K、V、③ 打分（四个键一个一个比）、④ softmax、⑤ 加权求和。The four tokens are at the top; in the tables below, each token takes one row (in X, Q, K, V, the score table S, the weight table A and the output Y alike), with every number written out. Use ▶ Play, Next step, Back and Restart, or jump straight to a stage with the row of Stage buttons: ① Look up, ② Q, K, V, ③ Scores (the four keys compared one at a time), ④ Softmax, ⑤ Weighted sum.
        • 查询：点四个词元之一（默认"递"）。它那一行在所有表里框出来，下面一步步写出分数、e 的次方、权重和输出；从它连到其他词元的弧线，粗细表示权重。Query: tap one of the four tokens (the default is "pass"). Its row is outlined in every table, and below, its scores, powers of e, weights and output are written out step by step; the arcs from it to the other tokens show the weights by their thickness.
        • 两个开关：只看前面（掩码）；两个头：第二个头有自己的 WQ，专门找"给谁"，两张表并排。Two switches: Causal mask (only looking back); Two heads: the second head has its own WQ, which looks specifically for "to whom", and the two tables sit side by side.
        • 变长以后：三个预设，玩具：4 个词元、Llama 3.1 8B · 2048 个词元、Llama 3.1 8B · 131 072 个词元；也可以拖词元数 T（4 到 131,072）和宽度 d（4 到 4096），看乘表和打分各要多少运算、打分表有多少个格子、哪一部分占大头。这里的乘表算上了输出表 WO。As it grows: three presets, Toy: 4 tokens, Llama 3.1 8B · 2,048 tokens and Llama 3.1 8B · 131,072 tokens; you can also drag the number of tokens T (4 to 131,072) and the width d (4 to 4096) to see how many operations the table multiplications and the scoring each take, how many cells the score table has, and which part is the bigger one. The table multiplications here include the output table WO.

        先玩：依次点四个词元：每个词元最看重谁？为什么"把"去看"那杯水"？打开"只看前面"：哪几行变了，哪一行没变？打开"两个头"：在第二个头里，"递"最看重谁？在"变长以后"里先选 Llama 3.1 8B · 2048 个词元，再换成 131 072 个词元：打分那一部分涨了多少倍，乘表那一部分呢？Try first: Tap the four tokens in turn: which token does each one pay most attention to? Why does "ba" look at "that glass of water"? Turn on "Causal mask": which rows change, and which row stays the same? Turn on "Two heads": in the second head, which token does "pass" pay most attention to? In "As it grows", first choose Llama 3.1 8B · 2,048 tokens, then switch to 131,072 tokens: how many times bigger does the scoring part get, and the table-multiplication part?

        这一步的要点：注意力让每个词元用查询去比对所有词元的键，softmax 成比例，再按比例混合它们的值；"看谁"是按内容现算的。参数只有几张 d × d 的表，和句子长度无关；运算约 8Td² + 4T²d，句子一长，T² 那一项就占了上风。掩码让它只看前面，多头让它同时看几种关系，位置编码告诉它词序。The point of this step: attention lets each token compare its query with the keys of all the tokens, turn the scores into proportions with softmax, and mix their values in those proportions; "who to look at" is worked out on the spot from the content. The only parameters are a few d × d tables, independent of sentence length; the operations come to about 8Td² + 4T²d, and once the sentence gets long, the T² term takes over. The mask makes it look only back, multiple heads let it follow several kinds of relation at once, and positional encoding tells it the word order.

        第 ⑧ 步 · Transformer 和大语言模型Step ⑧ · Transformers and large language models

        注意力加 MLP，叠 32 层：一个 80 亿参数的模型里面Attention plus an MLP, stacked 32 layers deep: inside an 8-billion-parameter model

        机器人听懂"把那杯水递给我"以后，要想出步骤：先找到杯子，抓起来，转身，递到人手边，等对方接住再松手。做这件事的是一个大语言模型，比如 Llama 3.1 8B（Llama 3）。它从头到尾只做一件事：读进一串词元，给下一个词元打分。写出一个，接到末尾，再写下一个（前面词元的键和值都存着，新词元只要自己走一遍）。拆开看，一个词元在里面走的路是这样的：Once the robot has understood "Pass me that glass of water", it has to work out the steps: find the glass, pick it up, turn round, hold it out to the person’s hand, and let go once they have taken it. The job is done by a large language model, such as Llama 3.1 8B (Llama 3). From start to finish it does just one thing: read in a sequence of tokens and give scores for the next token. It writes one, appends it to the end and writes the next (the keys and values of the earlier tokens are all kept, so the new token only has to go through once on its own). Taken apart, this is the path one token follows inside it:

        • 切词：文字先被切成词元，每个词元是词表里的一个编号。Llama 3 的词表有 128,256 个词元，常用的字和词各占一个，少见的拆成几块。Tokenising: the text is first cut into tokens, and each token is an id, its number in the vocabulary. Llama 3’s vocabulary has 128,256 tokens; common characters and words get one each, and rare ones are split into several pieces.
        • 查表：一张 128,256 行、4096 列的表，编号是几就取第几行，得到这个词元的 4096 个数。这一步没有乘法，只是取出来。Embedding lookup: a table with 128,256 rows and 4096 columns; the id says which row to take, and that row is the token’s 4096 numbers. This step has no multiplications; it only reads a row out.
        • 32 个块：每个块是"归一化 → 注意力 → 加回去 → 归一化 → MLP → 加回去"。注意力让这个词元去看前面的词元（第 ⑦ 步，带掩码）；MLP 对每个词元自己的 4096 个数再做一次变换（第 ③ 步）；"加回去"是第 ⑤ 步的残差连接。32 blocks: each block is "normalise → attention → add back → normalise → MLP → add back". Attention lets the token look at the tokens before it (step ⑦, with the mask); the MLP transforms each token’s own 4096 numbers once more (step ③); "add back" is the residual connection of step ⑤.
        • 输出：最后一个块出来的 4096 个数，再归一化一次，乘一张 4096 × 128,256 的表，得到 128,256 个分数，softmax 成概率，挑出下一个词元。Output: the 4096 numbers coming out of the last block are normalised once more and multiplied by a 4096 × 128,256 table, giving 128,256 scores; softmax turns them into probabilities, and the next token is picked.
        u = x + fattn(N(x))y = u + fmlp(N(u))fmlp(v) = Wdown (φ(Wgate v) ⊙ Wup v)

        一个块：先把输入归一化，做一次注意力，结果加回输入；再归一化，过一次 MLP，结果再加回去。Llama 的 MLP 有三张表：一张算"门"并弯一下，一张算内容，两者对应相乘，再用第三张表变回 d 个数。One block: normalise the input, apply attention once and add the result back to the input; normalise again, pass through the MLP and add the result back again. Llama’s MLP has three tables: one computes a "gate" and bends it, one computes the content, the two are multiplied number by number, and the third table turns the result back into d numbers.

        注意力和 MLP 分工不同：注意力是词元之间交换信息（"递"去看"那杯水"），MLP 是每个词元自己消化（它只看这一个词元的 d 个数，对每个词元用同一套权重）。两样交替叠起来，信息先在词元之间流动，再在每个词元里加工，一层层往上。两处都把结果"加回去"，所以每个块只是在原来那 d 个数上做修改，叠很多层也训练得动。MLP 先把 d 个数放大到 dff 个，在更宽的地方弯一下，再收回 d 个数；多一张"门"表，是让一部分数决定另一部分数通过多少，实践中效果更好。Attention and the MLP do different jobs: attention exchanges information between tokens ("pass" looks at "that glass of water"), while the MLP is each token digesting on its own (it sees only that one token’s d numbers, and uses the same weights for every token). With the two stacked in alternation, information first flows between tokens and is then processed within each token, layer by layer up the stack. Both steps "add back" their result, so each block only modifies the d numbers that are already there, and even a stack of many layers can still be trained. The MLP first widens the d numbers to dff, applies the bend in that wider space, then brings them back to d; the extra "gate" table lets some numbers decide how much of the others gets through, which works better in practice.

        x, u, y
        进块时、注意力之后、出块时的值，每个词元都是 d 个数the values entering the block, after attention, and leaving the block; d numbers per token
        N
        归一化（Llama 用 RMSNorm：每个词元的 d 个数除以它们的均方根，再乘一组可学的数）normalisation (Llama uses RMSNorm: each token’s d numbers are divided by their root mean square, then multiplied by a set of learnable numbers)
        fattn, fmlp
        注意力（第 ⑦ 步）和 MLPattention (step ⑦) and the MLP
        Wgate, Wup, Wdown
        MLP 的三张表：d → dff，d → dff，dff → dthe MLP’s three tables: d → dff, d → dff, dff → d
        v
        MLP 的输入：一个词元归一化之后的 d 个数（这里就是 N(u)）the MLP’s input: one token’s d numbers after normalisation (here, N(u))
        φ, ⊙
        弯一下（Llama 用的是 SiLU，一种圆滑的 ReLU）；对应位置相乘the bend (Llama uses SiLU, a smooth ReLU); multiplication position by position

        Llama 3.1 8B：d = 4096，dff = 14,336，32 个块。一个块的注意力有 4 张表：WQ 和 WO 各 4096 × 4096，WK 和 WV 各 4096 × 1024（下面讲为什么小），一共 41,943,040 个参数；MLP 三张 4096 × 14,336，一共 176,160,768 个；两个归一化 8,192 个。一个块 218,112,000 个参数，约 2.2 亿。Llama 3.1 8B: d = 4096, dff = 14,336, 32 blocks. The attention in one block has 4 tables: WQ and WO are 4096 × 4096 each, WK and WV are 4096 × 1024 each (why they are smaller is explained below), 41,943,040 parameters in all; the MLP has three 4096 × 14,336 tables, 176,160,768 in all; the two normalisations have 8,192. One block has 218,112,000 parameters, about 220 million.

        "块"是 Transformer 的基本单位，后面的看图（第 ⑨ 步）、听声音（第 ⑩ 步）、动作模型（第 ⑬ 步）用的都是这样的块，差别主要在词元从哪来、要不要掩码、叠几层多宽；细节上也有小差别：MLP 带不带门、用哪种归一化、位置怎么告诉它，Whisper 的解码器每块还多一层交叉注意力。The "block" is the basic unit of the Transformer. Seeing images (step ⑨), hearing sound (step ⑩) and the action model (step ⑬) later on all use blocks like this; the main differences are where the tokens come from, whether there is a mask, and how many layers are stacked and how wide. There are small differences in the details too: whether the MLP has a gate, which normalisation is used, how the model is told about position, and Whisper’s decoder has an extra cross-attention layer in each block.

        80 亿个参数在哪，算一个词元多少运算Where the 8 billion parameters are, and how many operations one token takes

        P ≈ L (2d2 + 2 d dkv + 3 d dff) + 2 nvocab dF ≈ 2 (P − nvocab d) + 4 L T dMkv = 2 L dkv q

        参数是每个块的注意力（WQ、WO 两张 d × d，WK、WV 两张 d × dkv）加 MLP 三张表，乘以块数，再加上输入的查表和输出的那张大表。算一个词元的运算，是除了查表以外每个参数 2 次，再加上和前面 T 个词元打分、混合的那部分。每个词元要为后面的词元存下每一层的键和值。The parameters are each block’s attention (WQ and WO, two d × d tables; WK and WV, two d × dkv tables) plus the MLP’s three tables, times the number of blocks, plus the embedding table at the input and the big table at the output. The operations for one token are 2 per parameter, leaving out the embedding table, plus the part for scoring and mixing with the T tokens before it. Every token has to store its keys and values in every layer for the tokens that come after it.

        MLP 那三张表对每个词元各用一次，所以和第 ③ 步一样，每个参数 2 次运算；查表只是取出一行，不算乘法。打分那部分是第 ⑦ 步的 4T2d 分摊到每个词元：一个新词元要和前面 T 个词元各做一次点积、各混合一次，每层 4Td。Each of the MLP’s three tables is used once per token, so, as in step ③, each parameter costs 2 operations; the embedding lookup only reads out a row and involves no multiplication. The scoring part is step ⑦’s 4T2d spread over the tokens: a new token does one dot product with each of the T tokens before it and mixes each of them in once, 4Td per layer.

        L
        块的个数（层数）number of blocks (layers)
        dkv
        键和值的宽度：键值头的个数 × 每个头的宽度width of the keys and values: number of key–value heads × width of each head
        nvocab
        词表大小；查表和输出各有一张 nvocab × d 的表vocabulary size; the embedding lookup and the output each have an nvocab × d table
        T
        这个词元前面已经有几个词元（上下文长度）how many tokens already come before this one (the context length)
        Mkv, q
        每个词元的键值缓存（字节）；每个数的字节数KV cache per token (bytes); bytes per number

        P = 32 × 218,112,000 + 2 × 128,256 × 4096 + 4096 = 8,030,261,248。其中 MLP 占 70.2%（56.4 亿），注意力占 16.7%（13.4 亿），查表和输出两张表占 13.1%（各 5.25 亿）。BF16 存是 16.1 GB，FP8 是 8.0 GB，FP4 是 4.0 GB。算一个词元：2 × (8,030,261,248 − 525,336,576) ≈ 150 亿次，约 15 GFLOP（第 13 篇按 2 × 全部参数粗算，写的是 1.6 × 1010，多算了查表那 1 GFLOP）；如果前面已经有 2000 个词元，再加 4 × 32 × 2000 × 4096 ≈ 1.05 GFLOP。键值缓存：dkv = 8 × 128 = 1024，2 × 32 × 1024 × 2 字节 = 131,072 字节，每个词元约 131 kB；2000 个词元是 262 MB，13 万个词元是 17 GB。P = 32 × 218,112,000 + 2 × 128,256 × 4096 + 4096 = 8,030,261,248. Of these, the MLP accounts for 70.2% (5.64 billion), attention for 16.7% (1.34 billion), and the embedding and output tables for 13.1% (525 million each). Stored in BF16 that is 16.1 GB, in FP8 8.0 GB, in FP4 4.0 GB. One token: 2 × (8,030,261,248 − 525,336,576) ≈ 15 billion operations, about 15 GFLOP (page 13 estimated it roughly as 2 × all the parameters and wrote 1.6 × 1010, counting an extra 1 GFLOP for the embedding lookup); if 2000 tokens already come before it, add 4 × 32 × 2000 × 4096 ≈ 1.05 GFLOP. KV cache: dkv = 8 × 128 = 1024, and 2 × 32 × 1024 × 2 bytes = 131,072 bytes, about 131 kB per token; 2000 tokens take 262 MB, and 130,000 tokens 17 GB.

        算一个词元 15 GFLOP，可往外写字时每写一个，都要把 16 GB 的权重从内存里读一遍，而且只能一个一个写：这就是第 13 篇讲的"内存墙"。读进来的那句话（加上前面的提示，比如 2000 个词元）倒是可以一次并行算完，约 2000 × 15 GFLOP = 30 TFLOP，每个权重被用 2000 次。One token takes 15 GFLOP, but when the model writes, every token it writes means reading all 16 GB of weights from memory once more, and it can only write one token at a time: this is the "memory wall" covered on page 13. The sentence it reads in (plus the prompt before it, say 2000 tokens), on the other hand, can be computed in parallel in one go: about 2000 × 15 GFLOP = 30 TFLOP, with each weight used 2000 times.

        WK 和 WV 为什么只有 4096 × 1024？Llama 3.1 8B 有 32 个查询头，却只有 8 组键和值，每 4 个查询头共用一组（70B 是 64 个查询头共用 8 组），叫分组查询注意力。论文说这样做是为了推理更快、键值缓存更小（Llama 3）：缓存只有 32 组时的四分之一。更极端的是 Gemma 2B（Gemma Team 2024）：8 个查询头只用 1 组键值，每个词元的缓存只有 2 × 18 层 × 256 × 2 字节 = 18,432 字节。它 d = 2048、18 层、dff = 16,384，词表 256,128；除查表以外 19.8 亿个参数，查表 5.2 亿（它的输出直接用查表那张表，不另存一张：所以 P 里只加一个 nvocab d；可输出那一步照样要乘这张表，每个词元的运算约 2 × 25 亿 ≈ 5 GFLOP）。论文表 1 写的前馈宽度 32,768，是门和内容两张表加起来。第 ⑬ 步的动作模型就是用它当骨干。Why are WK and WV only 4096 × 1024? Llama 3.1 8B has 32 query heads but only 8 sets of keys and values, each set shared by 4 query heads (the 70B has 64 query heads sharing 8 sets); this is called grouped-query attention. The paper says this is to make inference faster and the KV cache smaller (Llama 3): the cache is a quarter of what it would be with 32 sets. Gemma 2B goes further still (Gemma Team 2024): its 8 query heads share just 1 set of keys and values, so each token’s cache is only 2 × 18 layers × 256 × 2 bytes = 18,432 bytes. It has d = 2048, 18 layers, dff = 16,384 and a vocabulary of 256,128; 1.98 billion parameters outside the embedding table and 520 million in it (its output reuses the embedding table rather than storing a separate one, so P includes only one nvocab d; but the output step still has to multiply by this table, so each token takes about 2 × 2.5 billion ≈ 5 GFLOP). The feedforward width of 32,768 given in the paper’s Table 1 is the gate and content tables added together. The action model in step ⑬ uses it as its backbone.

        同样的块加宽加深，就是更大的模型：Llama 3.1 70B 是 d = 8192、80 层、dff = 28,672，算下来约 706 亿个参数，每个词元约 139 GFLOP。BF16 存要 141 GB，比机器人身上 128 GB 的内存还大；用 FP8（71 GB）放得下，可每写一个词元都要读一遍这 71 GB，在每秒 273 GB 的内存上，每秒写不到 4 个词元（8B 用 FP8 约 34 个）。所以机器人身上跑 8B，更难的问题交给云上的 70B（第 13 篇）。Make the same blocks wider and deeper and you get a bigger model: Llama 3.1 70B has d = 8192, 80 layers and dff = 28,672, which works out at about 70.6 billion parameters and about 139 GFLOP per token. In BF16 it needs 141 GB, more than the robot’s 128 GB of memory; in FP8 (71 GB) it fits, but every token it writes means reading those 71 GB once more, and with memory that delivers 273 GB per second, that is fewer than 4 tokens per second (the 8B in FP8 manages about 34). So the robot runs the 8B on board and hands the harder problems to the 70B in the cloud (page 13).

        • 图从上往下读：最上面是句子切成的词元和查表；中间框里是一个块：归一化、注意力、加回去、归一化、MLP、加回去，每个箭头上写着这里的数是什么形状（T 个词元，每个 d 个数）；左边一列细条是叠起来的 L 层；最下面是最后的归一化、输出的大表和 softmax。右边是键值缓存。Read the diagram from top to bottom: at the top are the tokens the sentence is cut into, and the embedding lookup; the box in the middle is one block: normalise, attention, add back, normalise, MLP, add back, with each arrow labelled with the shape of the numbers at that point (T tokens, d numbers each); the column of thin strips on the left is the L layers stacked up; at the bottom are the final normalisation, the big output table and softmax. On the right is the KV cache.
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：分 12 步，"递"这个词元从查表出发，一个框一个框往下走，再把同样的块走完 L 层（层数计数从 1 走到 L），最后变成下一个词元的概率。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: in 12 steps, the token "pass" sets off from the embedding lookup, moves down box by box, goes through the same block for all L layers (the layer counter runs from 1 to L), and finally becomes probabilities for the next token.
        • 四个模型按钮：玩具（第 ⑦ 步那句话的前 3 个词元"把、那杯水、递"，d = 4，只有 1 层，词表 4 个，每个箭头上写出"递"的 4 个数，最后猜下一个是"给我"）、Gemma 2B、Llama 3.1 8B（默认）、Llama 3.1 70B。读数：参数按查表、注意力、MLP、归一化、输出分段；BF16、FP8、FP4 各占多少字节；每个词元多少运算。Four model buttons: Toy (the first 3 tokens of the step ⑦ sentence, "ba", "that glass of water", "pass"; d = 4, just 1 layer and a vocabulary of 4; the 4 numbers of "pass" are written on every arrow, and at the end it guesses that the next token is "to me"), Gemma 2B, Llama 3.1 8B (default) and Llama 3.1 70B. Readouts: parameters broken down into embedding, attention, MLP, normalisation and output; how many bytes in BF16, FP8 and FP4; operations per token.
        • 拖 上下文（1 到 131,072 个词元）：打分那部分运算和键值缓存跟着涨。Drag Context (1 to 131,072 tokens): the scoring operations and the KV cache grow with it.

        先玩：先选玩具，一步一步看"递"的 4 个数在每个框里怎么变，最后为什么猜"给我"。再选 Llama 3.1 8B：参数里哪一段最大？每个词元的运算，大约是参数的几倍？把上下文拖到 13 万：键值缓存有多大，和权重本身比呢？换成 Gemma 2B 再拖一次：它的缓存为什么小那么多？Try first: Choose Toy first and watch, step by step, how the 4 numbers of "pass" change in each box, and why it ends up guessing "to me". Then choose Llama 3.1 8B: which part of the parameters is the biggest? Operations per token: roughly how many times the parameter count? Drag Context to 130,000: how big is the KV cache, and how does it compare with the weights themselves? Switch to Gemma 2B and drag again: why is its cache so much smaller?

        这一步的要点：大语言模型 = 查表 + L 个"注意力 + MLP"块（各带归一化和残差）+ 输出表。参数大头在 MLP（Llama 3.1 8B 里占七成），每个词元每个参数约 2 次运算（8B 约 15 GFLOP），再加上随上下文变长的注意力。BF16 一个参数 2 字节，8B 就是 16 GB；每个词元还要存键值缓存（8B 每个词元约 131 kB），分组查询注意力让它小几倍。The point of this step: a large language model = embedding lookup + L "attention + MLP" blocks (each with normalisation and residual connections) + output table. Most of the parameters are in the MLP (70% in Llama 3.1 8B); each token costs about 2 operations per parameter (about 15 GFLOP for the 8B), plus attention, which grows with the context. In BF16 a parameter takes 2 bytes, so the 8B is 16 GB; each token also has to store its KV cache (about 131 kB per token for the 8B), which grouped-query attention makes several times smaller.

        第 ⑨ 步 · 图像变成词元Step ⑨ · Images into tokens

        把照片切成小方块，当成一句话来读Cut the photo into small patches and read it like a sentence

        第 ⑧ 步的 Transformer 读的是一串词元。能不能让它也读图？ViT（视觉 Transformer）的做法很直接（Dosovitskiy 等 2021）：把照片切成一个个小方块，每个方块拉成一串数，乘一张表变成一个词元，再把这些词元排成一行，送进和语言模型一样的块里。动作模型用来"看"的 SigLIP So400m/14 就是这样一个网络（SigLIP 配置），这一步跟着一张 224 × 224 的照片走一遍：The Transformer of step ⑧ reads a string of tokens. Can it be made to read pictures too? ViT (the Vision Transformer) does it in a very direct way (Dosovitskiy et al. 2021): cut the photo into small patches, flatten each patch into a string of numbers, multiply that by a table to turn it into a token, then line the tokens up in a row and feed them into the same blocks a language model uses. SigLIP So400m/14, which the action model uses to "see", is one such network (SigLIP config); this step follows one 224 × 224 photo all the way through:

        • 切块：每块 14 × 14 个像素，一行 224 / 14 = 16 块，一共 16 × 16 = 256 块。Cut into patches: each patch is 14 × 14 pixels, 224 / 14 = 16 patches to a row, 16 × 16 = 256 patches in all.
        • 拉平：一块有 14 × 14 个像素，每个像素 3 个数，拉成一串 588 个数。Flatten: a patch has 14 × 14 pixels with 3 numbers each, laid out as one string of 588 numbers.
        • 变成词元：乘一张 588 → 1152 的表，每块变成 1152 个数；再加上一串表示"我在第几行第几列"的位置编码（也是学出来的）。256 块就是 256 个词元。Turn into tokens: multiply by a 588 → 1152 table, and each patch becomes 1152 numbers; then add a positional encoding (also learned), a string of numbers saying "which row and column I am in". 256 patches make 256 tokens.
        • 27 个块：和第 ⑧ 步一样的"归一化 → 注意力 → 加回去 → 归一化 → MLP → 加回去"，只是不用掩码：每一块都可以看所有的块。宽度 1152，MLP 中间 4304，16 个头。27 blocks: the same "normalise → attention → add back → normalise → MLP → add back" as in step ⑧, only with no mask: every patch can look at all the patches. Width 1152, 4304 in the middle of the MLP, 16 heads.
        • 输出：256 个词元，每个 1152 个数，各自描述"这一块里、结合整张图看，有什么"。动作模型把它们各乘一张 1152 → 2048 的表，变成和文字词元一样宽，再接到文字的词元旁边（第 ⑬ 步）。Output: 256 tokens of 1152 numbers each, each describing "what is in this patch, seen in the light of the whole picture". The action model multiplies each one by a 1152 → 2048 table, making it as wide as a text token, and then places them beside the text tokens (step ⑬).
        T = (n / k)2F ≈ L (8 T d2 + 4 T d dff + 4 T2 d)

        词元个数等于"图的边长除以方块边长"的平方。一张图的运算是每层三部分加起来再乘层数：注意力的四张表，MLP 的两张表，还有打分和混合。The number of tokens is the square of "the image’s side divided by the patch’s side". The work for one image is three parts per layer, added up and multiplied by the number of layers: attention’s four tables, the MLP’s two tables, and the scoring and mixing.

        每个词元都要乘一遍每张权重表，所以前两项和 T 成正比：每个权重被 T 个词元各用一次；第三项是第 ⑦ 步的 T2。"切块再乘表"其实就是一个 14 × 14、步长 14 的卷积（第 ④ 步）：核和图上一块一样大，一格不重叠地滑。Every token has to be multiplied by every weight table, so the first two terms are proportional to T: each weight is used once by each of the T tokens; the third term is the T2 of step ⑦. "Cut into patches, then multiply by a table" is really a 14 × 14 convolution with stride 14 (step ④): the kernel is the size of one patch of the picture, and it slides along with not a single pixel of overlap.

        n, k
        图的边长；方块的边长（像素）the image’s side; the patch’s side (in pixels)
        T
        词元个数（方块个数）number of tokens (number of patches)
        d, dff, L
        每个词元的数；MLP 中间的宽度；块数numbers per token; width of the MLP’s middle layer; number of blocks

        SigLIP So400m/14：n = 224，k = 14，T = 256，d = 1152，dff = 4304，L = 27。每层乘表约 7.8 GFLOP，打分和混合约 0.30 GFLOP；27 层加上切块那一步，一张图约 219 GFLOP（约 2190 亿次运算），其中注意力的打分和混合只有 8.2 GFLOP。参数约 4.12 亿（我们按它的配置算的，不含最后汇总用的那个小头）：每层 1524 万，乘 27 层；切块的表 588 × 1152 加偏置 678,528 个；位置编码 256 × 1152 = 294,912 个。BF16 约 0.82 GB。SigLIP So400m/14: n = 224, k = 14, T = 256, d = 1152, dff = 4304, L = 27. Per layer, the table multiplications take about 7.8 GFLOP and the scoring and mixing about 0.30 GFLOP; the 27 layers plus the patch-cutting step come to about 219 GFLOP per image (about 219 billion operations), of which attention’s scoring and mixing is only 8.2 GFLOP. About 412 million parameters (we counted them from its config, leaving out the small summarising head at the end): 15.24 million per layer, times 27 layers; the patch table, 588 × 1152 plus biases, 678,528; the positional encoding, 256 × 1152 = 294,912. About 0.82 GB in BF16.

        219 GFLOP 是 ResNet-50（8.2 GFLOP）的 27 倍：一是它大，4.12 亿个参数是 ResNet-50（2556 万）的 16 倍；二是每一层都让 256 个词元各过一遍 1500 多万个参数，每个参数用 256 次（ResNet-50 平均每个参数用约 160 次）。同样是 ViT，8600 万参数的 ViT-B/16 只是 ResNet-50 的 4 倍多。它值得，是因为它从第一层起就能让相隔很远的两块互相看（卷积要叠很多层，感受野才长那么大），而且和语言模型用的是同一种零件，出来的东西乘一张表就能和文字放在一起。原论文也发现，训练数据少时，卷积网络"只看邻居、到处一样"的先天假设更占便宜；数据够多时，ViT 更好（Dosovitskiy 等 2021）。219 GFLOP is 27 times ResNet-50 (8.2 GFLOP): first, it is big, with 412 million parameters, 16 times as many as ResNet-50 (25.56 million); second, every layer puts each of the 256 tokens through more than 15 million parameters, so each parameter is used 256 times (ResNet-50 uses each parameter about 160 times on average). Another ViT, the 86-million-parameter ViT-B/16, costs only a little over 4 times as much as ResNet-50. It is worth it because from the very first layer it lets two patches far apart look at each other (a convolutional network has to stack many layers before its receptive field grows that large), and because it is built from the same parts as a language model, so what comes out can sit next to text after one multiplication by a table. The original paper also found that with little training data, the convolutional network’s built-in assumptions, "look only at the neighbours, the same everywhere", give it the edge; with enough data, ViT does better (Dosovitskiy et al. 2021).

        分辨率和方块大小Resolution and patch size

        224 × 224 对一张厨房照片来说很粗：一个 14 × 14 的方块，在头部相机的原图上是约 80 × 45 个像素，杯子把手可能就在一块里。想看得更细，有两个办法，都要多花运算。一是把图放大：PaliGemma 用同一个 SigLIP，把图放到 448 × 448（1024 个词元）或 896 × 896（4096 个词元）（Beyer 等 2024）。按上面的公式，448 时约 973 GFLOP，896 时约 5.46 TFLOP：词元变成 16 倍，乘表也是 16 倍，打分是 256 倍，打分那部分从不到 4% 涨到 38%。二是把方块切小：同样 224 × 224，方块边长 16 是 196 个词元，8 是 784 个，32 只有 49 个。常用的 ViT-B/16 就是方块 16、12 层、宽 768，8600 万个参数，一张图约 35 GFLOP。224 × 224 is coarse for a kitchen photo: one 14 × 14 patch covers about 80 × 45 pixels of the head camera’s original image, so the handle of the glass may fit inside a single patch. There are two ways to see finer detail, and both cost more operations. One is to enlarge the image: PaliGemma uses the same SigLIP but scales the image to 448 × 448 (1024 tokens) or 896 × 896 (4096 tokens) (Beyer et al. 2024). By the formula above, that is about 973 GFLOP at 448 and about 5.46 TFLOP at 896: the tokens become 16 times as many, the table multiplications 16 times too and the scoring 256 times, so the scoring’s share rises from under 4% to 38%. The other is to cut smaller patches: on the same 224 × 224, a patch side of 16 gives 196 tokens, 8 gives 784, and 32 only 49. The widely used ViT-B/16 has patches of 16, 12 layers and width 768: 86 million parameters and about 35 GFLOP per image.

        • 左边是一张画出来的厨房照片，盖着 16 × 16 的格子。▶ 播放、下一步、上一步：一块被拿出来，拉成 588 个数，乘 588 × 1152 的表，变成 1152 个数的词元，加上位置编码，排进 256 个词元那一行，送进 27 层。点任何一块，就跟着那一块走。On the left is a drawing of the kitchen with a 16 × 16 grid over it. ▶ Play, Next step, Back: one patch is lifted out, flattened into 588 numbers, multiplied by the 588 × 1152 table into a token of 1152 numbers, given its positional encoding, placed in the row of 256 tokens and sent into the 27 layers. Tap any patch to follow that one.
        • 分辨率：224、448、896，看词元个数、运算，和注意力占的比例。拖 块的边长（8、14、16、32）：词元个数 = (224 / 边长)²。Resolution: 224, 448, 896; see the number of tokens, the operations and the share taken by attention. Drag Patch side (8, 14, 16, 32): number of tokens = (224 / side)².
        • 下面三根条比较 ResNet-50、SigLIP So400m/14 和 ViT-B/16 的参数和运算。The three bars below compare the parameters and operations of ResNet-50, SigLIP So400m/14 and ViT-B/16.

        先玩：点杯子所在的那一块，跟着它走一遍：它在哪一步变成了 1152 个数？把分辨率换成 896：词元多了几倍，运算多了几倍，为什么不一样？把块的边长拖到 8：词元多少个？Try first: Tap the patch with the glass in it and follow it all the way: at which step does it become 1152 numbers? Set the resolution to 896: how many times more tokens, how many times more operations, and why are the two different? Drag the patch side to 8: how many tokens?

        这一步的要点：ViT 把图切成 k × k 的方块，每块乘一张表变成一个词元（等于一个步长 k 的卷积），然后用和语言模型一样的块来处理，不加掩码。词元个数 (n/k)² 决定了成本：乘表和词元个数成正比，打分和它的平方成正比。SigLIP So400m/14 看一张 224 × 224 的图：256 个词元，约 4.1 亿个参数，约 219 GFLOP。The point of this step: ViT cuts the image into k × k patches and multiplies each by a table to make one token (the same as a convolution with stride k), then processes them with the same blocks as a language model, without a mask. The number of tokens, (n/k)², sets the cost: the table multiplications are proportional to the number of tokens, the scoring to its square. SigLIP So400m/14 looking at one 224 × 224 image: 256 tokens, about 410 million parameters, about 219 GFLOP.

        第 ⑩ 步 · 声音进网络Step ⑩ · Sound into the network

        Whisper：先把声音画成图，再编码、再写字Whisper: draw the sound as a picture, encode it, then write it down

        唤醒词网络叫醒机器人以后，那句 2 秒钟的"把那杯水递给我"要变成文字。麦克风给的是一串数：每秒 16000 个，2 秒是 32,000 个，每个数是那一刻空气压力的大小。直接把这 32,000 个数当输入，太长，也太"原始"：人听出一个字，靠的是哪些频率在响、响多久。所以很多听声音的网络（包括 Whisper），第一步都不是网络，而是一个固定的算法，把声音画成一张频谱图：横轴是时间，纵轴是频率（第 12 篇第 ⑨ 步细讲，那里叫语谱图）。也有直接吃原始波形、用卷积自己学这一步的，比如 wav2vec 2.0（Baevski 等 2020）。Whisper 是这样做的（Radford 等 2022）：Once the wake-word network has woken the robot, the 2-second "Pass me that glass of water" has to become text. What the microphone gives is a string of numbers: 16000 a second, 32,000 in 2 seconds, each one the air pressure at that instant. Taken directly as the input, these 32,000 numbers are too long, and too "raw": people recognise a word by which frequencies are sounding, and for how long. So in many networks that listen (Whisper included), the first step is not a network at all but a fixed algorithm that draws the sound as a spectrogram: time along the horizontal axis, frequency up the vertical one (page 12 covers it in detail in step ⑨). Some networks instead take in the raw waveform and learn this step themselves with convolutions, such as wav2vec 2.0 (Baevski et al. 2020). Here is how Whisper does it (Radford et al. 2022):

        • 画成图：每 10 毫秒取一段 25 毫秒长的声音（400 个数），算出它在 80 个频带上各有多响，取对数。频带按人耳的感觉排（Mel 刻度）：低频分得细，高频分得粗。每 10 毫秒得到 80 个数，2 秒是 80 × 200 的一张图。Draw it as a picture: every 10 milliseconds, take a 25-millisecond stretch of sound (400 numbers), work out how loud it is in each of 80 frequency bands, and take the logarithm. The bands are spaced the way the human ear hears (the Mel scale): finely at low frequencies, coarsely at high ones. Every 10 milliseconds gives 80 numbers, so 2 seconds make an 80 × 200 picture.
        • 补到 30 秒：Whisper 总是一次听 30 秒，不够就补静音。30 秒是 80 × 3000 的图，我们那 2 秒只占最左边的 200 列。Pad to 30 seconds: Whisper always listens to a 30-second window at a time, padding any shortfall with silence. 30 seconds is an 80 × 3000 picture, and our 2 seconds take up only the leftmost 200 columns.
        • 卷积开头：两层沿时间方向的卷积，核宽 3，把每个时刻的 80 个数变成 768 个数；第二层步长 2，3000 个时刻变成 1500 个位置。这就是 1500 个"声音词元"。Opening convolutions: two convolution layers running along time, kernel width 3, turn the 80 numbers at each moment into 768 numbers; the second layer has stride 2, so 3000 moments become 1500 positions. These are the 1500 "sound tokens".
        • 编码器：12 个和第 ⑨ 步一样的块（不加掩码），宽 768，让 1500 个位置互相看，消化成 1500 × 768 个数。Encoder: 12 blocks like those of step ⑨ (no mask), width 768, which let the 1500 positions look at one another and digest them into 1500 × 768 numbers.
        • 解码器：又是 12 个块，一个词元一个词元地往外写字，和第 ⑧ 步的语言模型一样带掩码；不同的是每个块里多一层交叉注意力，用来看编码器的输出。Decoder: another 12 blocks, which write out the text one token at a time, with a mask like the language model of step ⑧; the difference is that each block has an extra layer of cross-attention for looking at the encoder’s output.
        Q = X WQ K = Xenc WK V = Xenc WVS = Q KT / √d

        交叉注意力和第 ⑦ 步的注意力只差一点：查询来自解码器这边正在写的字，键和值来自编码器那边的声音。Cross-attention differs from the attention of step ⑦ in just one point: the queries come from the text being written on the decoder side, and the keys and values from the sound on the encoder side.

        写每一个字时，解码器要知道"现在该听哪一段"。它用自己的查询去和 1500 个声音位置的键比对，看中哪段就把哪段的值拿过来。写"杯"字时，有几个头的权重会集中在 2 秒里说"杯"的那两三百毫秒附近（Whisper 正是靠这几个头给每个字标时间）。这也是把两种东西（声音和文字）接在一起的一种办法，第 ⑬ 步还会碰到。For each word it writes, the decoder needs to know "which stretch to listen to now". It compares its query with the keys of the 1500 sound positions and takes the values of whichever stretch it picks. When it writes "bēi" (glass), a few heads put their weight around the two or three hundred milliseconds of the 2 seconds in which "bēi" is said (these are the very heads Whisper relies on to time-stamp each word). This is also one way to join two kinds of thing (sound and text) together, and we will meet it again in step ⑬.

        X
        解码器这边已经写出的 Tout 个词元，每个 d 个数the Tout tokens the decoder has written so far, d numbers each
        Xenc
        编码器的输出：1500 个位置，每个 d 个数the encoder’s output: 1500 positions, d numbers each
        S
        打分表，Tout 行、1500 列：每个字对每个声音位置的分数。多头时每个头各算一张，除以的是每头的数的平方根：Whisper small 12 个头、每头 64 个数，除以 √64 = 8the score table, Tout rows by 1500 columns: a score for every written token against every sound position. With several heads, each head computes its own table, and the divisor is the square root of the numbers per head: Whisper small has 12 heads of 64 numbers each, so it divides by √64 = 8

        Whisper small，d = 768。编码器的输出 Xenc 一段只算一次，它的键和值也只算一次：12 层 × 2 张表 × 1500 × 768 × 768 ≈ 212 亿次乘加，约 42 GFLOP。之后每写一个字，交叉注意力只需算自己的查询（768 × 768 次乘加），和 1500 个键各做一次点积，再按权重混合 1500 个值：后两样每层 2 × 1500 × 768 ≈ 230 万次乘加。Whisper small, d = 768. The encoder’s output Xenc is computed only once per window, and so are its keys and values: 12 layers × 2 tables × 1500 × 768 × 768 ≈ 21.2 billion multiply–adds, about 42 GFLOP. After that, for each token it writes, cross-attention only has to compute its own query (768 × 768 multiply–adds), take one dot product with each of the 1500 keys, and mix the 1500 values by weight: the last two come to 2 × 1500 × 768 ≈ 2.3 million multiply–adds per layer.

        编码器在前面把声音一次消化好，解码器写多少个字都反复用它：这种"编码器–解码器"的组合，适合"输入一大块、输出一串"的任务，比如听写和翻译。The encoder digests the sound once, up front, and the decoder keeps reusing it however many words it writes: this "encoder–decoder" combination suits tasks of the "a big chunk in, a sequence out" kind, such as transcription and translation.

        多大，多少运算，为什么浪费了 93%How big, how many operations, and why 93% is wasted

        Whisper 的论文列了五种大小（Radford 等 2022，表 1）：tiny 3900 万个参数，base 7400 万，small 2.44 亿，medium 7.69 亿，large 15.5 亿。后来的 large-v3 改用 128 个频带（OpenAI）；2024 年的 large-v3-turbo 把解码器从 32 层减到 4 层，8.09 亿个参数（OpenAI）。拿 small 算（我们按结构算出约 2.41 亿，和论文的 244M 差不多）：The Whisper paper lists five sizes (Radford et al. 2022, Table 1): tiny with 39 million parameters, base 74 million, small 244 million, medium 769 million, large 1.55 billion. The later large-v3 switched to 128 frequency bands (OpenAI); the 2024 large-v3-turbo cut the decoder from 32 layers to 4, for 809 million parameters (OpenAI). Taking small as the example (counting from its structure we get about 241 million, close to the paper’s 244M):

        • 卷积开头：约 6.4 GFLOP。Opening convolutions: about 6.4 GFLOP.
        • 编码器：T = 1500 个位置，d = 768，dff = 3072，L = 12，用第 ⑨ 步的公式，一段约 338 GFLOP。Encoder: T = 1500 positions, d = 768, dff = 3072, L = 12; with the formula from step ⑨, about 338 GFLOP per window.
        • 交叉注意力的键和值：一段约 42 GFLOP。Cross-attention keys and values: about 42 GFLOP per window.
        • 解码器：每写一个词元约 0.33 GFLOP。一句话十几个词元，加起来几 GFLOP。Decoder: about 0.33 GFLOP for each token written. A sentence is a dozen or so tokens, a few GFLOP in all.

        加起来，听写这一句约 0.39 万亿次运算，八成多在编码器。可编码器是按 30 秒一段算的：不管你说 2 秒还是 29 秒，它都算 1500 个位置。我们这 2 秒只占 30 秒的 1/15，所以编码器 93% 的运算花在了补上的静音上。这是 Whisper 的设计取舍：训练时统一按 30 秒切，模型简单，长录音也好处理；代价是短命令很不划算，而且它不能边听边出字。这也是为什么一直开着的唤醒词用的是第 ⑥ 步那个 79 kB 的小网络，而不是 Whisper。Added up, transcribing this one sentence takes about 0.39 trillion operations, over 80% of them in the encoder. But the encoder works in 30-second windows: whether you speak for 2 seconds or 29, it computes 1500 positions. Our 2 seconds are only 1/15 of the 30, so 93% of the encoder’s work goes on the padded silence. This is a design trade-off in Whisper: cutting everything into 30-second pieces for training keeps the model simple and makes long recordings easy to handle; the price is that short commands are very poor value, and it cannot write while it is still listening. It is also why the always-on wake word uses the small 79 kB network of step ⑥ rather than Whisper.

        声音、图像、文字走到这里，用的是差不多的块。差别主要在最前面：文字查表，图像切块，声音先画成频谱图再卷积。到了块里，都是"T 个词元，每个 d 个数"。So far, sound, images and text all use much the same blocks. The differences are mostly at the very front: text goes through an embedding lookup, images are cut into patches, and sound is first drawn as a spectrogram and then convolved. Inside the blocks, it is all "T tokens of d numbers each".

        • 从上往下六条：① 那句话的声音；② 它的频谱图，80 行 × 200 列；③ 补到 30 秒，3000 列（压缩着画，2 秒那一段高亮）；④ 卷积开头，3000 个时刻变成 1500 个位置；⑤ 编码器，一层一层填满；⑥ 解码器，每写一个字，画出它的交叉注意力看向前 2 秒里的哪些位置（示意）。Six strips, from top to bottom: ① the sound of the sentence; ② its spectrogram, 80 rows × 200 columns; ③ padded to 30 seconds, 3000 columns (drawn squeezed, with the 2-second stretch highlighted); ④ the opening convolutions, 3000 moments becoming 1500 positions; ⑤ the encoder, filling up layer by layer; ⑥ the decoder: for each token it writes, lines show which positions in the first 2 seconds its cross-attention looks at (illustrative).
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：分 14 步，一条一条往下走；解码器那条一步写一个字，写出每个字的运算。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: 14 steps, going down strip by strip; on the decoder strip each step writes one token and shows what it costs.
        • 模型：tiny、base、small、medium、large（按钮下写着层数和宽度）。读数：参数、BF16 占的字节、帧数和位置数、每段编码器的运算、每个词元解码器的运算、花在补静音上的比例。Model: tiny, base, small, medium, large (the number of layers and the width are written under each button). Readouts: parameters, bytes in BF16, frames and positions, the encoder’s operations per window, the decoder’s operations per token, and the share spent on padded silence.

        先玩：走到解码器那一条，看它写"杯"字时，交叉注意力主要看向哪一段。把模型从 small 换到 large：编码器一段的运算变成几倍？如果你说的是 15 秒的话，浪费的比例是多少？Try first: Go down to the decoder strip and see which stretch cross-attention mainly looks at when it writes "bēi". Switch the model from small to large: how many times more operations does the encoder need per window? If you spoke for 15 seconds, what share would be wasted?

        这一步的要点：声音先被一个固定的算法画成频谱图（每 10 毫秒 80 个数），再用卷积变成 1500 个"声音词元"。Whisper 是编码器–解码器：编码器把一段声音一次消化好，解码器一个词元一个词元地写字，靠交叉注意力回头听。small 有约 2.4 亿个参数，编码一段约 338 GFLOP，按 30 秒一段算，2 秒的命令 93% 都花在了静音上。The point of this step: Sound is first drawn as a spectrogram by a fixed algorithm (80 numbers every 10 milliseconds), then turned into 1500 "sound tokens" by convolutions. Whisper is an encoder–decoder: the encoder digests a window of sound in one go, and the decoder writes the text one token at a time, listening back through cross-attention. Whisper small has about 240 million parameters and takes about 338 GFLOP to encode a window; because it works in 30-second windows, 93% of the work on a 2-second command goes on silence.

        第 ⑪ 步 · 输出一整张图Step ⑪ · Outputting a whole image

        U-Net：先缩小看懂，再放大画准U-Net: shrink to understand, then scale up to draw precisely

        ResNet-18 告诉机器人"画面里有个杯子"，可要抓它，还得知道杯子的边到底在哪：哪些像素是杯子，哪些是桌子和墙。这要输出一整张图：每个像素一个数，"这个像素属于杯子的把握"。这类任务叫分割，输入 256 × 256 × 3，输出 256 × 256 × 1，65,536 个数。ResNet-18 tells the robot "there is a glass in the picture", but to grab it, the robot also has to know exactly where the glass’s edge is: which pixels are glass and which are table and wall. That means outputting a whole image: one number per pixel, "how sure the network is that this pixel belongs to the glass". This kind of task is called segmentation: the input is 256 × 256 × 3 and the output 256 × 256 × 1, 65,536 numbers.

        难处在于两个要求互相拉扯。要认出"这是杯子"，得看得够大，所以网络要像 ResNet 那样把图一路缩小（第 ⑤ 步）；可缩到 16 × 16 时，原图上 16 × 16 个像素挤在一格里，杯子的边早就糊了。U-Net（Ronneberger 等 2015）的办法是：左边一路缩小，右边一路放大回来，画出来像一个 U；关键是在每一级，把左边同样大小的那张特征图，直接跳连到右边，拼在放大回来的特征图旁边。The difficulty is that two demands pull against each other. To recognise "this is a glass", the network has to see a large enough area, so, like ResNet, it keeps shrinking the image (step ⑤); but by the time it is down to 16 × 16, 16 × 16 pixels of the original are squeezed into one cell, and the glass’s edge has long since blurred. U-Net (Ronneberger et al. 2015) solves this as follows: shrink all the way down on the left, scale all the way back up on the right, and the drawing looks like a U. The key is that at every level, the feature map of the same size on the left goes straight across to the right through a skip connection and is joined on beside the scaled-up feature map.

        • 往下：每一级两层 3 × 3 卷积，然后取最大池化把边长减半。我们这个小 U-Net 的通道是 32、64、128、256：256 × 256 × 32 → 128 × 128 × 64 → 64 × 64 × 128 → 32 × 32 × 256。Going down: at each level, two 3 × 3 convolutions, then max pooling halves the side length. Our small U-Net has 32, 64, 128 and 256 channels: 256 × 256 × 32 → 128 × 128 × 64 → 64 × 64 × 128 → 32 × 32 × 256.
        • 谷底：16 × 16 × 512，图最小、通道最多，一格能看到很大一片。The bottom: 16 × 16 × 512, the smallest map and the most channels; each cell sees a large area.
        • 往上：每一级先把图放大一倍（每个数复制成 2 × 2），再用一个 2 × 2 的卷积把通道减半；然后把左边同级那张图拼过来，通道又翻倍；再两层 3 × 3 卷积。32 × 32 × 256 → 64 × 64 × 128 → 128 × 128 × 64 → 256 × 256 × 32。Going up: at each level, first scale the map up to twice the side (each number copied into a 2 × 2 block), then halve the channels with a 2 × 2 convolution; next, join on the map from the same level on the left, which doubles the channels again; then two 3 × 3 convolutions. 32 × 32 × 256 → 64 × 64 × 128 → 128 × 128 × 64 → 256 × 256 × 32.
        • 输出：一个 1 × 1 的卷积把 32 个通道变成 1 个，每个像素弯一下（S 形），得到 0 到 1 之间的把握。Output: a 1 × 1 convolution turns the 32 channels into 1, and each pixel goes through the bend (sigmoid), giving a number between 0 and 1 for how sure the network is.
        dl = gl( [ u(dl+1), el ] )

        右边第 l 级的输出，是把下面一级的结果放大，和左边同一级的特征图拼在一起，再过两层卷积。The output of level l on the right is the result from the level below, scaled up and joined with the feature map of the same level on the left, then put through two convolutions.

        从谷底上来的那一路知道"这一片是什么"，但位置很粗；从左边跳过来的那一路是高分辨率的，知道"边在哪个像素"，但只看了一小片，不知道是什么。拼在一起，后面的卷积就能同时用上两样：在"是杯子"的那一片里，沿着清楚的边画出轮廓。没有跳连，输出只能从 16 × 16 的谷底一路插值上来，边是糊的。The path coming up from the bottom knows "what this area is", but its sense of position is coarse; the path skipping across from the left is high-resolution and knows "which pixel the edge is on", but it has only seen a small area and does not know what it is. Once they are joined, the convolutions that follow can use both at once: inside the area that "is glass", they draw the outline along the sharp edge. Without the skip connections, the output can only be interpolated all the way up from the 16 × 16 bottom, and the edge is blurred.

        el
        左边（往下那一路）第 l 级的特征图；l = 0 是最大的那一级Feature map at level l on the left (the way down); l = 0 is the largest level
        dl
        右边（往上那一路）第 l 级的输出Output of level l on the right (the way up)
        u
        放大一倍再用 2 × 2 卷积把通道减半Scale up to twice the side, then halve the channels with a 2 × 2 convolution
        [ , ]
        按通道拼在一起：两张一样大的图叠成一张，通道数相加Join along the channels: two maps of the same size stacked into one, their channel counts added
        gl
        这一级的两层 3 × 3 卷积（各带归一化和弯一下）This level’s two 3 × 3 convolutions (each with normalisation and the bend)

        第 3 级：谷底 d4 是 16 × 16 × 512，放大成 32 × 32，2 × 2 卷积减成 256 个通道；左边的 e3 也是 32 × 32 × 256；拼起来 32 × 32 × 512；两层卷积 512 → 256、256 → 256，得到 d3 = 32 × 32 × 256。这一级的乘加：放大后的卷积 4 × 512 × 256 × 32 × 32 ≈ 0.54 G，第一层 9 × 512 × 256 × 32 × 32 ≈ 1.21 G，第二层 0.60 G，一共约 2.35 G。Level 3: the bottom, d4, is 16 × 16 × 512; it is scaled up to 32 × 32, and a 2 × 2 convolution cuts it to 256 channels. On the left, e3 is also 32 × 32 × 256; joined, they make 32 × 32 × 512. Two convolutions, 512 → 256 and 256 → 256, give d3 = 32 × 32 × 256. Multiply–adds at this level: the convolution after upsampling, 4 × 512 × 256 × 32 × 32 ≈ 0.54 G; the first layer, 9 × 512 × 256 × 32 × 32 ≈ 1.21 G; the second layer, 0.60 G; about 2.35 G in all.

        跳连本身只是"复制过来、拼上去"，没有参数；可拼上去以后，下一层卷积的输入通道翻倍，这个小 U-Net 因此多了约 78 万个参数、24 亿次乘加（约占全部运算的 18%）。它还要求左边那些大特征图一直存着（一共约 393 万个数），等右边用到为止。A skip connection itself is just "copy it over, join it on", with no parameters; but once it is joined on, the next convolution has twice as many input channels, so this small U-Net gains about 780,000 parameters and 2.4 billion multiply–adds (about 18% of all the operations). It also means the large feature maps on the left have to be kept in memory (about 3.93 million numbers in all) until the right side uses them.

        多大，多少运算How big, how many operations

        按第 ④ 步的公式一级一级加起来，这个小 U-Net 有 7,765,985 个参数（FP32 约 31.1 MB），一张 256 × 256 的图要 136.8 亿次乘加，约 27.4 GFLOP，是 ResNet-18 看一张 224 × 224 的图的 7.5 倍。参数和运算又一次分在两头：参数最多的是谷底（354 万）和紧挨着它的那一级（230 万），那里通道最多；运算最多的是往上那四级，每级都是约 2.35 G 次乘加，加起来占了近七成，往下那四级只有 0.66 到 0.91 G：往上时每级多了"放大后的卷积"，第一层卷积的输入又因为拼接多了一倍通道。和 ResNet 一样，每级边长减半、通道加倍，运算两头抵消，所以同一侧每一级的运算都差不多。Adding it up level by level with the formulas from step ④, this small U-Net has 7,765,985 parameters (about 31.1 MB in FP32), and one 256 × 256 image takes 13.68 billion multiply–adds, about 27.4 GFLOP: 7.5 times what ResNet-18 needs for one 224 × 224 image. Once again the parameters and the operations sit at opposite ends. Most of the parameters are at the bottom (3.54 million) and in the level right next to it (2.3 million), where there are the most channels; most of the operations are in the four levels on the way up, about 2.35 G multiply–adds each, together nearly 70% of the total, while the four levels on the way down take only 0.66 to 0.91 G each. On the way up, each level has the extra "convolution after upsampling", and its first convolution has twice the input channels because of the join. As in ResNet, each level halves the side length and doubles the channels, and the two cancel out in the operations, so every level on the same side costs about the same.

        输入变大时，参数不变，运算和面积成正比：512 × 512 是 4 倍，1024 × 1024 是 16 倍。原论文的通道是这里的两倍（64 到 1024），参数约 3100 万。U-Net 后来在别处也很常见：画图的扩散模型、第 ⑭ 步的 Diffusion Policy，用它来从噪声里一步步"擦"出结果，因为那也是"输入一张图（或一串动作），输出同样大小的一张"。When the input gets bigger, the parameters stay the same and the operations grow in proportion to the area: 512 × 512 takes 4 times as many, and 1024 × 1024 takes 16 times as many. The original paper uses twice the channels used here (64 to 1024), with about 31 million parameters. U-Net later became common elsewhere too: diffusion models that draw pictures, and the Diffusion Policy of step ⑭, use it to "wipe" the result out of the noise step by step, because that too is "an image (or a sequence of actions) in, one of the same size out".

        • 中间是那个 U：左边往下，右边往上，方块的大小表示边长和通道，灰色箭头是跳连。下面是三张小图：输入的照片、这一步的特征图（按真实的格子画）、输出的杯子掩码（示意）；再下面两排条是每一级的乘加和参数，蓝色是放大那一步占的。In the middle is the U: down on the left, up on the right; the size of each box shows its side length and channels, and the grey arrows are the skip connections. Below are three small pictures: the input photo, the feature map at this step (drawn on its real grid) and the output glass mask (illustrative). Below those, two rows of bars show each level’s multiply–adds and parameters, with blue for the share taken by the upsampling step.
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：图沿着 U 一级一级往下走，再一级一级往上走。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: the image travels down the U level by level, then back up level by level.
        • 去掉跳连：掩码只能从 16 × 16 的谷底放大回来，边变糊，读数里写出错了多少个像素。通道：一半 16…256、我们的 32…512、2015 原版 64…1024。Remove the skip connections: the mask can only be scaled back up from the 16 × 16 bottom, so the edge blurs, and the readout shows how many pixels come out wrong. Channels: Half 16…256, Ours 32…512, 2015 paper 64…1024.
        • 拖 输入边长 n（128 到 1024），看运算怎么涨、参数为什么不变。Drag Input side n (128 to 1024) to see how the operations grow and why the parameters do not change.

        先玩：▶ 播放一遍，数一数往下缩了几次、往上放大了几次。对比下面的条：运算最多的是哪几级？打开"去掉跳连"，掩码的哪一部分变得最糊？把输入边长拖到 512：运算变成几倍？Try first: Press ▶ Play once and count how many times the image shrinks on the way down and how many times it is scaled up on the way up. Compare the bars below: which levels do the most operations? Turn on "Remove the skip connections": which part of the mask blurs the most? Drag the input side to 512: by what factor do the operations grow?

        这一步的要点：要输出一整张图，网络先缩小看懂"是什么"，再放大画准"在哪"；U-Net 用跳连把左边高分辨率的特征直接拼到右边。我们这个小 U-Net 有约 777 万个参数，一张 256 × 256 的图约 27.4 GFLOP，大头在往上那一路；跳连本身不带参数，但让后面的卷积变宽，还要把大特征图存着。The point of this step: To output a whole image, the network first shrinks to understand "what", then scales up to draw "where" precisely; U-Net uses skip connections to join the high-resolution features from the left straight onto the right. Our small U-Net has about 7.77 million parameters and takes about 27.4 GFLOP for one 256 × 256 image, most of it on the way up; the skip connections have no parameters of their own, but they make the convolutions after them wider, and the large feature maps have to be kept.

        第 ⑫ 步 · 点云Step ⑫ · Point clouds

        PointNet：一堆没有顺序的点，怎么喂给网络PointNet: how to feed a pile of unordered points into a network

        要伸手去抓，光知道杯子在照片上的哪些像素还不够，得知道它在空间里的位置和形状。深度相机每个像素给出那一点沿相机光轴的深度 Z，848 × 480 个像素就是 407,040 个深度；知道相机的内参（焦距和主点），每个像素都能换算成空间里的一个点 (x, y, z)（第 2 篇讲过怎么换）。把杯子那一块的点挑出来，随机取 1024 个，就是杯子的点云。To reach out and grab the glass, knowing which pixels of the photo it covers is not enough; the robot needs to know its position and shape in space. For every pixel, the depth camera gives the depth Z of that point along the camera’s optical axis, so 848 × 480 pixels give 407,040 depths. With the camera’s intrinsic parameters (focal length and principal point), each pixel can be turned into a point (x, y, z) in space (page 2 showed how). Pick out the points belonging to the glass, take 1024 of them at random, and you have the glass’s point cloud.

        点云和照片不一样。照片的像素排在整齐的格子上，左边挨着谁、上面挨着谁是固定的，所以卷积能滑。点云只是一堆点：同一个杯子，这 1024 个点可以按任何顺序列出来，每一帧拍到的点也不一样多（所以才随机取 1024 个）。直接把 1024 × 3 = 3072 个数接一个全连接层也不行：全连接层的每个输入位置有自己的权重，把点的顺序打乱，输出就变了，可杯子还是那个杯子。A point cloud is not like a photo. A photo’s pixels sit on a neat grid, and each pixel’s neighbours to the left and above are fixed, so a convolution can slide over it. A point cloud is just a pile of points: for the same glass, these 1024 points can be listed in any order, and each frame captures a different number of points (which is why we take 1024 at random). Feeding the 1024 × 3 = 3072 numbers straight into a dense layer does not work either: each input position of a dense layer has its own weights, so shuffling the order of the points changes the output, yet the glass is still the same glass.

        PointNet（Qi 等 2017）用了两招。第一，每个点过同一个小 MLP：每个点单独算，权重大家共用（相当于第 ④ 步的卷积取 k = 1，也就是第 ⑤ 步见过的 1 × 1 卷积）。第二，取最大：每个特征，在所有点里取最大的那个值。最大值不管谁先谁后，所以结果和点的顺序无关。先手算一个只有 4 个点、3 个特征的小例子（单位厘米，杯底中心是原点）：A (−4, 0, 0) 在杯底左边，B (4, 0, 0) 在杯底右边，C (0, 0, 12) 在杯口，D (0, 3, 6) 在杯身中间。共用的"小 MLP"只有一层：特征 1 = ReLU(x)，"往右伸出多远"；特征 2 = ReLU(−x)，"往左伸出多远"；特征 3 = ReLU(z)，"多高"。PointNet (Qi et al. 2017) uses two tricks.
