First, every point goes through the same small MLP: each point is computed on its own, and all of them share the weights (the same as the convolution of step ④ with k = 1, that is, the 1 × 1 convolution seen in step ⑤). Second, take the maximum: for each feature, keep the largest value over all the points. A maximum does not care which comes first, so the result does not depend on the order of the points. Start with a small example worked by hand, with only 4 points and 3 features (in centimetres, origin at the centre of the glass’s base): A (−4, 0, 0) on the left of the base, B (4, 0, 0) on the right of the base, C (0, 0, 12) at the mouth of the glass, D (0, 3, 6) halfway up the side. The shared "small MLP" has only one layer: feature 1 = ReLU(x), "how far it reaches to the right"; feature 2 = ReLU(−x), "how far it reaches to the left"; feature 3 = ReLU(z), "how high".

        gj = maxi hj(pi)y = f(g)

        每个点 pi 都过同一个小网络 h，得到一串特征；第 j 个全局特征，是所有点的第 j 个特征里最大的那个；最后再用一个网络 f，从全局特征算出想要的输出。Every point pi goes through the same small network h, giving a list of features; the j-th global feature is the largest of all the points’ j-th features; finally another network f computes the output we want from the global feature.

        "取最大"是一个和顺序无关的运算：把数打乱，最大的还是它。加法、平均也行，可最大值有个好处：它记住的是"有没有哪个点很像第 j 种图案"，一个点就够，不会被大量普通的点冲淡。小网络 h 对每个点都一样，所以不管有 1024 个点还是 5000 个点，参数都不变，只是运算跟着点数变。"Take the maximum" is an operation that ignores order: shuffle the numbers and the largest one is still the largest. A sum or an average would also work, but the maximum has an advantage: what it records is "is there any point that looks a lot like pattern j", and one point is enough; it is not diluted by lots of ordinary points. The small network h is the same for every point, so whether there are 1024 points or 5000, the parameters stay the same; only the operations change with the number of points.

        pi
        第 i 个点的 (x, y, z)The (x, y, z) of the i-th point
        h, hj
        每个点共用的小 MLP；它的第 j 个输出特征The small MLP shared by every point; its j-th output feature
        g, gj
        全局特征（一串数）；它的第 j 个数The global feature (a list of numbers); its j-th number
        maxi
        把 i 取遍所有点，取最大的Let i run over all the points and take the largest
        f, y
        后面的 MLP；输出（比如"是不是杯子"的分数，或者该抓的位置）The MLP that follows; the output (for example a score for "is it a glass", or where to grab)

        四个点的特征：A (0, 4, 0)，B (4, 0, 0)，C (0, 0, 12)，D (0, 0, 6)。每个特征取最大：特征 1 是 4（来自 B），特征 2 是 4（来自 A），特征 3 是 12（来自 C）。全局特征 g = (4, 4, 12)：这个东西左右各伸出 4 厘米、高 12 厘米。把四个点的顺序随便换，g 还是 (4, 4, 12)。D 没有在任何一个特征上拿到最大，把它删掉，g 也不变。The four points’ features: A (0, 4, 0), B (4, 0, 0), C (0, 0, 12), D (0, 0, 6). Take the maximum of each feature: feature 1 is 4 (from B), feature 2 is 4 (from A), feature 3 is 12 (from C). The global feature g = (4, 4, 12): this object reaches out 4 centimetres on each side and is 12 centimetres tall. Put the four points in any order and g is still (4, 4, 12). D does not have the maximum for any feature; delete it and g does not change either.

        真正决定输出的，只是那些至少拿到一次最大值的点，论文叫它们关键点。它们往往勾出物体的轮廓，所以 PointNet 对少几个点、多几个噪点不太敏感。Only the points that hold the maximum at least once really decide the output; the paper calls them critical points. They tend to trace the object’s outline, so PointNet is not very sensitive to a few missing points or a few extra noisy ones.

        真实的 PointNetThe real PointNet

        真实的小 MLP 宽得多：每个点先 3 → 64 → 64，再 64 → 64 → 128 → 1024，每个点变成 1024 个特征；取最大，得到 1024 个数的全局特征；再过 1024 → 512 → 256 → 输出。论文里还加了两个小 PointNet（叫 T-Net）：一个在最前面，估计一个 3 × 3 的矩阵，把点"摆正"；一个在 3 → 64 → 64 之后，估计一个 64 × 64 的矩阵，把特征"摆正"。The real small MLP is much wider: each point goes 3 → 64 → 64, then 64 → 64 → 128 → 1024, so each point becomes 1024 features; taking the maximum gives a global feature of 1024 numbers, which then goes through 1024 → 512 → 256 → output. The paper also adds two small PointNets (called T-Nets): one at the very start estimates a 3 × 3 matrix to "set the points straight"; one after 3 → 64 → 64 estimates a 64 × 64 matrix to "set the features straight".

        它的大小：带 T-Net 共约 350 万个参数，每个 1024 个点的样本约 4.4 亿次乘加，即 0.88 GFLOP；不带 T-Net 是 80 万个参数、1.48 亿次乘加（Qi 等 2017，表 6。表里写的是"FLOPs"，可按结构一层层数下来，它和乘加的次数对得上，所以本篇按乘加算）。其中每个点共用的那个 MLP，一个点要 147,648 次乘加，1024 个点约 1.5 亿次，差不多就是不带 T-Net 时的全部运算：点数加倍，这部分运算加倍，参数不变；它的每个权重被 1024 个点各用一次。Its size: with the T-Nets, about 3.5 million parameters in all and about 440 million multiply–adds per sample of 1024 points, that is 0.88 GFLOP; without the T-Nets, 800,000 parameters and 148 million multiply–adds (Qi et al. 2017, Table 6. The table says "FLOPs", but counting through the architecture layer by layer, its figures match the number of multiply–adds, so this page counts them as multiply–adds). Of this, the MLP shared by every point takes 147,648 multiply–adds per point, about 150 million for 1024 points, which is nearly all the operations of the version without T-Nets: double the points and these operations double, while the parameters stay the same; each of its weights is used once by each of the 1024 points.

        对机器人来说，PointNet 吃进杯子的点云，可以输出"这是杯子"，也可以训练成输出抓的位置和夹爪的朝向。后来的点云网络（比如先在每个点的邻居里做 PointNet 的 PointNet++、只在有点的格子上做卷积的稀疏体素网络、把点当词元的 Transformer）都在补它的短处：它把每个点单独看，再一下子取最大，不太看局部的邻居关系。For the robot, PointNet takes in the glass’s point cloud and can output "this is a glass", or it can be trained to output where to grab and which way the gripper should face. Later point-cloud networks (such as PointNet++, which first runs PointNet within each point’s neighbourhood; sparse voxel networks, which convolve only over the cells that contain points; and Transformers that treat points as tokens) all make up for its weakness: it looks at each point on its own and then takes the maximum all at once, paying little attention to local relations between neighbouring points.

        • 左边是杯子放在桌上的点云，1024 个点全画出来，可以拖着转；点一个点（或者按 换一个点），它的 (x, y, z) 进入共用的小 MLP：3 → 64 → 64 → 64 → 128 → 1024，每层一条色带，只画一次，因为每个点都过同一个。On the left is the point cloud of the glass on the table, with all 1024 points drawn; drag it to rotate. Tap a point (or press Another point) and its (x, y, z) goes into the shared small MLP: 3 → 64 → 64 → 64 → 128 → 1024, one colour strip per layer, drawn only once because every point goes through the same one.
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：所有点各过一遍小 MLP，排成一张"点 × 1024 个特征"的表，每一列取最大，得到全局特征，再过后面的 MLP 到输出。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: every point goes through the small MLP once, the results form a "points × 1024 features" table, the maximum of each column gives the global feature, and the MLP that follows takes it to the output.
        • 打乱点的顺序：全局特征一个数都不变。哪些点说了算：把至少拿到一次最大值的关键点标出来。交互里的权重是随机取的、没训练过，每个数都是真算的，但挑出的关键点不一定勾出轮廓；训练好的 PointNet 挑出的关键点会勾出物体的骨架（论文里的图）。Shuffle the points: not one number of the global feature changes. Which points decide: marks the critical points, the ones that hold the maximum at least once. The weights in the interactive are random and untrained; every number is really computed, but the critical points it picks do not necessarily trace the outline. A trained PointNet picks critical points that trace the object’s skeleton (see the figure in the paper).
        • 拖 点数 N（256 到 8192）：看共用 MLP 的运算怎么跟着涨，参数为什么不变。Drag Points N (256 to 8192) to see how the shared MLP’s operations grow with it and why the parameters do not change.

        先玩：点几个不同位置的点，看它们的特征条有什么不同。按"打乱点的顺序"，全局特征变了吗？按"哪些点说了算"：有多少个点说了算？它们多在杯壁、杯底，还是水面上？把点数从 1024 拖到 4096：运算变成几倍？Try first: Tap a few points in different places and see how their feature strips differ. Press "Shuffle the points": does the global feature change? Press "Which points decide": how many points decide? Are they mostly on the wall, the base or the water surface? Drag the number of points from 1024 to 4096: by what factor do the operations grow?

        这一步的要点：点云没有格子也没有顺序。PointNet 让每个点过同一个小 MLP，再对每个特征在所有点里取最大，所以结果和点的顺序无关，参数和点数无关。它约 350 万个参数，1024 个点约 0.88 GFLOP；只有拿到最大值的关键点真正决定输出。The point of this step: A point cloud has no grid and no order. PointNet sends every point through the same small MLP, then takes, for each feature, the maximum over all the points, so the result does not depend on the order of the points and the parameters do not depend on the number of points. It has about 3.5 million parameters and takes about 0.88 GFLOP for 1024 points; only the critical points, the ones that hold a maximum, really decide the output.

        第 ⑬ 步 · 多模态Step ⑬ · Multimodal

        三张图、一句话、21 个关节角，进同一个网络Three images, one sentence and 21 joint angles go into one network

        前面每个网络只吃一种东西：声音、照片、文字、点云。机器人伸手那 5 秒，要同时用上好几种：三台相机看到的、你说的那句话、自己 21 个关节现在的角度，算出接下来手臂怎么动。像 π0 这样的视觉–语言–动作模型（Black 等 2024）就是干这个的。它的做法可以一句话说完：把每种输入都变成词元，排成一行，交给同一个 Transformer。拆开看，一次调用是这样的：Every network so far takes in only one kind of thing: sound, photos, text or point clouds. In the 5 seconds the robot takes to reach out, it has to use several at once to work out how its arms should move next: what the three cameras see, the sentence you said, and the current angles of its own 21 joints. That is the job of a vision–language–action model such as π0 (Black et al. 2024). Its method fits in one sentence: turn every input into tokens, line them up in one sequence, and hand that to a single Transformer. Taken apart, one call goes like this:

        • 看：头部和两个手腕的三张照片，各缩成 224 × 224，各过一遍 SigLIP（第 ⑨ 步），各得到 256 个词元，一共 768 个。每张约 219 GFLOP，三张约 0.66 TFLOP（6600 亿次运算）。Look: three photos, from the head and the two wrists, are each shrunk to 224 × 224 and each passed once through SigLIP (step ⑨), giving 256 tokens each, 768 in all. That is about 219 GFLOP per photo, about 0.66 TFLOP for all three (660 billion operations).
        • 读："把那杯水递给我"查表变成词元（第 ⑧ 步），连同任务说明，算它约 32 个。Read: "Pass me that glass of water" is turned into tokens by embedding lookup (step ⑧); together with the task description, call it about 32.
        • 骨干：这约 800 个词元排成一行，进 Gemma 2B 的 18 个块（第 ⑧ 步），照片的词元和文字的词元在注意力里互相看。这一段约 3.3 TFLOP，最贵。Backbone: these roughly 800 tokens, lined up in one sequence, go through the 18 blocks of Gemma 2B (step ⑧), where the photo tokens and the text tokens look at each other through attention. This part is about 3.3 TFLOP, the most expensive.
        • 动作专家：一个约 3 亿参数的小 Transformer，专门处理"动作"这种词元：21 个关节角变成 1 个状态词元，再加 50 个动作词元，每个代表未来一个时刻 21 个关节的目标。它的每一层都能看骨干那一行词元的键和值（第 ⑦ 步），但用自己的权重。Action expert: a small Transformer of about 300 million parameters, dedicated to tokens of the "action" kind. The 21 joint angles become 1 state token, plus 50 action tokens, each standing for the targets of the 21 joints at one moment in the future. Each of its layers can look at the keys and values of the backbone’s sequence of tokens (step ⑦), but it uses its own weights.
        • 出动作：50 个动作词元一开始是随机的噪声，动作专家把它们"擦"10 遍，每遍让它们更像一段合理的动作，最后得到接下来 50 步、每步 21 个数的动作块。10 遍约 0.37 TFLOP（每遍只重算 50 个动作词元：除了乘自己的权重，还要看骨干那 800 个词元和状态词元；状态词元自成一块，只算一次，键和值存着）。Act: the 50 action tokens start out as random noise; the action expert "wipes" them 10 times, each pass making them look more like a sensible movement, and ends up with an action chunk: the next 50 steps, 21 numbers per step. The 10 passes take about 0.37 TFLOP (each pass recomputes only the 50 action tokens: besides multiplying by its own weights, it also looks at the backbone’s 800 tokens and the state token; the state token forms a block of its own, is computed only once, and its keys and values are kept).

        一次加起来约 4.3 万亿次运算（第 13 篇用"参数 × 词元 × 2"粗算的是 4.8 万亿，量级一样）；每秒 10 次，就是每秒 43 万亿次。π0 的论文在一块 RTX 4090 上测过：三张图的编码 14 毫秒，骨干 32 毫秒，动作专家 10 遍 27 毫秒，一共 73 毫秒（Black 等 2024）。有意思的是，动作专家的运算只有骨干的十分之一出头，用的时间却差不多（27 毫秒对 32 毫秒）：它每遍只有 50 个词元，每个权重只用 50 次，时间花在把权重从显存里读出来上（第 ⑮ 步、第 13 篇）。参数一共约 33 亿：SigLIP 约 4 亿，Gemma 2B 约 25 亿，动作专家约 3 亿；BF16 约 6.6 GB。One call adds up to about 4.3 trillion operations (page 13’s rough count, "parameters × tokens × 2", gives 4.8 trillion, the same order of magnitude); at 10 calls a second, that is 43 trillion a second. The π0 paper measured it on a single RTX 4090: 14 ms to encode the three images, 32 ms for the backbone, 27 ms for the action expert’s 10 passes, 73 ms in all (Black et al. 2024). Interestingly, the action expert does only a little over a tenth of the backbone’s operations, yet takes almost as long (27 ms against 32 ms): each pass has only 50 tokens, so each weight is used only 50 times, and the time goes on reading the weights out of GPU memory (step ⑮, page 13). In all there are about 3.3 billion parameters: SigLIP about 400 million, Gemma 2B about 2.5 billion, the action expert about 300 million; about 6.6 GB in BF16.

        两点值得停一下。第一，三张照片和一句话为什么能"排成一行"？因为到了 Transformer 里面，它们都只是"一个词元 = d 个数"：照片的 256 个词元先被一张表变成和文字词元一样宽的数（2048 个），从此不分你我。第二，"擦 10 遍"不是一种新的网络结构，而是一种用网络的办法：同一个动作专家，输入"现在这团带噪声的动作"和"擦到第几遍了"，输出"往哪个方向改"，连着用 10 次。这叫流匹配（扩散模型是它的近亲），第 7 篇专门讲。骨干那 3.3 TFLOP 只算一次，它的键和值存着，10 遍都能用，所以 10 遍加起来才 0.37 TFLOP。Two points are worth pausing on. First, why can three photos and a sentence go "in one sequence"? Because inside the Transformer they are all just "one token = d numbers": each photo’s 256 tokens are first turned by a table into numbers as wide as the text tokens (2048 of them), and from then on there is no telling them apart. Second, "wiping 10 times" is not a new network structure but a way of using a network: the same action expert takes in "the noisy blob of actions as it is now" and "which wipe this is", outputs "which way to change it", and is used 10 times in a row. This is called flow matching (diffusion models are close relatives of it); page 7 covers this. The backbone’s 3.3 TFLOP is spent only once, and its keys and values are kept for all 10 passes to use, which is why the 10 passes add up to just 0.37 TFLOP.

        几种东西，有几种拼法Several kinds of input, several ways to fuse them

        Sone: (Ta + Tb) × (Ta + Tb)Scross: Tb × Tay = γ(c) ⊙ x + β(c)

        排成一行时，打分表是"两边词元加起来"的平方那么大；交叉注意力时，只有一边看另一边，打分表是两边个数相乘；调制时，一边的信息只变成每个通道的一个放大倍数和一个偏移，作用在另一边上。In one sequence, the score table is the square of "the tokens on both sides added together"; in cross-attention, only one side looks at the other, and the score table is the two counts multiplied; in modulation, one side’s information becomes just a scale factor and a shift for each channel, applied to the other side.

        这三个式子对应三种把两路数据接起来的办法，越往后越省，也越"粗"。排成一行，每个词元都能看每个词元，最灵活；交叉注意力只让 b 去看 a，a 自己不受影响；调制只给 b 一个整体的"条件"，b 没法去看 a 的某一个地方。还有第四种最简单的：各算各的，最后把结果合起来（后期融合），中间一点都不交流。These three formulas match three ways of joining two streams of data; each is cheaper than the one before, and also "coarser". In one sequence, every token can look at every token: the most flexible. Cross-attention lets only b look at a, and a itself is left unaffected. Modulation gives b only one overall "condition", and b cannot look at any particular place in a. There is also a fourth way, the simplest: separate networks each compute on their own and the results are combined at the end (late fusion), with no exchange at all in between.

        Ta, Tb
        两路数据各有几个词元（比如 a 是照片，b 是动作）how many tokens each of the two streams has (e.g. a is the photos, b the actions)
        Sone, Scross
        排成一行时、交叉注意力时，一个头一层的打分表大小the size of the score table, per head and per layer, in one sequence and in cross-attention
        c, x, y
        条件（比如照片的特征）；被调制的那一路的数；调制后的结果the condition (e.g. the photo features); the numbers of the stream being modulated; the result after modulation
        γ(c), β(c)
        由条件算出来的每个通道的放大倍数和偏移the scale factor and shift for each channel, computed from the condition

        π0 的骨干：照片 768 个加文字约 32 个，约 800 个词元排成一行，打分表约 800 × 800 = 64 万格（每个头、每一层）。动作专家的 50 个动作词元每遍去看这 800 个：50 × 800 = 4 万格，外加看状态词元和自己 50 个的 50 × 51 格。调制就更小：Diffusion Policy 把照片的特征（连同"擦到第几遍"）变成 U-Net 每一层卷积、每个通道的 2 个数（Chi 等 2023）。π0’s backbone: 768 photo tokens plus about 32 text tokens make about 800 tokens in one sequence, with a score table of about 800 × 800 = 640,000 entries (per head, per layer). In each pass, the action expert’s 50 action tokens look at those 800: 50 × 800 = 40,000 entries, plus 50 × 51 for looking at the state token and at their own 50. Modulation is smaller still: Diffusion Policy turns the photo features (together with "which wipe this is") into 2 numbers for every channel of every convolutional layer in the U-Net (Chi et al. 2023).

        没有哪种拼法总是最好。排成一行能直接用上在网上大量图文数据里预训练好的视觉语言模型，这是 π0 这类模型最大的本钱；代价是词元一多就贵。交叉注意力适合"一边很长、一边很短"：Whisper 的解码器每写一个字，只用一个词元的查询去看 1500 个声音位置；π0 的动作专家每遍只有 50 个词元，去看骨干的 800 个，每擦一遍都很便宜（它没有单独的交叉注意力层，靠的是同一个注意力加掩码，效果一样）。No way of fusing is always best. One sequence can directly reuse a vision–language model already pretrained on huge amounts of image and text data from the web, which is the biggest asset of models like π0; the price is that it gets expensive as soon as there are many tokens. Cross-attention suits "one side long, one side short": for every word Whisper’s decoder writes, it uses the query of just one token to look at 1500 audio positions; π0’s action expert has only 50 tokens per pass, looking at the backbone’s 800, so each wipe is cheap (it has no separate cross-attention layer but relies on the same attention plus a mask, with the same effect).

        把前面的例子按拼法排一排：Here are the earlier examples, sorted by how they fuse:

        • 排成一行（早期融合）：π0、PaliGemma（Beyer 等 2024）：照片词元和文字词元直接排在一起。Figure 的 Helix 也用这一招把一快一慢两个网络接起来：每秒七到九次的 70 亿参数大模型给出一串数，投成每秒 200 次、8000 万参数的小网络的词元，和小网络自己的图像特征排在一起（Figure 2025）。One sequence (early fusion): π0 and PaliGemma (Beyer et al. 2024): photo tokens and text tokens are simply lined up together. Figure’s Helix uses the same trick to join a fast network and a slow one: a large model with 7 billion parameters, running seven to nine times a second, outputs a string of numbers that is projected into tokens for a small network with 80 million parameters, running 200 times a second, and lined up with that small network’s own image features (Figure 2025).
        • 交叉注意力：Whisper 的解码器看编码器（第 ⑩ 步）。π0 的动作专家效果上也是"一边看另一边"，但没有单独的交叉注意力层：状态和动作词元接在骨干那一行后面，进同一个注意力，用自己的一套权重；掩码让它们能看骨干，骨干看不到它们。Cross-attention: Whisper’s decoder looks at the encoder (step ⑩). π0’s action expert is in effect also "one side looking at the other", but without a separate cross-attention layer: the state and action tokens are appended after the backbone’s sequence and go into the same attention, with their own set of weights; the mask lets them see the backbone, but the backbone cannot see them.
        • 调制：Diffusion Policy 里，照片的特征只变成卷积 U-Net 每一层、每个通道的放大和偏移（FiLM），去调它擦噪声的方式。Modulation: in Diffusion Policy, the photo features only become a scale and a shift for each channel in each layer of the convolutional U-Net (FiLM), which adjust the way it wipes away the noise.
        • 各算各的（后期融合）：模块化的那条路：ResNet 认杯子，U-Net 抠像素，PointNet 找抓的位置，结果交给规划去组合。每个模块好测、好换，可它们之间不能互相帮忙：U-Net 不知道 PointNet 觉得哪里难抓。Late fusion (separate networks): the modular route. ResNet recognises the glass, U-Net picks out the pixels, PointNet finds where to grasp, and the results go to planning to be combined. Each module is easy to test and easy to swap, but they cannot help each other: the U-Net does not know which spots the PointNet finds hard to grasp.
        • 图从上往下读：三台相机的照片（头部、左腕、右腕）、那句话和 21 个关节角；照片过 SigLIP 变成 768 个图像词元（青色），话变成文字词元（紫色），排成一行进 Gemma 2B 的 18 层，旁边那个方块是按比例画的打分表；关节角变成状态词元（蓝色），和 50 个动作词元一起进动作专家，最后出来 50 步的动作块（红色）。Read the figure from top to bottom: the photos from the three cameras (head, left wrist, right wrist), the sentence and the 21 joint angles; the photos pass through SigLIP and become 768 image tokens (teal), the sentence becomes text tokens (purple), and together they go in one sequence through the 18 layers of Gemma 2B, with the square beside them showing the score table drawn to scale; the joint angles become a state token (blue), which goes into the action expert along with the 50 action tokens, and out comes the 50-step action chunk (red).
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：分 7 步，从三路输入一直走到动作块；到动作专家那一步，一团 50 × 21 的噪声在 10 遍里变成平滑的轨迹。下面一栏是每一段的运算，旁边是 π0 论文在 RTX 4090 上实测的时间。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: 7 steps, from the three inputs all the way to the action chunk; at the action-expert step, a 50 × 21 blob of noise turns into smooth trajectories over 10 passes. The panel below shows the operations of each part, next to the times measured on an RTX 4090 in the π0 paper.
        • 怎么拼：排成一行（默认，π0 的做法）、交叉注意力、调制、各算各的。每种都改画一遍，写出好处和坏处，以及注意力打分表有多大；后三种是我们画的示意，读数里的参数和运算仍是 π0 的，作对照。How to fuse: One sequence (the default, π0’s way), Cross-attention, Modulation, Late fusion. Each one redraws the figure and spells out its pros and cons and how big the attention score table is; the last three are our own sketches, and the parameters and operations in the readout stay π0’s, for comparison.

        先玩：▶ 播放一遍：哪一段的运算最大？看动作专家那 10 遍：第几遍以后，噪声已经看得出轨迹的样子？把"怎么拼"换成交叉注意力和调制：打分表小了多少，丢掉了什么？Try first: Run ▶ Play once: which part has the most operations? Watch the action expert’s 10 passes: after which pass can you already see the shape of a trajectory in the noise? Switch "How to fuse" to Cross-attention and to Modulation: how much smaller does the score table get, and what is lost?

        这一步的要点：多模态模型先把每种输入变成词元（照片切块、文字查表、关节角乘一张表），再拼起来。拼法有四种：排成一行（最灵活、最贵）、交叉注意力（一边看另一边）、调制（只给一个整体条件）、各算各的。π0 一次调用约 4.3 TFLOP，大头在骨干；"擦 10 遍"是同一个动作专家用 10 次，不是新的结构。The point of this step: a multimodal model first turns every input into tokens (photos cut into patches, text by embedding lookup, joint angles multiplied by a table), then joins them. There are four ways to join them: one sequence (the most flexible, the most expensive), cross-attention (one side looks at the other), modulation (only one overall condition) and late fusion (separate networks). One π0 call is about 4.3 TFLOP, most of it in the backbone; "wiping 10 times" is the same action expert used 10 times, not a new structure.

        第 ⑭ 步 · 积木怎么拼Step ⑭ · How the blocks fit together

        十种积木，拼出所有这些网络Ten kinds of building block make up all these networks

        回头看，前面十几个网络用到的零件，其实只有十来种。每一种都在某一步拆开算过：Looking back, the dozen or so networks so far use only about ten kinds of part. Each kind has been taken apart and worked through in one of the steps:

        积木Building block干什么What it does参数Parameters一个输入的运算Operations per input
        全连接Dense (fully connected)每个输出看每个输入（第 ②、③ 步）Every output looks at every input (steps ② and ③)nin × nout + nout每个权重用 1 次：约 2PEach weight used once: about 2P
        卷积Convolution一个小核在格子上滑，到处用同一组权重（第 ④ 步）A small kernel slides over the grid, using the same set of weights everywhere (step ④)k² × Cin × Cout + Cout，和图的大小无关k² × Cin × Cout + Cout, independent of the image size每个权重用 n′² 次Each weight used n′² times
        循环Recurrence带着一个固定大小的状态，一步一步读（第 ⑥ 步）Carries a fixed-size state and reads one step at a time (step ⑥)nh(nin + nh + 1)，带门的 × 3 或 × 4nh(nin + nh + 1), × 3 or × 4 with gates每一步用一遍全部权重，T 步串着算All the weights once per step, T steps one after another
        注意力Attention按内容现算谁看谁（第 ⑦ 步）Works out from the content, on the spot, who looks at whom (step ⑦)几张 d × d 的表A few d × d tables8Td² + 4T²d
        池化Pooling取最大或取平均，把图变小、把一堆点并成一个（第 ④、⑫ 步）Takes the max or the mean, shrinking an image or merging a pile of points into one (steps ④ and ⑫)0很少Very little
        上采样Upsampling把图放大（第 ⑪ 步）Enlarges an image (step ⑪)0（后面常跟一个卷积）0 (often followed by a convolution)很少Very little
        归一化Normalisation把数拉回合适的范围（第 ⑤、⑧ 步）Pulls the numbers back into a suitable range (steps ⑤ and ⑧)每个通道 2 个（或 1 个）2 per channel (or 1)很少Very little
        残差和跳连Residual and skip connections把前面的结果加回来或拼过来（第 ⑤、⑪ 步）Adds an earlier result back, or joins it on (steps ⑤ and ⑪)0很少，但要把前面的结果存着Very little, but the earlier result has to be kept
        门和路由Gates and routing一部分数决定另一部分记多少、走哪条路（第 ⑥ 步和下面）Some numbers decide how much the others keep, or which way they go (step ⑥ and below)算门的那几张表The tables that compute the gates和全连接一样Same as a dense layer
        查表Embedding lookup把编号（词元）变成一行数（第 ⑧ 步）Turns an ID (a token) into a row of numbers (step ⑧)nvocab × d0 次乘法，只是取出来0 multiplications; the row is just read out

        还要加上两样不是网络、却总在网络前后的东西：把原始数据变成网络能吃的形状（声音画成频谱图、照片切块、文字切词），和"怎么用这个网络"（算一遍，还是像流匹配那样连着算 10 遍）。有了这些，每个网络都能写成一条链：Add two more things that are not networks but always sit before or after one: turning raw data into a shape the network can take in (sound drawn as a spectrogram, photos cut into patches, text tokenised), and "how the network is used" (run once, or run 10 times in a row as in flow matching). With these, every network can be written as a chain:

        • ResNet-18：卷积 → 归一化 → 弯一下 → 池化 → 8 个残差块（各两层卷积）→ 平均池化 → 全连接。ResNet-18: convolution → normalisation → bend → pooling → 8 residual blocks (two convolutional layers each) → average pooling → dense layer.
        • ViT（SigLIP）：切块（一个大步长的卷积）+ 位置编码 → 27 ×（归一化 → 注意力 → 残差 → 归一化 → MLP → 残差）。ViT (SigLIP): cutting into patches (a convolution with a large stride) + positional encoding → 27 × (normalisation → attention → residual → normalisation → MLP → residual).
        • Whisper：频谱图 → 两层卷积 → 12 个注意力块（编码器）；查表 → 12 个"带掩码的注意力 + 交叉注意力 + MLP"块（解码器）→ 输出表。Whisper: spectrogram → two convolutional layers → 12 attention blocks (encoder); embedding lookup → 12 "masked attention + cross-attention + MLP" blocks (decoder) → output table.
        • Llama：查表 → 32 个注意力块 → 输出表。Llama: embedding lookup → 32 attention blocks → output table.
        • U-Net：（卷积 → 卷积 → 池化）× 4 → 谷底 → （上采样 → 2 × 2 卷积 → 跳连拼接 → 卷积 → 卷积）× 4 → 1 × 1 卷积。U-Net: (convolution → convolution → pooling) × 4 → the bottom → (upsampling → 2 × 2 convolution → join the skip connection → convolution → convolution) × 4 → 1 × 1 convolution.
        • PointNet：每个点过同一个 MLP → 取最大 → MLP。PointNet: every point through the same MLP → max pooling → MLP.
        • PaliGemma 和 π0：SigLIP（照片 → 词元）+ 查表（文字 → 词元）→ 排成一行进 Gemma；π0 再加一个动作专家，连着用 10 遍。PaliGemma and π0: SigLIP (photos → tokens) + embedding lookup (text → tokens) → one sequence into Gemma; π0 adds an action expert, used 10 times in a row.
        • Diffusion Policy（Chi 等 2023）：ResNet-18 把照片变成一串特征（不预训练，最后的平均池化换成"空间 softmax"，批归一化换成组归一化）→ 这串特征通过调制（FiLM）去控制一个沿时间方向的一维卷积 U-Net → U-Net 从噪声里把未来十几步的动作"擦"出来。论文说用 DDIM 擦 10 遍，在一块 RTX 3080 上约 0.1 秒（它的真机实验擦 16 遍）。Diffusion Policy (Chi et al. 2023): ResNet-18 turns the photos into a string of features (not pretrained, with the final average pooling replaced by a "spatial softmax" and batch normalisation replaced by group normalisation) → through modulation (FiLM), these features steer a 1D convolutional U-Net that runs along time → the U-Net "wipes" the actions for the next dozen or so steps out of the noise. The paper says that wiping 10 times with DDIM takes about 0.1 s on one RTX 3080 (its real-robot experiments wipe 16 times).
        • Mixtral 8x7B：Llama 那样的注意力块，只是每个块里的 MLP 换成了 8 个"专家"和一个路由器。Mixtral 8x7B: attention blocks like Llama’s, except that the MLP in each block is replaced by 8 "experts" and a router.

        路由：参数很多，每个词元只用一部分Routing: many parameters, but each token uses only some of them

        最后一条值得单独算一下。Llama 3.1 8B 每个词元要把 80 亿个参数全用一遍。想让模型知道得更多，就得更多参数，可每个词元的运算也跟着涨。混合专家（MoE）的想法是：参数可以很多，但每个词元只挑其中几份来用（Shazeer 等 2017）。The last one is worth working through on its own. Llama 3.1 8B uses all 8 billion of its parameters once for every token. To make a model know more you need more parameters, but then the operations per token go up too. The idea of a mixture of experts (MoE) is that there can be many parameters, but each token picks just a few parts of them to use (Shazeer et al. 2017).

        y = Σe ∈ S(x) ge(x) fe(x)M = Ptotal q F ≈ 2 Pactive

        一个词元的 MLP 输出，是被选中的那几个专家各自的输出，按路由器给的权重加起来。存储要装下所有专家；运算只算被选中的那几个。A token’s MLP output is made of the outputs of the few chosen experts, added up with the weights the router gives them. Storage has to hold every expert; operations are spent only on the chosen few.

        路由器就是一个小小的全连接层：输入这个词元的 d 个数，输出 E 个分数，一个专家一个；挑分数最高的 kE 个，把它们的分数 softmax 成权重。每个专家是一个普通的 MLP。不同的词元会走不同的专家，所以整个模型用到了全部参数，但每个词元只碰其中一小部分。这就是第 ⑥ 步说的"门"的另一种用法：门不只决定记多少，还决定走哪条路。The router is just a tiny dense layer: it takes the token’s d numbers and outputs E scores, one per expert; it picks the kE experts with the highest scores and turns their scores into weights with a softmax. Each expert is an ordinary MLP. Different tokens go to different experts, so the model as a whole uses all its parameters, but each token touches only a small part of them. This is another use of the "gate" from step ⑥: a gate decides not only how much to keep, but also which way to go.

        E, kE
        一层里一共几个专家；每个词元选几个how many experts a layer has in all; how many each token picks
        S(x)
        路由器为词元 x 选中的 kE 个专家the kE experts the router picks for token x
        fe, ge
        第 e 个专家（一个 MLP）；路由器给它的权重expert e (an MLP); the weight the router gives it
        Ptotal, Pactive
        全部参数；一个词元实际用到的参数all the parameters; the parameters one token actually uses

        Mixtral 8x7B：每层 8 个专家，每个词元选 2 个。"每个词元能用到 470 亿个参数，推理时只用 130 亿个"（Jiang 等 2024）。存储按 470 亿算：BF16 约 94 GB；运算按 130 亿算：每个词元约 26 GFLOP，比 470 亿参数的稠密模型少七成多。Mixtral 8x7B: 8 experts per layer, and each token picks 2. "Each token has access to 47 billion parameters, but only uses 13 billion during inference" (Jiang et al. 2024). Storage counts all 47 billion: about 94 GB in BF16; operations count only 13 billion: about 26 GFLOP per token, over 70% less than a dense model with 47 billion parameters.

        MoE 省运算，不省存储：全部专家都要放在内存里。一个人用时它还省带宽：每个词元只读被选中的专家。Mixtral 用 FP8 约 47 GB，放得进机器人 128 GB 的模块，每个词元读约 13 GB，每秒 273 GB 的内存下一秒约 21 个词元，同样大的稠密模型不到 6 个。批一大，所有专家都得读，就只剩省运算了。所以在内存装得下的机器人上，它反而比同样大的稠密模型写得快；内存装不下才用不了。MoE saves operations, not storage: all the experts have to sit in memory. With a single user it saves bandwidth too: each token reads only the chosen experts. In FP8, Mixtral takes about 47 GB, which fits in the robot’s 128 GB module; each token reads about 13 GB, so memory delivering 273 GB per second gives about 21 tokens a second, where a dense model of the same size manages fewer than 6. Once the batch is large, every expert has to be read and only the saving in operations is left. So on a robot whose memory can hold it, it actually writes faster than a dense model of the same size; it is ruled out only when the memory cannot hold it.

        • 选一个网络：ResNet-18、ViT、Whisper、PaliGemma、π0、PointNet、U-Net、扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）、Mixtral。图里从上往下是它的积木链：颜色表示是哪种积木，小记号标出池化（▼）、上采样（▲）、残差（⊕），箭头上写着数据的形状，重复的层用括号标出"× 27 层"这样的次数；右边的线是跳连、交叉注意力、调制和"连着用 10 遍"。每块旁边两根小条是它的参数和运算。Pick a network: ResNet-18, ViT, Whisper, PaliGemma, π0, PointNet, U-Net, Diffusion Policy, Mixtral. The figure shows its chain of building blocks from top to bottom: the colour says which kind of block it is, small marks show pooling (▼), upsampling (▲) and residuals (⊕), the arrows are labelled with the shape of the data, and repeated layers are marked with a bracket and a count such as "× 27 layers"; the lines on the right are skip connections, cross-attention, modulation and "used 10 times in a row". The two small bars beside each block are its parameters and operations.
        • ▶ 播放、下一步、上一步、重来：一个小点沿着链一块一块往下走，读数里累计"到这一步为止"的参数和运算；也可以直接点一块。精度：FP32、BF16、FP8、FP4，换算字节。▶ Play, Next step, Back, Restart: a small dot moves down the chain block by block, and the readout keeps a running total of the parameters and operations under "So far"; you can also tap a block directly. Precision: FP32, BF16, FP8, FP4, to convert to bytes.
        • 积木表：每一行是一种积木，每一列是一个网络，点表示用到了。点一行，链里用到这种积木的地方都亮起来；点列头换网络。Operation table: each row is one kind of building block, each column one network, and a dot means it is used. Tap a row and every place in the chain that uses that block lights up; tap a column head to switch network.

        先玩：在积木表里找一找：哪种积木几乎每个网络都用？哪种只有一两个网络用？哪一行是空的，为什么？选 π0：它的链里有几种颜色？选 Mixtral：参数和运算差了几倍，为什么？Try first: Look through the Operation table: which block does almost every network use? Which is used by only one or two networks? Which row is empty, and why? Pick π0: how many colours are there in its chain? Pick Mixtral: by what factor do its parameters and operations differ, and why?

        这一步的要点：几乎所有网络都由十来种积木拼成：全连接、卷积、循环、注意力、池化、上采样、归一化、残差和跳连、门和路由、查表。看一个新网络，先找出它用了哪几种、各在什么形状的数据上，参数和运算就能一段一段算出来。混合专家用路由让每个词元只用一部分参数：省运算，不省存储。The point of this step: almost every network is built from about ten kinds of building block: dense, convolution, recurrence, attention, pooling, upsampling, normalisation, residual and skip connections, gates and routing, embedding lookup. When you meet a new network, first find out which kinds it uses and on data of what shape; then its parameters and operations can be worked out part by part. A mixture of experts uses routing so that each token uses only part of the parameters: it saves operations, not storage.

        第 ⑮ 步 · 怎么选、要多大Step ⑮ · Choosing and sizing

        把所有网络放到一张图上All the networks on one chart

        前面每一步都算了两个数：参数有多少，一个输入要多少运算。把机器人身上的网络都放在一起：Every step so far has worked out two numbers: how many parameters there are, and how many operations one input takes. Here are all the robot’s networks together:

        唤醒词的 GRU 不在表里：论文只给了它连同中间结果一共 78.8 kB（8 比特）和每次 380 万次运算（第 ⑥ 步）；按它的结构估算，约 7.8 万个参数，下面的交互里画了它。The wake-word GRU is not in the table: the paper gives only its total size, 78.8 kB including intermediate results (8-bit), and 3.8 million operations per call (step ⑥). Estimated from its structure, it has about 78,000 parameters; the interactive below plots it.

        最右一列最有意思。参数和运算并不成正比：Llama 有 ResNet-18 的近 700 倍参数，写一个词元的运算却只是 ResNet-18 看一张图的 4 倍。原因就在"每个权重被用了几次"。The rightmost column is the most interesting. Parameters and operations are not proportional: Llama has nearly 700 times as many parameters as ResNet-18, yet writing one token takes only 4 times the operations ResNet-18 needs to look at one image. The reason lies in "how many times each weight is used".

        F / P ≈ 2 r

        一个输入的运算除以参数个数，约等于 2 乘以"每个权重平均被用了几次"。The operations for one input, divided by the number of parameters, come to about 2 times "how many times, on average, each weight is used".

        每用一次权重是一次乘加，2 次运算。全连接层对一个输入，每个权重只用一次，r = 1；卷积的权重在每个输出位置都用一次，r 是位置数；Transformer 的权重对每个词元各用一次，r 是词元数。注意力的打分和混合不用权重，会让 F / P 再高一点；查表的权重一个输入只取一行，会让 F / P 低一点。Each use of a weight is one multiply–add, 2 operations. In a dense layer each weight is used only once per input, so r = 1; a convolution’s weights are used once at every output position, so r is the number of positions; a Transformer’s weights are used once for each token, so r is the number of tokens. Attention’s scoring and mixing use no weights, which makes F / P a little higher; an embedding lookup reads only one row of its weights per input, which makes F / P a little lower.

        r
        对一个输入，每个权重平均被用了几次how many times each weight is used, on average, for one input

        MLP 和逐个写词元的语言模型 r ≈ 1；ResNet-18 在 224 × 224 的图上 r ≈ 155（前面的层一个核用几千次，后面的只用 49 次，平均下来）；SigLIP 的 256 个词元 r ≈ 256；Whisper 的编码器有 1500 个位置；U-Net 的大部分运算在 256 × 256、128 × 128 这样的大图上，r 上千。For the MLP and for a language model writing one token at a time, r ≈ 1; for ResNet-18 on a 224 × 224 image, r ≈ 155 (a kernel is used thousands of times in the early layers and only 49 times in the late ones; this is the average); for SigLIP’s 256 tokens, r ≈ 256; Whisper’s encoder has 1500 positions; most of U-Net’s operations happen on large maps, 256 × 256 or 128 × 128, so r runs into the thousands.

        这一列决定了网络在芯片上被什么卡住（第 13 篇）。按 FP8 存，F / P 就是每读一个字节的权重做几次运算，要和芯片的拐点（峰值 ÷ 带宽，第 13 篇第 ⑦ 步）比：比拐点大，瓶颈在算得多快；比拐点小，瓶颈在内存能多快把权重送过来。拐点不低：B200 约 560，Thor 按第 13 篇的假设约 1900。所以一次只算一张图、权重每次都从内存读的话，ResNet、SigLIP 也还在拐点左边，成批地算才能用上峰值的一大部分；逐个写词元的语言模型 r ≈ 1，一个人用时连峰值的百分之一都用不到（B200 上约 0.4%），除非同时给很多人写（一批 B 个人，r 就变成 B）。This column decides what holds a network back on a chip (page 13). With the weights stored in FP8, F / P is the number of operations done for each byte of weights read, and it has to be compared with the chip’s ridge point (peak ÷ bandwidth, page 13, step ⑦): above the ridge point, the bottleneck is how fast the chip computes; below it, the bottleneck is how fast memory can deliver the weights. These ridge points are high: about 560 for B200, and about 1900 for Thor under page 13’s assumptions. So if you compute one image at a time and read the weights from memory every time, even ResNet and SigLIP are still left of the ridge point; only computing in batches uses a large share of the peak. A language model writing one token at a time has r ≈ 1, and when it serves one person it uses less than one per cent of the peak (about 0.4% on B200), unless it writes for many people at once (with a batch of B people, r becomes B).

        装得下吗？跑得快吗？Does it fit? Is it fast enough?

        把一个网络放到一块芯片上，先算两笔账。装得下：M = P × q，加上中间结果（语言模型还有键值缓存）。机器人的模块有 128 GB：Llama 3.1 8B 用 BF16 要 16 GB，π0 类约 6.6 GB，Whisper small 约 0.5 GB，其他的加起来不到 0.1 GB，都放得下；Llama 3.1 70B 用 BF16 要 141 GB，放不下，用 FP8 要 71 GB，放得下，可每写一个词元都要读 71 GB。跑得快：两笔时间都要算，取慢的那个：算的时间是运算除以实际算力，读的时间是权重的字节除以带宽。NVIDIA 给 Thor 标的峰值是 FP4 稀疏 2070 TFLOPS（NVIDIA 2026），也就是每秒 2070 万亿次 FP4 运算，而且是按"稀疏"算的（单位一节）；稠密的、FP8 或 BF16 的实际速度要低得多，它没公布，下面的交互按"稠密是稀疏的一半，精度每高一级速度再减半，实际能用到一半"来估。按这个估计，π0 类在 Thor 上用 BF16 算一次约 55 毫秒：骨干一次处理 800 个词元，卡在算力上；动作专家每遍只有 50 个词元，卡在读权重上（第 13 篇第 ⑫ 步）。每秒 10 次，一秒里有一半多的时间在算它。Llama 3.1 8B 这种 r ≈ 1 的只看带宽：Thor 每秒能读 273 GB，每写一个词元差不多要把权重读一遍，BF16 时约 16 GB / 273 GB/s ≈ 59 毫秒，一秒约 17 个词元；FP8 约 34 个，FP4 约 68 个。70B 用 FP8，一秒不到 4 个。Before putting a network on a chip, work out two things. Does it fit? M = P × q, plus the intermediate results (and, for a language model, the KV cache). The robot’s module has 128 GB: Llama 3.1 8B in BF16 needs 16 GB, the π0-style model about 6.6 GB, Whisper small about 0.5 GB and all the others together less than 0.1 GB, so they all fit; Llama 3.1 70B in BF16 needs 141 GB and does not fit; in FP8 it needs 71 GB and fits, but every token it writes means reading 71 GB. Is it fast enough? Work out two times and take the slower one: the compute time is the operations divided by the real compute speed, and the read time is the bytes of weights divided by the bandwidth. NVIDIA quotes Thor’s peak as 2070 TFLOPS in sparse FP4 (NVIDIA 2026), that is, 2070 trillion FP4 operations a second, and counted the "sparse" way at that (see the units section); the real speed for dense work, or in FP8 or BF16, is much lower, and NVIDIA has not published it, so the widget below estimates it as "dense is half of sparse, each step up in precision halves it again, and half of that is actually reached". On that estimate, one call of the π0-style model on Thor in BF16 takes about 55 ms: the backbone handles 800 tokens at once and is held back by compute, while the action expert has only 50 tokens per pass and is held back by reading its weights (page 13, step ⑫). At 10 calls a second it keeps the chip busy more than half the time. A network with r ≈ 1 such as Llama 3.1 8B depends on bandwidth alone: Thor can read 273 GB a second, and every token it writes means reading roughly all of its weights once. In BF16 that is about 16 GB / 273 GB/s ≈ 59 ms, so about 17 tokens a second; about 34 in FP8 and about 68 in FP4. The 70B in FP8 manages fewer than 4 a second.

        要多大才够？这没有公式，要看任务多难、有多少数据、留给它多少时间和内存。语言模型上，人们测出了一条经验规律：参数和训练数据一起加大，损失按幂律稳定下降（Kaplan 等 2020）；固定训练的总运算，参数和训练词元应该同比例加，大约每个参数配 20 个词元，也就是训练词元数 D ≈ 20P（Hoffmann 等 2022）。机器人这边反过来：数据少、时间紧，所以最快的那一圈（每秒 50 次）用二十万参数的小 MLP，能等的那一步（想步骤）才用几十亿参数的大模型。How big is big enough? There is no formula: it depends on how hard the task is, how much data there is, and how much time and memory the network is given. For language models, people have measured an empirical rule: when parameters and training data grow together, the loss falls steadily, following a power law (Kaplan et al. 2020); for a fixed total number of training operations, parameters and training tokens should grow in proportion, about 20 tokens for each parameter, that is, the number of training tokens D ≈ 20P (Hoffmann et al. 2022). On the robot it is the other way round: data is scarce and time is tight, so the fastest loop (50 times a second) uses a small MLP with 200,000 parameters, and only the part that can wait (working out the steps) uses a large model with several billion parameters.

        什么任务、什么模态，用什么网络Which network for which task and which modality

        任务Task吃进什么Takes in吐出什么Produces常用的网络Common networks这台机器人上On this robot

        表背后是几条直觉，按数据的形状选积木：数据排在格子上、邻居之间有关系（照片、频谱图），用卷积，或者切块变成词元用 ViT；数据是一串、有先后（文字、声音），用注意力，要省内存、一直开着就用循环网络；数据是一堆没有顺序的点，用共享 MLP 加取最大；数据是编号（词、类别），先查表；要输出和输入一样大的图，用 U-Net 那样先缩再放加跳连；几种数据一起用，先各自变成词元，再选一种拼法（第 ⑬ 步）。数据少时，卷积这种自带"只看邻居、到处一样"假设的积木更占便宜；数据多时，假设更少的注意力往往更好。Behind the table are a few intuitions for choosing building blocks by the shape of the data. If the data sits on a grid and neighbours are related (photos, spectrograms), use convolution, or cut it into patches that become tokens and use a ViT. If the data is a sequence with an order (text, sound), use attention; to save memory and stay on all the time, use a recurrent network. If the data is a heap of points in no order, use a shared MLP plus max pooling. If the data is ids (words, classes), start with an embedding lookup. To output a picture as big as the input, shrink and then enlarge, with skip connections, the way U-Net does. To use several kinds of data together, first turn each into tokens, then choose a way to fuse them (step ⑬). With little data, building blocks like convolution, which come with the assumption "look only at neighbours, the same everywhere" built in, have the edge; with lots of data, attention, which assumes less, often does better.

        • 上面是一张对数坐标的散点图：横轴是参数，纵轴是一个输入的运算，每个点是一个网络，颜色表示吃进什么（Mixtral 画成两个点：实心是每个词元用到的 130 亿，空心是要存的 470 亿）。四条斜线是"每个参数用 1 次（运算 = 2 × 参数）""用 10 次""用 100 次""用 1000 次"。Above is a scatter plot on log scales: parameters across, operations per input up. Each dot is a network, and its colour shows what it takes in (Mixtral is drawn as two dots: the solid one is the 13 billion used per token, the hollow one the 47 billion that must be stored). The four diagonal lines are "each weight used once (operations = 2 × parameters)", "used 10 times", "used 100 times" and "used 1000 times".
        • 点一个网络（或者 ▶ 播放，从小到大挨个看一遍）：读数写出参数、按所选精度占的字节、一个输入的运算、每个参数被用了几次、每秒算几次、每秒的运算。Tap a network (or ▶ Play to go through them one by one, smallest to largest): the readout shows its parameters, its bytes at the chosen precision, its operations per input, how many times each parameter is used, how many times a second it runs, and its operations per second.
        • 下面选 芯片（Orin、Thor、B200）和 精度（BF16、FP8、FP4）：图上的红虚线是这块芯片最多装得下多少参数；表里每个网络一行，装不装得下、算一次要多久（卡在算力还是卡在读权重）、在它要的频率下忙不忙得过来。这里假设能用到峰值的一半，Thor 稠密的速度按"稀疏的一半"估，图上都写明了。Below, set Chip (Orin, Thor, B200) and Precision (BF16, FP8, FP4): the red dashed line on the chart marks the most parameters this chip can hold; the table has one row per network, showing whether it fits, how long one call takes (limited by compute or by reading the weights) and whether the chip keeps up at the rate the network needs. This assumes half the peak can be used, and Thor’s dense speed is estimated as "half the sparse speed"; the figure states both.

        先玩：哪些点贴着"用 1 次"那条线，哪些离它最远？为什么 U-Net 的参数比 ResNet-18 少，运算却多？选 Thor 和 BF16：Llama 3.1 8B 装得下吗？每秒能写几个词元？换成 FP4 呢？Try first: which dots sit on the "used once" line, and which are furthest from it? Why does U-Net have fewer parameters than ResNet-18 but more operations? Pick Thor and BF16: does Llama 3.1 8B fit? How many tokens a second can it write? And in FP4?

        这一步的要点：看一个网络，先算三样：参数 P，存储 M = P × q，一个输入的运算 F。F / P ≈ 2 × 每个权重被用的次数，拿它和芯片的拐点比：大于拐点受限于算力，小于拐点受限于内存带宽。全连接和逐个写词元的语言模型只用一次，总是卡在带宽上；卷积和 ViT 用几百上千次，在拐点高的芯片上还要成批地算。选网络先看数据的形状（格子、序列、点集、编号）和要输出什么，再看芯片装不装得下、算不算得过来。The point of this step: for any network, first work out three things: the parameters P, the storage M = P × q, and the operations per input F. F / P ≈ 2 × the number of times each weight is used; compare it with the chip’s ridge point: above it, compute is the limit; below it, memory bandwidth is. Dense layers, and language models writing one token at a time, use each weight once, so they are always held back by bandwidth; convolutions and ViTs use each weight hundreds or thousands of times, yet on chips with a high ridge point they still have to be run in batches. To choose a network, look first at the shape of the data (grid, sequence, set of points, ids) and at what it must output, then at whether the chip can hold it and keep up with the computing.

        小结Summary

        回到厨房：十秒钟里的每一次乘加Back to the kitchen: every multiply–add in those ten seconds

        不管叫什么名字，神经网络做的都是同一件事：把一串数乘上一串权重，加起来，弯一下，一层接一层。不同的网络，差别在"谁和谁相乘"：全连接是每个输出看每个输入；卷积是一小块权重在格子上到处用；循环网络把上一步的输出也当输入；注意力按内容现算谁看谁；PointNet 让每个点过同一个小网络再取最大。选哪一种，看数据长什么样；网络有多大、要算多少，按公式一段一段加起来就知道。Whatever it is called, a neural network does the same thing: multiply a list of numbers by a list of weights, add them up, then bend it, layer after layer. What differs between networks is "what gets multiplied by what": in a dense layer every output looks at every input; in a convolution a small patch of weights is used all over the grid; a recurrent network also takes its own output from the previous step as input; attention works out from the content, on the fly, who looks at whom; PointNet sends every point through the same small network and then takes the max. Which one to choose depends on what the data looks like; how big a network is and how much it has to compute, you find by adding up the formulas, piece by piece.

        开头的六个问题The six questions from the start

        • 一个输入，怎样在权重里一步步算成输出？每个神经元：每个输入乘一个权重，加起来，加偏置，弯一下（①）。一排神经元是一次矩阵乘向量（②），这一层的输出就是下一层的输入（③）。卷积是同一个只看一小块的神经元在每个位置都算一遍（④），循环网络把上一步的输出也当成输入（⑥），注意力的权重有一部分是按内容现算的（⑦）。拆到底，都是一长串乘加。How does an input get turned into an output, step by step, through the weights? In each neuron: multiply each input by a weight, add them up, add the bias, then bend it (①). A row of neurons is one matrix times a vector (②), and the output of one layer is the input of the next (③). A convolution is the same neuron, looking at only a small patch, computed again at every position (④); a recurrent network also takes its output from the previous step as an input (⑥); in attention, some of the weights are worked out on the spot from the content (⑦). Taken all the way apart, it is all one long string of multiply–adds.
        • 一个网络里面到底长什么样？一条积木链：每一段是一种操作，段和段之间是一个有形状的数组，比如 56 × 56 × 64，或者"T 个词元、每个 d 个数"。ResNet-18 把 224 × 224 × 3 一路变成 7 × 7 × 512，再变成 1000 个分数（⑤）；Llama 是查表、32 个"注意力 + MLP"块、输出表（⑧）；U-Net 是一个 U（⑪）。"结构"就是选哪几种积木、按什么顺序、每一段多宽（⑭）。What does a network actually look like inside? A chain of building blocks: each segment is one operation, and between segments sits an array with a shape, such as 56 × 56 × 64, or "T tokens, d numbers each". ResNet-18 turns 224 × 224 × 3 step by step into 7 × 7 × 512, then into 1000 scores (⑤); Llama is an embedding lookup, 32 "attention + MLP" blocks and an output table (⑧); U-Net is a U (⑪). "Architecture" just means choosing which building blocks, in what order, and how wide each segment is (⑭).
        • 一个网络要占多少存储？M = P × q。P 一段一段加：全连接 nin × nout + nout，卷积 k² × Cin × Cout + Cout，注意力几张 d × d 的表。手的策略 FP32 约 0.84 MB，ResNet-18 FP32 约 47 MB，Llama 3.1 8B 用 BF16 约 16 GB。用的时候还要加上中间结果和键值缓存；训练时每个参数约 16 字节（③ ⑧）。How much storage does a network take? M = P × q. Add up P segment by segment: a dense layer has nin × nout + nout, a convolution k² × Cin × Cout + Cout, attention a few d × d tables. The hand’s policy is about 0.84 MB in FP32, ResNet-18 about 47 MB in FP32, Llama 3.1 8B about 16 GB in BF16. When it runs, add the intermediate results and the KV cache; in training, it is about 16 bytes per parameter (③ ⑧).
        • 多大的输入，要做多少次运算？运算 = 2 × 乘加，乘加 = 每个权重被用的次数加起来。全连接一个输入约 2P；卷积是 2k²CinCoutn′²，和图的面积成正比（ResNet-18：224 × 224 时 3.63 GFLOP，1280 × 720 时约 67 GFLOP）；Transformer 每个词元约 2P，再加上随上下文变长的 4LTd，T 个词元一起算时注意力随 T² 涨（④ ⑤ ⑦ ⑧ ⑮）。How many operations does an input of a given size take? Operations = 2 × multiply–adds, and multiply–adds = the number of times each weight is used, added up. Dense layers need about 2P per input; a convolution needs 2k²CinCoutn′², in proportion to the image’s area (ResNet-18: 3.63 GFLOP at 224 × 224, about 67 GFLOP at 1280 × 720); a Transformer needs about 2P per token, plus 4LTd, which grows as the context gets longer, and when T tokens are computed together, attention grows with T² (④ ⑤ ⑦ ⑧ ⑮).
        • 什么任务、什么模态，该用什么网络？看数据的形状和要输出什么：格子（照片、频谱图）用卷积或 ViT；一串（文字、声音）用注意力，要一直开着、极省电就用很小的循环网络或卷积网络；一堆点用 PointNet；编号先查表；要输出一整张图用 U-Net。再算一算芯片装不装得下、算不算得过来（⑮）。Which network should you use for which task and which modality? Look at the shape of the data and at what has to come out: a grid (photos, spectrograms) calls for convolution or a ViT; a sequence (text, sound) for attention, or, if it must stay on all the time on very little power, for a very small recurrent or convolutional network; a pile of points for PointNet; ids are looked up in a table first; a whole image as output calls for a U-Net. Then work out whether the chip can hold it and whether it can keep up (⑮).
        • 几种模态、几种操作，怎样拼进一个网络？先各自变成词元：照片切块，文字查表，声音画成频谱图再卷积，关节角乘一张表。再选一种拼法：排成一行（π0、PaliGemma），交叉注意力（Whisper 的解码器；π0 的动作专家效果上也是，靠同一个注意力加掩码），调制（Diffusion Policy），各算各的（模块化）（⑬）。另外两种"组合"在用法上：同一个网络连着用 10 遍（流匹配），每个词元只用一部分参数（混合专家）（⑬ ⑭）。How do several modalities and several operations fit into one network? First turn each into tokens: photos are cut into patches, text is looked up in a table, sound is drawn as a spectrogram and then convolved, joint angles are multiplied by a table. Then choose a way to join them: one sequence (π0, PaliGemma), cross-attention (Whisper’s decoder; π0’s action expert works this way in effect too, through the same attention plus a mask), modulation (Diffusion Policy), late fusion (modular) (⑬). Two more kinds of "combining" lie in how the network is used: the same network run 10 times in a row (flow matching), and each token using only part of the parameters (mixture of experts) (⑬ ⑭).

        小测验Quick quiz

        一个神经元，权重 (0.5, 3, −10)，偏置 −4，输入 (4, 1, 0)。z 是多少？S 形输出呢？A neuron with weights (0.5, 3, −10), bias −4 and input (4, 1, 0). What is z? And the sigmoid output?

        z = 0.5 × 4 + 3 × 1 + 0 − 4 = 1，σ(1) = 1 / (1 + e−1) ≈ 0.73（①）。z = 0.5 × 4 + 3 × 1 + 0 − 4 = 1, σ(1) = 1 / (1 + e−1) ≈ 0.73 (①).

        一个 1024 → 4096 的全连接层，用 BF16 存。多少个参数、多少字节？一个输入多少运算？A 1024 → 4096 dense layer, stored in BF16. How many parameters, and how many bytes? How many operations per input?

        1024 × 4096 + 4096 = 4,198,400 个参数，约 8.4 MB；运算 2 × 1024 × 4096 ≈ 8.4 MFLOP（②）。1024 × 4096 + 4096 = 4,198,400 parameters, about 8.4 MB; operations 2 × 1024 × 4096 ≈ 8.4 MFLOP (②).

        3 → 4 → 2 的两层网络，中间不弯一下。它等于一个多少参数的网络？A two-layer 3 → 4 → 2 network with no bend in between. It is the same as a network with how many parameters?

        等于一层 3 → 2：3 × 2 + 2 = 8 个参数。原来的 26 个参数里，多出来的都是白费（③）。The same as a single 3 → 2 layer: 3 × 2 + 2 = 8 parameters. Of the original 26 parameters, all the extra ones are wasted (③).

        输入 56 × 56 × 64，3 × 3 卷积，128 个核，步长 2，补 1 圈零。输出多大？多少个权重、多少次乘加？Input 56 × 56 × 64, a 3 × 3 convolution with 128 kernels, stride 2, padded with 1 ring of zeros. How big is the output? How many weights, and how many multiply–adds?

        n′ = ⌊(56 + 2 − 3) / 2⌋ + 1 = 28，输出 28 × 28 × 128；权重 3 × 3 × 64 × 128 = 73,728 个；乘加 73,728 × 28 × 28 ≈ 5780 万次。这正是 ResNet-18 第二阶段的第一层（④ ⑤）。n′ = ⌊(56 + 2 − 3) / 2⌋ + 1 = 28, so the output is 28 × 28 × 128; weights 3 × 3 × 64 × 128 = 73,728; multiply–adds 73,728 × 28 × 28 ≈ 57.8 million. This is exactly the first layer of ResNet-18’s second stage (④ ⑤).

        普通循环层，每步输入 40 个数，隐藏状态 128 个数。多少个参数？换成 LSTM 呢？A plain recurrent layer with 40 numbers in at each step and a hidden state of 128 numbers. How many parameters? And as an LSTM?

        128 × (40 + 128 + 1) = 21,632 个；LSTM 是 4 倍，86,528 个（⑥）。128 × (40 + 128 + 1) = 21,632; an LSTM has 4 times as many, 86,528 (⑥).

        注意力的宽度 d = 4096。上下文多长时，打分和混合的运算开始超过四张表？Attention of width d = 4096. How long must the context be before the operations for scoring and mixing exceed those of the four tables?

        4T²d > 8Td²，也就是 T > 2d = 8192 个词元（⑦）。4T²d > 8Td², that is, T > 2d = 8192 tokens (⑦).

        ViT 用 16 × 16 的方块看一张 448 × 448 的图，有几个词元？和 224 × 224 比，打分表是几倍？A ViT looks at a 448 × 448 image in 16 × 16 patches. How many tokens is that? Compared with 224 × 224, how many times larger is the score table?

        (448 / 16)² = 784 个；224 × 224 时是 196 个，词元是 4 倍，打分表是 16 倍（⑦ ⑨）。(448 / 16)² = 784; at 224 × 224 it is 196, so 4 times the tokens and 16 times the score table (⑦ ⑨).

        Whisper 一次听 30 秒。你说了 6 秒，编码器大约有百分之几的运算花在补上的静音上？Whisper listens to 30 seconds at a time. If you speak for 6 seconds, roughly what percentage of the encoder’s operations is spent on the padded silence?

        24 / 30 = 80%（⑩）。24 / 30 = 80% (⑩).

        U-Net 的输入从 256 × 256 换成 512 × 512，参数和运算各变成几倍？If the U-Net’s input goes from 256 × 256 to 512 × 512, how many times larger do the parameters and the operations each become?

        参数不变；运算和面积成正比，变成 4 倍（⑪）。The parameters stay the same; the operations are proportional to the area, so they become 4 times as many (⑪).

        把 PointNet 的 1024 个点打乱顺序，输出会变吗？点数加倍呢？If you shuffle the order of PointNet’s 1024 points, does the output change? And if you double the number of points?

        打乱不变：取最大和顺序无关。点数加倍，参数不变，每个点共用的那部分运算加倍；输出可能略有变化（取到的点不一样了），但只要关键点还在，全局特征就差不多（⑫）。Shuffling changes nothing: the max does not depend on order. Doubling the points leaves the parameters unchanged and doubles the operations of the part every point shares; the output may change slightly (the points sampled are different), but as long as the critical points are still there, the global feature stays about the same (⑫).

        Mixtral 8x7B 用 BF16 存，要多少内存？每个词元的运算像多大的稠密模型？Mixtral 8x7B stored in BF16: how much memory does it need? Per token, its operations are like those of a dense model of what size?

        470 亿 × 2 字节 ≈ 94 GB；每个词元只用 130 亿个参数，约 26 GFLOP，像一个 130 亿参数的稠密模型（⑭）。47 billion × 2 bytes ≈ 94 GB; each token uses only 13 billion parameters, about 26 GFLOP, like a dense model with 13 billion parameters (⑭).

        Llama 3.1 8B 用 FP8 放在 273 GB/s 的模块上，一个人用的时候，每秒最多写几个词元？Llama 3.1 8B in FP8 on a 273 GB/s module, with one person using it: at most how many tokens a second can it write?

        每写一个词元差不多要读一遍 8 GB：273 / 8.03 ≈ 34 个（⑮，第 13 篇）。Each token written means reading roughly all 8 GB once: 273 / 8.03 ≈ 34 (⑮, page 13).

        想继续深入Going further

        • He 等，Deep Residual Learning for Image Recognition：ResNet 和残差连接，表 1 就是第 ⑤ 步那张结构表。He et al., Deep Residual Learning for Image Recognition: ResNet and residual connections; its Table 1 is the architecture table from step ⑤.
        • Vaswani 等，Attention Is All You Need：Transformer 的原始论文（第 ⑦、⑧ 步）。Vaswani et al., Attention Is All You Need: the original Transformer paper (steps ⑦ and ⑧).
        • Dosovitskiy 等，An Image is Worth 16x16 Words：把图切块当成词元（第 ⑨ 步）。Dosovitskiy et al., An Image is Worth 16x16 Words: cutting an image into patches and treating them as tokens (step ⑨).
        • Radford 等，Whisper：频谱图、卷积开头、编码器–解码器，表 1 是五种大小（第 ⑩ 步）。Radford et al., Whisper: spectrograms, the convolutional front end, encoder–decoder; Table 1 lists the five sizes (step ⑩).
        • Ronneberger 等，U-Net：先缩再放加跳连（第 ⑪ 步）。Ronneberger et al., U-Net: shrink, then enlarge, plus skip connections (step ⑪).
        • Qi 等，PointNet：共享 MLP 加取最大，和关键点（第 ⑫ 步）。Qi et al., PointNet: a shared MLP plus max pooling, and critical points (step ⑫).
        • Black 等，π0：视觉语言模型加动作专家，附推理时间的分解（第 ⑬ 步）。Black et al., π0: a vision–language model plus an action expert, with a breakdown of inference time (step ⑬).
        • Chi 等，Diffusion Policy：卷积 U-Net 从噪声里擦出动作（第 ⑭ 步）。Chi et al., Diffusion Policy: a convolutional U-Net that wipes away noise to reveal actions (step ⑭).
        • 第 9 篇：每个旋钮往哪拧：权重是怎么训练出来的，反向传播怎么倒着算。Page 9: Which Way to Turn Each Knob: how the weights are trained, and how backpropagation runs the computation backwards.
        • 第 7 篇：从噪声到样本：扩散和流匹配，动作专家"擦 10 遍"在做什么。Page 7: From Noise to Samples: diffusion and flow matching, and what the action expert is doing when it "wipes 10 times".
        • 第 13 篇：芯片里的一秒钟：这些乘加在芯片里怎么跑，为什么逐个写词元卡在内存带宽上。Page 13: One Second in Silicon: how these multiply–adds run on a chip, and why writing tokens one at a time is limited by memory bandwidth.
        本页约定：一次乘加算 2 次运算（FLOP）；论文和 torchvision 写的"FLOPs"如果其实是乘加（ResNet、PointNet），已经换算并注明。1 kB = 103 字节，1 MB = 106 字节，1 GB = 109 字节。参数个数包括偏置和归一化的参数，大多是按公开的结构一层层算出来的（PointNet、Mixtral、π0 的总数和唤醒词 GRU 的大小用的是论文的数字），能和官方数字对的地方都对过。厨房里的机器人是虚构的：它的传感器和计算模块照着 2025 到 2026 年的产品来配，网络照着 2015 到 2024 年的论文来配（见开场）。所有交互都在你的浏览器里实时计算，没有声音。Conventions on this page: one multiply–add counts as 2 operations (FLOP); where papers and torchvision write "FLOPs" but actually mean multiply–adds (ResNet, PointNet), the numbers have been converted and this is noted. 1 kB = 103 bytes, 1 MB = 106 bytes, 1 GB = 109 bytes. Parameter counts include biases and normalisation parameters; most were worked out layer by layer from the published architectures (the totals for PointNet, Mixtral and π0, and the size of the wake-word GRU, are the papers’ figures), and wherever they could be checked against official figures, they were. The robot in the kitchen is made up: its sensors and compute module are modelled on products from 2025 to 2026, and its networks on papers from 2015 to 2024 (see the opening). Everything interactive is computed live in your browser, and nothing plays sound.
